## Health of Animals Regulations {#health-of-animals-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[C.R.C.]{.abbr title="Consolidated Regulations of Canada"}, c. 296
:::

[HEALTH OF ANIMALS ACT](/eng/acts/H-3.3)

Regulations Respecting the Health of Animals
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-546836 .Part}

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations may be
cited as the [Health of Animals
Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._296).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/91-525, s. 2
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-546840 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2025-47, s. 8(F)

\]
:::

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** In these Regulations,

[[abattoir]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[abattoir]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a mobile
    abattoir; ([abattoir]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[accredited veterinarian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[accredited veterinarian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a veterinarian
    who is authorized to perform certain duties or functions under an
    agreement made under section 34 of the Act; ([vétérinaire
    accrédité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Health of Animals
    Act](/eng/acts/H-3.3); ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[advertisement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[advertisement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any representation by
    any means whatever for the purpose of promoting directly or
    indirectly the sale or disposal of a veterinary
    biologic; ([publicité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canadian Food Inspection
    Agency established by section 3 of the [Canadian Food Inspection
    Agency Act](/eng/acts/C-16.5); ([Agence]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[air carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[air carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the owner or operator of
    an aircraft engaged in the transportation of animals by
    air; ([transporteur aérien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[animal by-product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[animal by-product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an animal by-product
    that originated from a bird or from any mammal except a member of
    the orders [Rodentia]{.otherLang lang="la"}, [Cetacea]{.otherLang
    lang="la"}, [Pinnipedia]{.otherLang lang="la"} and
    [Sirenia]{.otherLang lang="la"}; ([sous-produit
    animal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[animal embryo]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[animal embryo]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2001-210, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[animal embryo transfer centre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[animal embryo transfer centre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/97-85, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[animal food]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[animal food]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means food containing an animal
    product or animal by-product for chickens, turkeys, ducks, geese,
    ratites, game birds, ruminants, swine or horses; ([aliments pour
    animaux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[animal glands and organs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[animal glands and organs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes ox gall or
    bile, rennet and similar substances derived from domestic
    animals; ([glandes et organes d'animaux]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[animal pathogen]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[animal pathogen]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any animal pathogen
    derived through biotechnology; ([agent
    zoopathogène]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[animal product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[animal product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an animal product that
    originated from a bird or from any mammal except a member of the
    orders [Rodentia]{.otherLang lang="la"}, [Cetacea]{.otherLang
    lang="la"}, [Pinnipedia]{.otherLang lang="la"} and
    [Sirenia]{.otherLang lang="la"}; ([produit animal]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[animal semen distribution centre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[animal semen distribution centre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
    establishment or vehicle from which animal semen is
    distributed; ([centre de distribution de sperme
    animal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[animal semen production centre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[animal semen production centre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
    establishment where semen from ruminants or swine is collected or
    processed; ([centre de production de sperme animal]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[antibiotic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[antibiotic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2002-438, s.
    6\]]{.Repealed}

[[approved disinfection establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[approved disinfection establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
    any factory, plant or other place approved by the Minister for the
    cleaning, disinfection or treatment of any animal by-product or any
    other thing; ([établissement de désinfection
    approuvé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[approved laboratory]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[approved laboratory]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an establishment
    or vehicle approved by the Minister for carrying out examinations or
    tests of animals; ([laboratoire approuvé]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[assigned name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[assigned name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, with reference to a
    veterinary biologic, the name, in English or French, specified in a
    permit to import a veterinary biologic or in a product
    licence; ([nom assigné]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[association]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/97-85, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[bee product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bee product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes bee pollen, bee
    propolis, royal jelly, beeswax and honey; ([produit
    d'abeille]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[biotechnology]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[biotechnology]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the application of
    science and engineering to the direct or indirect use of living
    organisms or parts or products of living organisms in their natural
    or modified forms; ([biotechnologie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bird of the parrot family]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bird of the parrot family]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means parrot,
    Amazon, Mexican double head, African grey, cockatoo, macaw,
    love-bird, lorie, lorikeet, and any other member of the species
    [psittacidae]{.otherLang lang="la"}; ([psittacidés]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[blood meal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[blood meal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means dried blood of an
    animal; ([farine de sang]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bone meal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bone meal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means ground animal bones, hoofs
    or horns which may include pieces of hide, flesh or sinew; ([farine
    d'os]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bovine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bovine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means cattle or bison domestically
    raised or kept, but for the purposes of the import reference
    document does not include a bison that has ever been in contact with
    or part of a wild herd; ([bovin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[brucellosis-accredited area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[brucellosis-accredited area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2019-99, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[brucellosis-free area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[brucellosis-free area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2019-99, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[brucellosis free listed herd]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[brucellosis free listed herd]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/97-85, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[brucellosis free region]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[brucellosis free region]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/93-159, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[brucellosis low incidence region]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[brucellosis low incidence region]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/93-159, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[brucellosis region]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[brucellosis region]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/93-159, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[calf]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[calf]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2001-210, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means air carrier, sea carrier,
    motor carrier or railway company; ([transporteur]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[cattle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[cattle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means animals of the species [Bos
    taurus]{.otherLang lang="la"} or [Bos indicus]{.otherLang
    lang="la"}; ([boeuf]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[certified brucellosis-free herd]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[certified brucellosis-free herd]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/78-205, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[chick]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[chick]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to any species, a
    bird up to 72 hours of age that has not been fed or
    watered; ([poussin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[communicable disease]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[communicable disease]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an infectious or
    contagious disease; ([maladie transmissible]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[country of origin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[country of origin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to an animal, the country from which
      the animal was imported into Canada, where the animal has lived
      for no less than 60 days in that country in association with other
      animals of its own species and, in any other case, means the
      country in which the animal was born,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to an animal embryo, animal product
      or animal by-product, the country in which the animal embryo,
      animal product or animal by-product was taken from an animal, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} with respect to an animal product or animal
      by-product --- other than non-fertilized ova, semen and meat ---
      that has undergone processing that would prevent the introduction
      of any reportable disease, any disease referred to in Schedule VII
      and any serious epizootic disease to which the species from which
      the product or by-product was derived is susceptible and that can
      be transmitted by the product or by-product, the country in which
      the product or by-product underwent that processing; ([pays
      d'origine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[disease]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[disease]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, for the purposes of Parts III
    and IV, any reportable disease or other serious epizootic disease to
    which an animal or germplasm is susceptible and which can be
    transmitted by the animal or germplasm; ([maladie]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[domestic cat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[domestic cat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the animal [felis
    domesticus]{.otherLang lang="la"}; ([chat
    domestique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[domestic dog]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[domestic dog]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the animal [canis
    domesticus]{.otherLang lang="la"}; ([chien
    domestique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[egg product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[egg product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any of the following,
    namely, whole egg, egg shells, egg yolk, egg albumen or any mix of
    these, in a liquid, dried, frozen or fresh form; ([produit
    d'oeuf]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[equine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[equine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a horse, ass, mule or
    zebra; ([équidés]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[eradication area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eradication area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/79-295, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[establishment licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[establishment licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence issued
    by the Minister in respect of an establishment; ([permis
    d'établissement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[expiration date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[expiration date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a date designating the
    end of the period during which a veterinary biologic, when properly
    stored and handled, may be expected to be effective; ([date de
    péremption]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fertilizer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fertilizer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    section 2 of the [Fertilizers
    Act](/eng/acts/F-10); ([engrais]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fertilizer supplement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fertilizer supplement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning
    as the word [[supplement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in section 2 of the
    [Fertilizers Act](/eng/acts/F-10); ([supplément
    d'engrais]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[flock of origin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[flock of origin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[herd of
    origin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the flock or herd of which an
    animal was a member, where the animal was a member of that flock or
    herd for not less than 60 days immediately preceding its importation
    into Canada and in any other case means the flock or herd in which
    it was born; ([troupeau de provenance]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fowl plague]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fowl plague]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means highly pathogenic avian
    influenza; ([peste aviaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[game bird]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[game bird]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a wild bird kept in
    captivity and includes pheasants, partridges, quail, grouse, guinea
    fowl and pea fowl, but does not include doves, pigeons, ducks,
    geese, birds of the parrot family or songbirds; ([gibier à
    plumes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[gluestock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[gluestock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the hair, bones, hoofs,
    horns, fleshings, hide cuttings or parings of an animal or any other
    part of an animal that may be used in the manufacture of
    glue; ([carnasse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[hatching eggs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[hatching eggs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the fertilized eggs of
    poultry; ([oeufs d'incubation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Health of Animals Branch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Health of Animals Branch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/82-590, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[herd]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[herd]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/91-525, s.
    3\]]{.Repealed}

[[herd]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[herd]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[flock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in
    respect of ruminants, means all ruminants

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that are owned by, or are in the possession or
      under the care or control of, any person and are kept

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on one or more parts of any single premises, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} on two or more premises that are
        geographically separate, where the ruminants have been
        interchanged or have had contact, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that are kept on a community pasture, grazing
      association unit or other common premises where the ruminants of
      more than one owner are kept, and includes any ruminants owned by
      the owners of those ruminants that are kept on any other premises
      and that are under the care or control of any of those
      owners; ([troupeau]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[herd of negative status]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[herd of negative status]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/97-85, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[inspect]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[inspect]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes
    test; ([inspecter]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[inspection]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[inspection]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an inspection carried out
    by an inspector, except where a customs officer carries out an
    inspection pursuant to section 16 of the
    Act; ([inspection]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[inspection port]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[inspection port]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place listed in
    Schedule II where facilities exist to inspect animals; ([poste
    d'inspection]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/97-85, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any legend, word or mark
    attached to, included in, belonging to or accompanying any
    veterinary biologic or package; ([étiquette]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[level 2 containment laboratory]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[level 2 containment laboratory]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    laboratory that meets or exceeds the criteria for the containment of
    pathogens at containment level 2 according to the Laboratory
    Biosafety Guidelines published by the Public Health Agency of Canada
    on its Internet site, as amended from time to time; ([laboratoire de
    niveau de confinement 2]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[licensed establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[licensed establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place
    consisting of one or more rooms or buildings for which an
    establishment licence has been issued by the
    Minister; ([établissement agréé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[livestock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[livestock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means animals of the bovine,
    caprine, equine, ovine and porcine species; ([animaux de
    ferme]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[livestock dealer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[livestock dealer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/97-85, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[manufacture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[manufacture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes
    prepare; ([fabriquer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[manure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[manure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes guano, feces with or without
    urine and anything containing feces from any bird, ruminant or
    porcine; ([fumier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[meat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[meat]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the edible part of a carcass that
    is the muscle associated with the skeleton, tongue, diaphragm,
    heart, gizzard or mammalian oesophagus, with or without accompanying
    and overlying fat, together with those parts of the bones, skin,
    sinews, nerves, blood vessels and other tissues that normally
    accompany the muscle and are not ordinarily removed in dressing a
    carcass, but does not include the muscle associated with the lips,
    snout, scalp or ears; ([viande]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[meat meal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[meat meal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
    [[tankage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the rendered and dried carcass
    or part of the carcass of an animal; ([farine de
    viande]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}or[résidus de
    graisse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[milk]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[milk]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the lacteal secretion obtained
    from the mammary gland of any ruminant, in concentrated, dried,
    frozen, reconstituted or fresh form; ([lait]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[milk product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[milk product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any of the following,
    namely, partly-skimmed milk, skim milk, cream, butter, buttermilk,
    butter oil, whey, whey butter or whey cream, in concentrated, dried,
    frozen or reconstituted or fresh form, but does not include milk
    proteins, milk sugars and milk enzymes; ([produit du
    lait]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of Agriculture
    and Agri-Food; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[motor carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[motor carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the owner or operator of
    a motor vehicle engaged in the transportation of animals by motor
    vehicle; ([transporteur routier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[non-ambulatory animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[non-ambulatory animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means livestock,
    poultry, rabbits or an animal of the cervid or camelid species that
    is unable to stand without assistance or to move without being
    dragged or carried. ([animal non ambulatoire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[non-designated region]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[non-designated region]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/85-545, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[official vaccinate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[official vaccinate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2001-210, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[official veterinarian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[official veterinarian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a country means,
    for the purposes of all Parts except Part II, a veterinarian
    employed by the government of that country; ([vétérinaire
    officiel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[package]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[package]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes anything in which any
    veterinary biologic is wholly or partly contained, placed or
    packed; ([emballage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[part of a country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[part of a country]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2001-210, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[poultry]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[poultry]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means domestic fowl and pigeons and
    includes any bird that is in captivity; ([volaille]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[product licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[product licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a licence issued by
    the Minister to manufacture the veterinary biologic described in the
    licence; ([permis de fabrication]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[product outline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[product outline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a detailed description
    of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the process followed in preparing a veterinary
      biologic and any diluent to be used therewith,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the methods and procedures to be employed in
      handling, storing, administering and testing a veterinary biologic
      and any diluent to be used therewith, and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the tests used to establish the purity, safety,
      potency and efficacy of a veterinary biologic, and the purity and
      safety of any diluent to be used therewith, and the results of all
      such tests; ([données générales sur le produit]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[purebred]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[purebred]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/97-85, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[quarantine port]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[quarantine port]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place listed in
    Schedule I where facilities exist to quarantine animals; ([poste de
    quarantaine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[railway company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[railway company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a railway company
    engaged in the transportation of livestock by rail; ([compagnie de
    chemin de fer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[raw wool, hair or bristles]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[raw wool, hair or bristles]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means wool, hair
    or bristles taken from an animal but does not include wool tops,
    wool waste, wool noils, wool laps, small trade samples of wool, lime
    pulled wool and hair, scoured wool and hair and carbonized wool and
    hair; ([laine, poils et soies bruts]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[registered processed egg station]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered processed egg station]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2018-108, s. 403\]]{.Repealed}

[[ruminant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ruminant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an animal of the suborder
    [Ruminatiae]{.otherLang lang="la"} and includes an animal of the
    family [Camelidae]{.otherLang
    lang="la"}; ([ruminant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[sea carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[sea carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the owner or operator of a
    vessel engaged in the transportation of animals by
    water; ([transporteur maritime]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[serious epizootic disease]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[serious epizootic disease]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a disease
    designated by the Minister under section 2.2; ([épizootie
    grave]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[song bird]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[song bird]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means canary, finch, oriole,
    cardinal and any other songster; ([oiseau chanteur]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an ear tag stamped with the
    letters "H of A" and includes any device used for the identification
    of an animal pursuant to the Act and these
    Regulations; ([bague]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[test]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the collection of body tissue or fluid from an
      animal, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the injection of an animal for the purpose of
      determining that animal's freedom from or infection with
      disease; ([épreuve]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[tuberculosis-accredited advanced area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tuberculosis-accredited advanced
    area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2019-99, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[tuberculosis-accredited area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tuberculosis-accredited area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2019-99, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[tuberculosis-accredited herd]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tuberculosis-accredited herd]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/97-85, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[tuberculosis-free area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[tuberculosis-free area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/2019-99, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

[[United States]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[United States]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the states of the United
    States that are on the mainland of the North American continent and
    the state of Hawaii; ([États-Unis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[untanned hide or skin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[untanned hide or skin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the outer
    covering of ruminants, ratites, equines and porcines that is raw and
    has not been chemically processed into a permanent and durable form
    of leather; ([peau ou cuir non tannés]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[veterinarian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[veterinarian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person duly qualified
    to practice veterinary medicine under the laws of the place where he
    practises; ([vétérinaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[veterinary biologic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[veterinary biologic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any veterinary
    biologic derived through biotechnology. ([produit biologique
    vétérinaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Veterinary Inspection, Operations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Veterinary Inspection,
    Operations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/97-85, s.
    1\]]{.Repealed}

[[veterinary inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[veterinary inspector]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
    SOR/97-85, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

**[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
interpreting any document prepared by the Agency that is incorporated by
reference into these Regulations, words and expressions that are used
but not defined in that document have the same meaning as in these
Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-41, s. 1](#1289054-1289586){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1289054-1289586.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [Designation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1516131 .Part}

- **[[2.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  order to prevent the introduction of any vector, disease or toxic
  substance into Canada or into another country from Canada, the
  Minister may, by notice, designate a disease as a serious epizootic
  disease for the purposes of any provision of these Regulations,
  including any provision related to importation, exportation or
  possession of animals or things.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may designate the disease if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the disease is not listed as a reportable disease
    in the schedule to the [Reportable Diseases
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-91-2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the disease is not listed as an immediately
    notifiable disease in Schedule VII to these Regulations; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the disease may have a serious impact on human or
    animal health.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 10](#1515094-1516139){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515094-1516139.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[Segregation and Inspection of Animals]{.HTitleText1} {#h-546960 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/79-839, s. 2

\]
:::

### [Segregation and Confinement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-546963 .Subheading}

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where an
  animal

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is affected or suspected of being affected with a
    communicable disease,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has been in contact with an animal so affected or
    suspected of being so affected,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is in an eradication area or control zone, or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is imported or tendered for import into Canada,

  an inspector may order the person having the possession, care or
  custody of the animal to keep separate the animal in a place and
  manner suitable for inspection and testing within the period of time
  specified by the inspector.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who receives an order referred to in
  subsection (1) shall comply with the order.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 2(F)
- SOR/79-839, s. 3
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 2](#1124064-1124582){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124064-1124582.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 3"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[3.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3.1 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/79-839, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

### [Inspection]{.HTitleText2} {#h-546975 .Subheading}

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** An inspector may inspect
any animal in Canada that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is affected or suspected of being affected with a
  communicable disease;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} has been in contact with an animal so affected or
  suspected of being so affected;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} is in an eradication area or control zone; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} is imported or tendered for import into Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 4(F)
- SOR/79-839, s. 4
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 3](#1124066-1124584){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124066-1124584.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 4"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where an
  animal is affected or suspected of being affected with a communicable
  disease or has been in contact with an animal so affected or suspected
  of being so affected, a veterinary inspector may order the person
  having the possession, care or custody of the animal,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to quarantine, keep separate or treat the animal,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to destroy the animal, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to destroy the animal and dispose of its carcass

  in such a manner, at such a place or places, under such conditions and
  within such period of time as are necessary to prevent the spread of
  the communicable disease, which manner, place or places, conditions
  and time shall be specified in the order.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who receives an order referred to in
  subsection (1) shall comply with the order.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where an order referred to in subsection (1)
  requiring an animal to be destroyed or destroyed and disposed of is
  not complied with within the time specified therein, a veterinary
  inspector may have the animal

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} removed to and destroyed at an establishment where
    food animals are slaughtered by the holder of a licence that is
    issued under paragraph 20(1)(b) of the [Safe Food for Canadians
    Act](/eng/acts/S-1.1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} destroyed at a time and place determined by him,
    and have its carcass disposed of as determined by him.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-839, s. 4
- SOR/81-348, s. 1
- [SOR/2018-108, s. 404](#1044674-1110070){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044674-1110070.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 18(F)](#1124109-1124689){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124109-1124689.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20190115.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** Where an inspector finds or
suspects that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a thing is a disease agent,

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an animal or thing is affected by or contaminated
  with a communicable disease, or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any record or document required by or under the Act
  and these Regulations to prevent the spread of any disease within
  Canada, or to any other country from Canada, by an animal or thing is
  not produced for inspection by an inspector,

the inspector may order the person who owns or has possession, care or
control of the animal or thing, to quarantine the animal or thing, and
the provisions of section 91.4 apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 2
:::

## [PART I.1]{.HLabel1}[Specified Risk Material]{.HTitleText1} {#h-547002 .Part}

**[[6.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.1 .sectionLabel}** In this Part ,
[[specified risk material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the skull, brain,
trigeminal ganglia, eyes, tonsils, spinal cord and dorsal root ganglia
of cattle aged 30 months or older, and the distal ileum of cattle of all
ages, but does not include material from a country of origin, or a part
of a country of origin, that is designated under section 7 as posing a
negligible risk for Bovine Spongiform Encephalopathy.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-264, s. 1
- SOR/2009-18, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 6.1"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[6.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.2 .sectionLabel}** Every person who
slaughters, cuts up or debones cattle for human consumption as food
shall ensure that the specified risk material has been removed from the
cattle.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-264, s. 1
:::

- **[[6.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.21
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who slaughters, cuts up
  or debones cattle for human consumption as food shall ensure that,
  immediately after removal of the specified risk material, the
  specified risk material is stained with a conspicuous and indelible
  dye and collected in a dedicated container that is marked with a
  statement in both official languages indicating that the contents are
  specified risk material.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cattle that are slaughtered, cut up or deboned on a
    farm, as defined in subsection 172(1), if the specified risk
    material from the carcasses of those cattle is not removed from the
    farm except for submission to a level 2 containment laboratory;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} cattle that are slaughtered, cut up or deboned at
    an abattoir if all parts of the carcass of any animal remain on the
    premises of the abattoir other than those parts that are for human
    consumption as food or samples that are submitted to a level 2
    containment laboratory; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} samples of specified risk material, or carcasses
    --- or parts of carcasses --- of cattle from which the specified
    risk material has not been removed, that are submitted to a level 2
    containment laboratory.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-147, s. 12
- SOR/2009-220, s. 3
- SOR/2012-286, s. 47
- SOR/2014-23, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6.21-20121214.html "Link to previous version of section 6.21"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[6.22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who removes the
  specified risk material from the carcasses of cattle that died or were
  condemned before they otherwise would have been slaughtered for human
  consumption as food shall ensure that the specified risk material is
  stained with a conspicuous and indelible dye and collected in a
  dedicated container that is marked with a statement in both official
  languages indicating that the contents are specified risk material.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the specified risk material has not been removed
  from the carcasses of cattle that died or were condemned before they
  otherwise would have been slaughtered for human consumption as food,
  the person who has the possession, care or control of the carcasses
  and any parts of them shall ensure that the carcasses and any parts of
  them containing the specified risk material are stained with a dye
  that is conspicuous, indelible and safe for consumption by animals.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the carcasses of cattle if the specified risk
    material from each of the carcasses and each of the carcasses that
    contains specified risk material remain on the premises on which the
    cattle were determined to be dead or are submitted to a level 2
    containment laboratory; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} samples of specified risk material, or carcasses
    --- or parts of carcasses --- of cattle from which the specified
    risk material has not been removed, that are submitted to a level 2
    containment laboratory.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-147, s. 12
- SOR/2012-286, s. 48
- SOR/2015-55, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6.22-20121214.html "Link to previous version of section 6.22"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[6.23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.23
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), this
  section applies to every person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} who is required by these Regulations to remove or
    stain specified risk material;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} who collects the carcasses of cattle that died or
    were condemned before they otherwise would have been slaughtered for
    human consumption as food if the specified risk material has not
    been removed from the carcasses; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} who receives specified risk material from another
    person or who receives from another person the carcasses of cattle
    that died or were condemned before they otherwise would have been
    slaughtered for human consumption as food if the specified risk
    material has not been removed from the carcasses.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person shall keep, for a period of 10 years, a
  record for each day on which the specified risk material is removed,
  stained or received or the carcasses are collected or received that
  includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person's name and address and the date of the
    removal, staining, collection or reception;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the combined weight of the specified risk material
    and the carcasses or parts of carcasses collected or received, as
    well as the number of those carcasses;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the dye used to identify the specified
    risk material or the carcasses;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the numbers of the approved tags, as defined in
    section 172, that are on the carcasses or, in the case of carcasses
    not bearing approved tags, the information referred to in paragraph
    187(2)(a) in respect of the carcasses;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the person treated, confined or destroyed the
    specified risk material or the carcasses, the date on which and the
    manner in which the specified risk material or the carcasses were
    treated, confined or destroyed; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the person did not confine or destroy the
    specified risk material or the carcasses,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person, if any, who
      received the specified risk material or carcasses from him or her,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person, if any, who
      transported the specified risk material or the carcasses to
      another location and the manner in which the specified risk
      material or the carcasses were transported, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address, if known to the person,
      of the person, if any, who confined or destroyed the specified
      risk material or the carcasses.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply in respect of samples of
  specified risk material, or carcasses --- or parts of carcasses --- of
  cattle from which the specified risk material has not been removed,
  that are submitted to a level 2 containment laboratory.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-147, s. 12
- SOR/2012-286, s. 49
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 11(F)](#1515096-1516140){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515096-1516140.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6.23-20121214.html "Link to previous version of section 6.23"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[6.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.3 .sectionLabel}** No person shall use or
export for human consumption as food specified risk material in any
form, whether or not incorporated into another thing, where the
specified risk material was removed from cattle slaughtered in Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-264, s. 1
:::

- **[[6.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except in accordance with a permit
  or a licence issued by the Minister under section 160, no person shall
  receive, remove from any premises, use, transport out of any premises,
  treat, store, export, sell, distribute, confine or destroy specified
  risk material in any form, whether or not incorporated into another
  thing, if the specified risk material was removed from cattle
  slaughtered in Canada or was removed from, or is contained in, the
  carcasses of cattle that died or were condemned before they otherwise
  would have been slaughtered for human consumption as food.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to require a permit for
  staining under section 6.21 or 6.22.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of specified
  risk material that was removed from cattle slaughtered for human
  consumption as food or that was removed from, or is contained in, the
  carcasses of cattle that died or were condemned before they otherwise
  would have been slaughtered for human consumption as food if the
  specified risk material remains on the premises on which the cattle
  were slaughtered or were determined to be dead.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of the use,
  as a fertilizer or as a fertilizer supplement, of material that is
  derived from specified risk material in any form that was removed from
  cattle slaughtered for human consumption as food or from cattle that
  died or were condemned before they otherwise would have been
  slaughtered for human consumption as food if the fertilizer or
  fertilizer supplement remains on the premises on which the cattle were
  slaughtered, died or were condemned.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of the
  submission, to a level 2 containment laboratory, of samples of
  specified risk material, or carcasses --- or parts of carcasses --- of
  cattle from which the specified risk material has not been removed or
  in respect of activities related to the use of specified risk material
  in a level 2 containment laboratory.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall not issue a permit for the purpose
  of subsection (1) if the specified risk material is to be received,
  removed from any premises, used, transported, treated, stored,
  exported, sold or distributed in any form, whether or not incorporated
  into another thing, for human consumption as food.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall not issue a permit for the
  destruction of specified risk material unless the destruction is to be
  by incineration, or another method, that will ensure that the
  specified risk material and anything in which it is incorporated will
  not be used as food for humans and will not, or will not be likely to,
  result in the spread within Canada of Bovine Spongiform
  Encephalopathy.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-147, s. 13
- SOR/2012-286, s. 50
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 12(F)](#1515098-1516141){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515098-1516141.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6.4-20121214.html "Link to previous version of section 6.4"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[6.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except in accordance with a permit
  issued under section 160, no person shall feed to any animal material
  in any form --- whether or not incorporated into another thing ---
  that is derived from specified risk material.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a person who feeds a
  pet food, pet chew or pet treat to an animal if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the country of origin of that product is the United
    States;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person, on entry into Canada from the United
    States, was in possession of the product and was accompanied by the
    animal to which it is fed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person legally imported both the product and
    the animal into Canada; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the product is fed only to the animal that
    accompanied the person into Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-147, s. 13
- SOR/2009-18, s. 5
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6.5-20070712.html "Link to previous version of section 6.5"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[6.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.6 .sectionLabel}** Sections 6.4 and 6.5 do
not apply in respect of material produced as a result of the destruction
of specified risk material in accordance with a permit issued under
section 160 for the purpose of section 6.4 if the material would not, or
would not be likely to, result in the spread within Canada of Bovine
Spongiform Encephalopathy.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-147, s. 13
:::

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Importation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-547068 .Part}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547069 .Subheading}

#### [Designation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-547070 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2007-24, s. 2

\]
:::

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purpose of preventing the introduction of a disease into Canada from
  an animal or thing imported into Canada, the Minister may designate a
  country or part of a country as being free of a disease or as posing a
  negligible risk for a disease.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The designation shall be in writing and be based on
  the following criteria respecting the country or part of the country
  that is the subject of the designation:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the prevalence of the disease;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time since the most recent outbreak of the
    disease;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the disease surveillance programs in effect;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the measures taken to prevent the introduction or
    spread of the disease;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the natural barriers to the movement of the
    disease;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the zoosanitary infrastructure; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any other criteria relevant to the state, extent or
    propagation of the disease.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may amend or repeal the designation.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of these Regulations, the Minister
  may describe, in writing, a part of a country.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-839, s. 5
- SOR/97-85, s. 3
- SOR/2007-24, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 7"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Electronic Documents]{.HTitleText3} {#h-547087 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2007-24, s. 4

\]
:::

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may accept a certificate or any other document referred to in
  this Part, any of Parts III to VIII or Part XVI that is transmitted in
  electronic form, including by facsimile.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who imports anything in respect of which
  a certificate or other document has been transmitted in electronic
  form to the Minister shall, upon the Minister's request, provide the
  Minister with an original signed copy of the certificate or document.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 6

- SOR/2010-296, s. 1

#### [Powers of Inspectors]{.HTitleText3} {#h-547095 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2007-24, s. 5

\]
:::

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** Where an inspector finds or
suspects that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a thing is a disease agent,

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an animal or thing imported into Canada is affected
  by or contaminated with a communicable disease, or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any information or documentation required by or under
  the Act and these Regulations to prevent the introduction of any
  disease into Canada by an animal or thing is not presented to an
  inspector,

the inspector may order the person who owns or has possession, care or
control of the animal or thing to quarantine the animal or thing, and
the provisions of section 91.4 apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 6
- SOR/97-85, s. 3
:::

### [Importation of Germplasm and Animals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547105 .Subheading}

#### [Definitions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-547106 .Subheading}

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in this
section apply in this section and in sections 11 to 17.

[[area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a country or, if so identified in
    the import reference document, a part of a country or a number of
    contiguous countries or contiguous parts of
    countries. ([région]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[area of origin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[area of origin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a regulated animal, the area in
      which the animal was born or the area where the animal has lived
      without restrictions relating to disease, such as restrictions for
      isolation or quarantine, for the sixty-day period immediately
      before the date of entry; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to germplasm, the area in which the
      germplasm was taken from a regulated animal or into which it was
      previously imported for unrestricted use. ([région
      d'origine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[embryo]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[embryo]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fertilized ovum, other than a
    hatching egg, before it is implanted into an
    animal. ([embryon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[equivalent risk area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[equivalent risk area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a
    species of regulated animal, an area identified as an equivalent
    risk area for that species in the import reference
    document. ([région à risque équivalent]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[germplasm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[germplasm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means semen, male or female germ
    cells or genetic material taken from a male or female germ cell for
    the purpose of producing a zygote and includes embryos but does not
    include a hatching egg. ([matériel génétique]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[import reference document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[import reference document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the document
    prepared by the Agency and entitled Import Reference Document,
    bearing the date January 25, 2007 and policy number
    AHPD-DSAE-IE-2002-3-4. ([document de référence]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[low risk area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[low risk area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a species
    of regulated animal, an area identified as a low risk area for that
    species in the import reference document. ([région à faible
    risque]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[official veterinarian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[official veterinarian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a veterinarian
    who is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} employed by the authority responsible for
      implementing and supervising or auditing the carrying out of
      veterinary services in a country, the issuance of certificates
      respecting the health and origin of animals in that country and
      the performance of inspections of regulated animals for the
      purpose of protecting animal and public health in that country; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} authorized by that authority to issue those
      certificates and perform those inspections, if the systems for
      providing veterinary services, issuing authorizations and
      performing inspections in that country are equivalent to the
      corresponding systems in Canada in terms of their effectiveness in
      protecting animal and public health. ([vétérinaire
      officiel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[regulated animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[regulated animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a hatching egg,
    turtle, tortoise, bird, honeybee or mammal, but does not include

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} germplasm;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} members of the orders [Cetacea]{.otherLang
      lang="la"}, [Pinnipedia]{.otherLang lang="la"} and
      [Sirenia]{.otherLang lang="la"}; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} members of the order [Rodentia]{.otherLang
      lang="la"}, other than

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} prairie dogs ([Cynomys sp.]{.otherLang
        lang="la"}), African Giant Pouched Rats ([Cricetomys
        gambianus]{.otherLang lang="la"}) and squirrels of the family
        [Sciuridae]{.otherLang lang="la"}, from any country, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any other members of the order from Africa.
        ([animal réglementé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[species]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[species]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of germplasm, the
    species of the animal that it is from. ([espèce]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[undesignated area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[undesignated area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a
    species of regulated animal, an area identified as an undesignated
    area for that species in the import reference document. ([région non
    désignée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 7
- SOR/97-85, s. 4
- SOR/2001-210, s. 3
- SOR/2003-264, s. 2

### [Germplasm]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547130 .Subheading}

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall import germplasm of any regulated animal except

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with a permit issued by the Minister
    under section 160; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with all applicable provisions of the
    import reference document.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Canine semen is exempt from the requirements of these
  Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-210, s. 3
:::

### [Regulated Animals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547138 .Subheading}

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 51, no person
  shall import a regulated animal except

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with a permit issued by the Minister
    under section 160; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with subsections (2) to (6) and all
    applicable provisions of the import reference document.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A regulated animal may be imported without a permit
  from an area that is an equivalent risk area for an animal of that
  species if it is accompanied by a certificate of an official
  veterinarian from that area that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} clearly identifies the animal and its area of
    origin; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} verifies that a veterinarian inspected the animal
    within five days before it was exported to Canada and found it to be
    clinically healthy and fit to travel without unnecessary suffering.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A regulated animal may be imported without a permit
  from an area that is a low risk area for an animal of that species if
  the person importing the animal meets any applicable post-entry
  conditions set out in the import reference document and the animal is
  accompanied by a certificate of an official veterinarian from that
  area that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} clearly identifies the animal and its area of
    origin; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shows that the animal conforms with all applicable
    conditions, other than post-entry conditions, that are set out in
    the import reference document.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A regulated animal may be imported without a permit
  from an area that is an undesignated area for an animal of that
  species if there are provisions in the import reference document that
  relate to the importation of that species and those provisions are
  complied with.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A regulated animal that is imported for slaughter or
  for confinement in a restricted premises, such as a zoo or laboratory
  facility, may be imported without a permit if all applicable
  provisions of the import reference document are complied with and the
  following conditions are met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the animal is to be transported directly from its
    port of entry to its destination in accordance with a licence that
    has been issued by the Minister under section 160;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the animal is being transported by means of a
    conveyance that has had all exits by which the animal could leave
    the conveyance sealed by an official of the government of the
    country from which it is imported; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the animal will not come into contact with the
    national herd in Canada.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} A regulated animal, other than a porcine, may be
  imported without a permit if it is transported directly between Rainy
  River, Ontario and Sprague, Manitoba via the state of Minnesota by
  means of a conveyance that has had all exits by which the animal could
  leave the conveyance sealed by an official of the government of Canada
  or of the United States.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-210, s. 3
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 13](#1515103-1516143){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515103-1516143.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 12"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import a
regulated animal if a certificate required by this Part, or required
under an import permit required by this Part, contains any false or
misleading information.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 8
- SOR/79-839, s. 7
- SOR/83-900, s. 1
- SOR/85-689, s. 1
- SOR/97-85, s. 5
- SOR/2001-210, s. 3
:::

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall import a regulated animal that has, on or after the day
  described in subsection (2), been in contact with another animal, or
  with a thing used in respect of another animal, if that other animal
  poses a greater risk for the transmission or spreading of a disease
  than does the regulated animal.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The day referred to in subsection (1) is the earliest
  of the days on which begins any period of isolation, any testing or
  the performance of any other procedure required in respect of the
  importation by the import reference document or by a permit issued
  under section 160.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/83-900, s. 2
- SOR/85-689, s. 2(F)
- SOR/97-85, s. 6
- SOR/2001-210, s. 3
:::

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import a
regulated animal unless it is clearly identified at the time of
importation.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-210, s. 3
:::

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** Every person who imports
a regulated animal shall keep records that clearly indicate its original
source and the date and place of importation.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2001-210, s. 3
:::

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  request of the government of the United States, subject to subsection
  (3), a person may import a regulated animal from the United States for
  the purpose of re-entering the United States without meeting the
  requirements of paragraphs 12(1)(a) and (b), sections 15 and 16 and
  Part XV, if the President of the Canadian Food Inspection Agency
  determines that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a transportation route within the United States
    usually used to transport the animal cannot be used because the
    route is obstructed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no reasonable alternative transportation route
    within the United States exists; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} as a result of the obstruction, the welfare of the
    animal is at risk.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), if a regulated animal is
  exported to the United States under subsection 69.1(1), a person may
  import that animal without meeting the requirements of paragraphs
  12(1)(a) and (b), sections 15 and 16 and Part XV.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall import a regulated animal under
  subsection (1) or (2) unless it is accompanied by a certificate issued
  by a veterinary inspector, a certificate issued by an accredited
  veterinarian that is approved in writing by a veterinary inspector or
  a certificate of an official veterinarian that specifies

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the species of the animal and the number of animals
    of each species being transported; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of the seal affixed to the conveyance
    used for the transport of the animal, unless, in the opinion of the
    veterinary inspector, the accredited veterinarian or the official
    veterinarian, the conveyance cannot be sealed.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-114, s. 2](#1294805-1295507){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1294805-1295507.html"
  role="button"}
:::

**[[18 to 33.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18_to_33.1
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2001-210, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Importation of Animal Products]{.HTitleText1} {#h-547172 .Part}

### [Dairy Products and Certain Eggs]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547173 .Subheading}

- **[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall import milk or milk products into Canada from a country
  other than the United States or from a part of such a country, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the country or part of the country is designated as
    free of foot and mouth disease pursuant to section 7; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person produces a certificate of origin signed
    by an official of the government of the country of origin that shows
    that the country of origin or part of such a country is the
    designated country or part thereof referred to in paragraph (a).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), no person shall import
  unfertilized bird eggs or egg products into Canada from a country
  other than the United States or from a part of such a country, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the country of origin or part of such a country is
    designated as free of avian pneumoencephalitis (Newcastle disease)
    and fowl plague pursuant to section 7;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person produces a certificate of origin signed
    by an official of the government of the country of origin that shows
    that the country of origin or part of such a country is the
    designated country or part thereof referred to in paragraph (a); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the eggs are packed in containers that are clean
    and free from dirt and residue of eggs.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (2)(a) does not apply to eggs imported into
  Canada if they are transported under seal of an inspector direct from
  the place of entry to a processed egg product establishment approved
  by the Minister.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} In subsection (3), [[processed egg product
  establishment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an establishment where eggs
  or processed egg products are processed, treated or preserved by the
  holder of a licence that is issued under paragraph 20(1)(b) of the
  [Safe Food for Canadians Act](/eng/acts/S-1.1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 21
- SOR/92-650, s. 1
- SOR/97-85, s. 28
- [SOR/2018-108, s. 405](#1044676-1110073){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044676-1110073.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-34-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 34"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[34.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsections 34(1)
  and (2), a person may import an animal product referred to therein if
  the person produces a document that shows the details of the treatment
  of the animal product and the inspector is satisfied, based on the
  source of the document, the information contained in the document and
  any other relevant information available to the inspector and, where
  necessary, on an inspection of the animal product, that the
  importation of the animal product into Canada would not, or would not
  be likely to, result in the introduction into Canada, or the spread
  within Canada, of a vector, disease or toxic substance.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsections 34(1) and (2), a person
  may import a dairy product or an egg from a country other than the
  United States under and in accordance with a permit issued by the
  Minister under section 160.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2000-184, s. 65\]]{.Repealed}

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Importation of Animal By-products, Animal Pathogens and other Things]{.HTitleText1} {#h-547193 .Part}

### [Importation Prohibited]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547194 .Subheading}

**[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import
into Canada an animal by-product, manure or a thing containing an animal
by-product or manure except in accordance with this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-839, s. 15
- SOR/80-428, s. 4
- SOR/92-708, s. 1
- SOR/97-85, s. 34
:::

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person may import into Canada an animal by-product, manure or a thing
  containing an animal by-product or manure, other than one described in
  section 45, 46, 47, 47.1, 49, 50, 51, 51.2 or 53, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the country of origin is the United States and the
    by-product, manure or thing is not derived from an animal of the
    subfamily [Bovinae]{.otherLang lang="la"} or [Caprinae]{.otherLang
    lang="la"};

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the country of origin, or the part of that country,
    is designated under section 7 as being free of, or as posing a
    negligible risk for, any reportable disease, any disease referred to
    in Schedule VII and any serious epizootic disease to which the
    species from which the by-product, manure or thing was derived is
    susceptible and that can be transmitted by the by-product, manure or
    thing, and the person produces a certificate of origin signed by an
    official of the government of that country attesting to that origin;
    or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the by-product, manure or thing has been collected,
    treated, prepared, processed, stored and handled in a manner that
    would prevent the introduction into Canada of any reportable
    disease, any disease referred to in Schedule VII and any serious
    epizootic disease to which the species from which the by-product,
    manure or thing was derived is susceptible and that can be
    transmitted by the by-product, manure or thing, and the person
    produces a certificate signed by an official of the government of
    the country of origin that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} attests that the by-product, manure or thing has
      been collected, treated, prepared, processed, stored and handled
      in that manner, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} shows the details of how it was collected,
      treated, prepared, processed, stored and handled.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of manure
  found in or on a vehicle that is entering Canada from the United
  States if the manure was produced by animals, other than swine, that
  are being transported by the vehicle.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 23(F)
- SOR/80-428, s. 4
- SOR/82-590, s. 2
- SOR/97-85, s. 34
- SOR/2009-18, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-41-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 41"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[41.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 41, a person may
  import into Canada an animal by-product or a thing containing an
  animal by-product, other than one described in section 45, 46, 47,
  47.1, 49, 50, 51, 51.2 or 53, if an inspector has reasonable grounds
  to believe that the importation of the by-product or thing, by its
  nature, end use or the manner in which it has been processed, would
  not, or would not be likely to, result in the introduction into Canada
  of any reportable disease, any disease referred to in Schedule VII and
  any serious epizootic disease to which the species from which the
  by-product was derived is susceptible and that can be transmitted by
  the by-product, and the by-product or thing is not intended for use as
  animal food or as an ingredient in animal food.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, in respect of any animal by-product
  or thing containing an animal by-product that has been imported in
  accordance with subsection (1), use or cause it to be used as animal
  food or as an ingredient in animal food.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 34
- SOR/97-362, s. 1
- SOR/2009-18, s. 7
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-41.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 41.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Raw Wool, Hair or Bristles, Hide or Skin]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547213 .Subheading}

**[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** A person may import into
Canada raw wool, hair or bristles or an untanned hide or skin from a
country or a part of a country not referred to in section 41 if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the article is transported under seal of an inspector
  directly from the place of entry to an approved disinfection
  establishment for disinfection in accordance with these Regulations;
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an untanned hide or skin, an inspector
  is satisfied that the article

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is a hard dried hide or skin of an animal,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has been pickled in a solution of salt containing
    mineral acid and was packed in a leakproof container while still wet
    with such solution; or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is the hide or skin of an animal and has been
    treated with lime so as to become de-haired.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 34
- SOR/97-478, s. 10(E)
:::

### [Animal Glands and Organs]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547222 .Subheading}

- **[[42.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person may import raw animal glands and animal organs into Canada from
  a country or a part of a country not referred to in section 41 if the
  glands and organs are transported under seal of an inspector directly
  from the place of entry to an establishment approved by the Minister
  for the purposes of processing in accordance with subsection (2).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An establishment referred to in subsection (1) shall
  process the raw glands and organs in a manner that would prevent the
  introduction of any reportable disease, any disease referred to in
  Schedule VII and any serious epizootic disease to which the species
  from which the glands and organs was taken is susceptible and that can
  be transmitted by the glands and organs.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who imports raw animal glands or animal
  organs into Canada shall not transport them or cause them to be
  transported to any place other than an establishment referred to in
  subsection (1) until after they have been processed in accordance with
  subsection (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 34
- SOR/2009-18, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-42.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 42.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Boneless Beef]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547229 .Subheading}

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** A person may import into
Canada cooked, boneless beef from a country or a part of a country not
referred to in section 41 if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it was processed in a place and in a manner approved
  by the Minister;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is accompanied by a meat inspection certificate of
  an official veterinarian of the exporting country in a form approved
  by the Minister; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} on examination, an inspector is satisfied that it is
  thoroughly cooked.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 24(F)
- SOR/97-85, s. 35
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 14(F)](#1515105-1516144){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515105-1516144.html"
  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-43-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 43"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/97-85,
s. 35\]]{.Repealed}

### [Gluestock]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547237 .Subheading}

- **[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall import gluestock into Canada unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the conditions referred to in section 41 are met;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person importing it transports it directly from
    the place of entry to an establishment approved by the Minister for
    purposes of processing the gluestock in accordance with subsection
    (2).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An establishment referred to in paragraph (1)(b)
  shall process the gluestock in a manner that would prevent the
  introduction of any reportable disease, any disease referred to in
  Schedule VII and any serious epizootic disease to which the species
  from which the gluestock was derived is susceptible and that can be
  transmitted by the gluestock.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who imports gluestock into Canada shall not
  transport it or cause it to be transported to any place other than an
  establishment referred to in paragraph (1)(b) until it has been
  processed in accordance with subsection (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 36
- SOR/2009-18, s. 9
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 15](#1515107-1516146){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515107-1516146.html"
  role="button"}
:::
