Version](section-45-20090630.html "Link to previous version of section 45"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Meat and Bone Meal, Bone Meal, Blood Meal, Tankage, Feather Meal, Fish Meal, Rendering Plant Products, Animal Manure, Garbage and Ship's Refuse]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547246 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2006-147, s. 14
- SOR/2009-18, s. 10(F)

\]
:::

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import
into Canada meat and bone meal, bone meal, blood meal, tankage (meat
meal), feather meal, fish meal or any other product of a rendering plant
unless, in addition to the requirements of sections 166 to 171,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the country of origin, or the part of that country,
  is designated under section 7 as being free of, or as posing a
  negligible risk for, any reportable disease, any disease referred to
  in Schedule VII and any serious epizootic disease to which the species
  from which the product was derived is susceptible and that can be
  transmitted by the product, and the person produces a certificate of
  origin signed by an official of the government of that country
  attesting to that origin; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an inspector has reasonable grounds to believe that
  the product has been processed in a manner that would prevent the
  introduction of any reportable disease, any disease referred to in
  Schedule VII and any serious epizootic disease to which the species
  from which the product was derived is susceptible and that can be
  transmitted by the product.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 25
- SOR/80-428, s. 5
- SOR/92-708, s. 2
- SOR/97-85, s. 36
- SOR/97-362, s. 2
- SOR/2006-147, s. 15
- SOR/2009-18, s. 11
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-46-20070712.html "Link to previous version of section 46"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 47.1,
no person shall import into Canada garbage or refuse that contains or is
suspected of containing an animal product or an animal by-product from
any country other than the United States.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 26
- SOR/78-597, s. 5
- SOR/79-295, s. 6
- SOR/80-428, s. 6
- SOR/81-348, s. 4
- SOR/82-590, s. 3
- SOR/88-409, s. 1
- SOR/92-708, s. 3
:::

- **[[47.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section,

  [[aircraft garbage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[aircraft garbage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means garbage that
      contains or is suspected to contain an animal product or an animal
      by-product and that originated in food that was taken on board an
      aircraft and was served or intended to be served for consumption
      on the aircraft by the passengers or crew of the aircraft en route
      to Canada; ([déchets d'aéronef]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[animal manure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[animal manure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means manure that is
      produced by animals on board a ship or aircraft while *en route*
      to or after arrival in Canada; ([fumier animal]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[ship's refuse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ship's refuse]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means refuse that contains
      or is suspected to contain an animal product or an animal
      by-product and that originated in food that was taken on board a
      vessel and was served or intended to be served for consumption on
      the vessel by the passengers or crew of the vessel en route to
      Canada; ([rebuts de navire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (6) and (7), no person shall
  discharge in Canada ship's refuse or animal manure from a country
  other than the United States unless the ship's refuse or animal manure
  is discharged in a closed leakproof container at the first port of
  entry or, where necessary, at another place approved by the Minister,
  under the supervision of or to the satisfaction of an inspector in
  such a manner that the discharge would only pose a negligible risk of
  the introduction into Canada, or spread within Canada, of a vector,
  disease or toxic substance and is without delay

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} incinerated;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} heated throughout to a temperature of at least
    100^0^C for a period of not less than 30 minutes and disposed of in
    a sanitary landfill site approved by the Minister, under the
    supervision of or to the satisfaction of an inspector in such a
    manner that the disposition would only pose a negligible risk of the
    introduction into Canada, or spread within Canada, of a vector,
    disease or toxic substance; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} moved to a sanitary landfill site approved by the
    Minister and buried with a covering of not less than 1.8 meters
    under the supervision of or to the satisfaction of an inspector in
    such a manner that the burial would only pose a negligible risk of
    the introduction into Canada, or spread within Canada, of a vector,
    disease or toxic substance.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (6) and (7), no person shall,
  without meeting the requirements of subsection (2), discharge in
  Canada ship's refuse from the United States that contains or is
  suspected of containing an animal product or an animal by-product that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} did not originate in the United States or Canada;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not eligible for importation into the United
    States or Canada, other than as ship's refuse.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3), (6) and (7), no person
  shall discharge in Canada aircraft garbage from any country or ship's
  refuse from the United States, unless the aircraft garbage or ship's
  refuse is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} treated and disposed of in accordance with the
    requirements of subsection (2); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} without delay moved, under the supervision of or to
    the satisfaction of an inspector in such a manner that the movement
    would only pose a negligible risk of the introduction into Canada,
    or spread within Canada, of a vector, disease or toxic substance,
    and disposed of in a sanitary landfill site approved by the
    Minister.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (6) and (7), no person shall
  discharge ship's refuse or animal manure referred to in subsection
  (2), ship's refuse referred to in subsection (3) or aircraft garbage
  or ship's refuse referred to in subsection (4), from an aircraft or
  vessel, as the case may be, at a place where it cannot be treated and
  disposed of in accordance with this section.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Aircraft garbage, ship's refuse and animal manure may
  be discharged at a place where that garbage, refuse or manure cannot
  be treated and disposed of in accordance with this section if it is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} without delay transported to, and treated and
    disposed of in accordance with this section at, another place
    approved by the Minister; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} handled, transported and kept under the supervision
    of or to the satisfaction of an inspector in such a manner that the
    handling, transportation or keeping would only pose a negligible
    risk of the introduction into Canada, or spread within Canada, of a
    vector, disease or toxic substance.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may permit aircraft garbage, ship's
  refuse and animal manure to be discharged by a person at a place where
  it is temporarily impossible to treat and dispose of that garbage,
  refuse or manure in accordance with this section, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister is satisfied, based on the
    circumstances, that it will subsequently be treated and disposed of
    in accordance with this section; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is stored in the interim at that place in such a
    manner and for such period of time as an inspector may specify in
    order to prevent the introduction into Canada, or spread within
    Canada, of a vector, disease or toxic substance.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} Every person who transports aircraft garbage, ship's
  refuse or animal manure under subsection (6) or stores aircraft
  garbage, ship's refuse or animal manure under subsection (7) shall
  comply with any requirements of an inspector under those provisions in
  respect of that transportation or storage, as the case may be.

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/97-85, s. 37\]]{.Repealed}

- [(10)]{.lawlabel} The owner or person in charge of a vessel or
  aircraft referred to in this section shall ensure that the
  requirements of this section are complied with.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/90-349, s. 1
- SOR/92-708, s. 3
- SOR/97-85, s. 37
- SOR/97-151, s. 28
- SOR/2000-184, s. 66
- SOR/2009-18, ss. 12, 19(F)
- SOR/2012-286, s. 51(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-47.1-20090630.html "Link to previous version of section 47.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Ships' Stores]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547284 .Subheading}

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** A person may import into
Canada meat or meat by-products from a country or a part of a country
not referred to in section 41 if the meat or meat by-product is kept on
a ship as ships' stores.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 27(F)
- SOR/97-85, s. 38
:::

### [Carcasses of Game Animals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547288 .Subheading}

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall import the carcass of a game animal unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Canada Border Services Agency has determined
    under the [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) that the carcass
    originated in the United States; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person importing the carcass holds a hunting
    permit for that game animal issued under the applicable legislation
    of the United States or a state of the United States.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The number of carcasses that a person may import into
  Canada shall be the number indicated on the hunting permit described
  in paragraph (1)(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 28(F)
- SOR/97-85, s. 38
- SOR/2009-18, s. 13
- SOR/2012-286, s. 52(E)
- SOR/2017-94, s. 13
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-49-20121214.html "Link to previous version of section 49"){.PITLink}
:::

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547296 .Subheading}

**[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import
into Canada

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} matted or blood-stained wool, hair or bristles; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any animal by-product or manure that was derived from
  an animal affected with anthrax, foot-and-mouth disease, rinderpest or
  Bovine Spongiform Encephalopathy or any reportable disease, any
  disease referred to in Schedule VII and any serious epizootic disease
  to which the species from which the by-product or manure was derived
  is susceptible and that can be transmitted by the by-product or
  manure.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 39
- SOR/2009-18, s. 14
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-50-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 50"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Animal Pathogens, Animals or Other Organisms, Animal Blood and Animal Serum]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547302 .Subheading}

**[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 51.2,
no person shall, except under and in accordance with a permit issued by
the Minister under section 160, import into Canada any

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} animal pathogen;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} animal, animal product, animal by-product or other
  organism carrying an animal pathogen or part of one; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} animal blood or animal serum, other than a veterinary
  biologic,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} from a bird or a mammal, except a member of the
    orders [Rodentia]{.otherLang lang="la"}, [Cetacea]{.otherLang
    lang="la"}, [Pinnipedia]{.otherLang lang="la"} and
    [Sirenia]{.otherLang lang="la"}, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that are to be used in animals.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/95-54, s. 2
- SOR/97-85, s. 40
- SOR/97-478, s. 11
- SOR/2004-80, s. 16(E)
- [SOR/2018-79, s. 4(F)](#1044379-1244984){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044379-1244984.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-51-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 51"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[51.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.1 .sectionLabel}** Where any animal
pathogen, animal or other organism, animal blood or animal serum
referred to in section 51 is imported into Canada under a permit, no
person shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} move the imported pathogen, animal or other organism,
  blood or serum from the place or places specified in the permit to any
  other place, except under and in accordance with that permit or
  another permit issued for that purpose by the Minister under section
  160; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} introduce into an animal the imported pathogen,
  animal or other organism, blood or serum, except under and in
  accordance with that permit or another permit issued for that purpose
  by the Minister under section 160.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/95-54, s. 2
:::

- **[[51.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person may import into Canada animal blood or animal serum, other than
  a veterinary biologic, if it does not contain an animal pathogen or
  part of one and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the country of origin is the United States and the
    blood or serum is not derived from an animal of the subfamily
    [Bovinae]{.otherLang lang="la"} or [Caprinae]{.otherLang lang="la"};

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the country of origin, or the part of that country,
    is designated under section 7 as being free of, or as posing a
    negligible risk for, any reportable disease, any disease referred to
    in Schedule VII and any serious epizootic disease to which the
    species from which the blood or serum was derived is susceptible and
    that can be transmitted by the blood or serum, and the person
    produces a certificate of origin signed by an official of the
    government of that country attesting to that origin; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the blood or serum has been collected, treated,
    prepared, processed, stored and handled in a manner that would
    prevent the introduction into Canada of any reportable disease, any
    disease referred to in Schedule VII and any serious epizootic
    disease to which the species from which the blood or serum was
    derived is susceptible and that can be transmitted by the blood or
    serum, and the person produces a certificate signed by an official
    of the government of the country of origin that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} attests that the blood or serum has been
      collected, treated, prepared, processed, stored and handled in
      that manner, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} shows the details of how the blood or serum was
      collected, treated, prepared, processed, stored and handled.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall in any manner expose to or use in a
  live animal, animal blood or animal serum imported in accordance with
  subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 41
- SOR/97-478, s. 12
- SOR/2009-18, s. 15
- SOR/2012-286, s. 53(F)
- [SOR/2018-79, s. 4(F)](#1044379-1244985){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044379-1244985.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-51.2-20121214.html "Link to previous version of section 51.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Importation Permitted]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547326 .Subheading}

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Part, a
  person may import into Canada an animal by-product if the person
  produces a document that shows the details of the treatment of the
  by-product and an inspector has reasonable grounds to believe ---
  based on the source of the document, the information contained in the
  document and any other relevant information available to the inspector
  and, if necessary, on an inspection of the by-product --- that the
  importation of the by-product would not, or would not be likely to,
  result in the introduction into Canada, or the spread within Canada,
  of a vector, disease or toxic substance.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding anything in this Part, a person may
  import an animal by-product under and in accordance with a permit
  issued by the Minister under section 160.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2000-184, s. 67\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/92-650, s. 3
- SOR/95-475, s. 4(F)
- SOR/97-85, s. 42
- SOR/2000-184, s. 67
- SOR/2009-18, s. 16
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-52-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 52"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Animal Food]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547333 .Subheading}

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall import into Canada any animal food that contains an
  animal product or animal by-product unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the country of origin

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} of the animal food is the United States and the
      animal food is not derived from an animal of the subfamily
      [Bovinae]{.otherLang lang="la"} or [Caprinae]{.otherLang
      lang="la"}; or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} or the part of that country of the animal food
      --- and each animal product and animal by-product contained in
      that food --- is designated under section 7 as being free of, or
      as posing a negligible risk for, any reportable disease, any
      disease referred to in Schedule VII and any serious epizootic
      disease to which the species from which the food, product or
      by-product was derived is susceptible and that can be transmitted
      by the food, product or by-product, and the person produces a
      certificate of origin signed by an official of the government of
      that country attesting to that origin; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of animal food that is carried on board
    a vessel, the master of the vessel certifies that no ruminants or
    swine, other than those imported in accordance with a permit issued
    under section 160, were taken on board the vessel.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall import into Canada any animal product
  or animal by-product that is to be used as animal food or as an
  ingredient in animal food unless the country of origin, or the part of
  that country, is designated under section 7 as being free of, or as
  posing a negligible risk for, any reportable disease, any disease
  referred to in Schedule VII and any serious epizootic disease to which
  the species from which the product or by-product was derived is
  susceptible and that can be transmitted by the product or by-product,
  and the person produces a certificate of origin signed by an official
  of the government of that country attesting to that origin.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 29
- SOR/97-85, s. 43
- SOR/97-362, s. 3
- SOR/97-478, s. 13
- SOR/2002-438, s. 7(F)
- SOR/2009-18, s. 17
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-53-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 53"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART V]{.HLabel1}[Importation of Fodder]{.HTitleText1} {#h-547343 .Part}

**[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import
into Canada from any country other than the United States any fodder to
be used to feed ruminants, swine or horses except grains, cereals and
seeds.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 43
:::

## [PART VI]{.HLabel1}[Importation of Packing Material, Beehives and Beeswax]{.HTitleText1} {#h-547347 .Part}

### [Packing Material]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547348 .Subheading}

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall import into Canada hay, straw or grasses in which
  merchandise, goods or articles are packed unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the shipment is accompanied by a certificate of an
    official veterinarian of the exporting country showing that the hay,
    straw or grasses were disinfected in a manner approved by the
    Minister; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where no such certificate accompanies the shipment,
    the hay, straw or grasses are without delay

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} disinfected under the supervision of an inspector
      at a fumigation station approved by the Minister, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} incinerated.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), hay, straw or grasses
  in which merchandise, goods or articles are packed may be imported
  into Canada from a country of origin or part of such a country
  designated by the Minister as free of foot-and-mouth disease pursuant
  to section 7.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 44
- SOR/2012-286, s. 54(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-55-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 55"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/97-85,
s. 45\]]{.Repealed}

### [Beehives and Beeswax]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547359 .Subheading}

**[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import
into Canada

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} used beehives or used beehive equipment; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} bee products for bee feeding unless

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} they are accompanied by a certificate stating that
    they have been treated in a manner approved by the Minister to
    prevent the introduction or spread of any disease, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} they are transported under seal of an inspector
    direct from the point of importation to an establishment approved by
    the Minister for treatment.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 46
:::

## [PART VII]{.HLabel1}[Quarantine of Imported Animals]{.HTitleText1} {#h-547367 .Part}

### [Quarantine Ports and Inspection Ports]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547368 .Subheading}

- **[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  as otherwise provided in these Regulations, every animal imported into
  Canada may be admitted only at a quarantine port, an inspection port
  or other place approved by the Minister.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every animal imported into Canada by air from any
  country other than the United States may be admitted only at Gander,
  Halifax, Montreal, Toronto, Winnipeg, Calgary, Edmonton, Vancouver or
  other place approved by the Minister.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided in these Regulations,
  every animal imported into Canada is subject to inspection, testing
  and treatment at a quarantine port, an inspection port or other place
  approved by the Minister for inspection purposes.

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, for the
purpose of preventing the introduction of communicable disease into
Canada or into any other country from Canada, require any animal
imported into Canada to be quarantined, and the provisions of section
91.4 apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 47
:::

**[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** An animal required to be
quarantined pursuant to these Regulations shall be quarantined at a
quarantine port or other place approved by the Minister for quarantine
purposes.

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** No person shall remove
from a quarantine port, inspection port or other place approved by the
Minister pursuant to section 60 an animal subject to quarantine pursuant
to section 59 until the animal has been quarantined by an inspector.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 48
:::

**[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may order
the owner or the person having the possession, care or control of an
animal imported by or under these Regulations to remove the animal from
Canada or to destroy the animal if the animal fails to prove negative to
any test for a disease required by or under these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 48
:::

**[[63 to 65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63_to_65 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/97-85, s. 48\]]{.Repealed}

**[[66 and 67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66_and_67
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/98-584, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

**[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/97-85,
s. 49\]]{.Repealed}

## [PART VIII]{.HLabel1}[Exportation of Animals, Animal Products and Products of Rendering Plants]{.HTitleText1} {#h-547386 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2006-147, s. 16

\]
:::

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547389 .Subheading}

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, no person
  shall export animal germplasm, livestock or poultry unless the person
  has

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provided to a veterinary inspector or accredited
    veterinarian evidence that the animal germplasm, livestock or
    poultry meets the sanitary requirements of the importing country;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} obtained a certificate issued by a veterinary
    inspector --- or an accredited veterinarian , endorsed by a
    veterinary inspector --- that identifies the animal germplasm,
    livestock or poultry and sets out the following information

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a statement that a veterinary inspector or
      accredited veterinarian has inspected the animal germplasm,
      livestock or poultry and found it to be free from any communicable
      diseases, and that the animal germplasm, livestock or poultry
      meets the sanitary requirements of the importing country,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date and place of inspection, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if tests are required prior to export, a
      statement that they were performed, and that the results of the
      tests on the animal germplasm, livestock or poultry meet the
      testing requirements of the importing country; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} provided to a veterinary inspector any requested
    information or documentation that demonstrates how the person
    intends to meet the requirements of Parts XII and XV respecting the
    health, protection and transport of animal germplasm, livestock or
    poultry, as applicable.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(b) does not apply in respect of
  animal germplasm, livestock or poultry destined for export to the
  United States if certification is not requested by the United States.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall export animal semen out of Canada
  unless the semen, from the time it was collected, was stored in an
  animal semen production centre or other place approved by the
  Minister.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall export animal germplasm, livestock or
  poultry unless the certificate referred to in paragraph (1)(b) bears
  the mark of the official export stamp referred to in subsection (4).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The official export stamp required by this section
  shall contain the words "Government of Canada --- CANADIAN FOOD
  INSPECTION AGENCY --- Gouvernement du Canada --- AGENCE CANADIENNE
  D'INSPECTION DES ALIMENTS" and it shall be applied on a certificate
  only by a veterinary inspector or a person authorized by one.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person except a veterinary inspector or a person
  authorized by him shall have in his possession an official export
  stamp or any facsimile thereof.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of this section, [[animal
  germplasm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to livestock, means semen, male or
    female germ cells or genetic material taken from a male or female
    germ cell and includes embryos;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to poultry, means hatching eggs and
    semen; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} with respect to bees, means semen.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(b) and subsection (3) do not apply to a
  regulated animal, other than a porcine, if it is transported directly
  between Rainy River, Ontario and Sprague, Manitoba via the state of
  Minnesota by means of a conveyance that has had all exits by which the
  animal could leave the conveyance sealed by an official of the
  Government of Canada or of the Government of the United States.

::: HistoricalNote

:::

- **[[69.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph 69(1)(b) and
  subsection 69(3) and subject to subsection (2), a person may export a
  [[regulated animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 10,
  to the United States for the purpose of re-entering Canada without
  meeting the requirements of Part XV if the President of the Canadian
  Food Inspection Agency determines that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a transportation route within Canada usually used
    to transport the animal cannot be used because the route is
    obstructed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no reasonable alternative transportation route in
    Canada exists; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} as a result of the obstruction, the welfare of the
    animal is at risk.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall export a [[regulated
  animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 10, under
  subsection (1) unless it is accompanied by a certificate issued by a
  veterinary inspector, or a certificate issued by an accredited
  veterinarian that is approved in writing by a veterinary inspector,
  that specifies

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the species of the animal and the number of animals
    of each species being transported; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of the seal affixed to the conveyance
    used for the transport of the animal, unless, in the opinion of the
    veterinary inspector or the accredited veterinarian, the conveyance
    cannot be sealed.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-114, s. 3](#1294807-1295518){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1294807-1295518.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 17](#1515121-1516163){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515121-1516163.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-69.1-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 69.1"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, no person
  shall export out of Canada any of the following products unless the
  person meets the requirements of subsection (1.1):

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a substance from a rendering plant;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a fertilizer that contains a substance from a
    rendering plant;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a fertilizer supplement that contains a substance
    from a rendering plant;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an animal food that contains a substance from a
    rendering plant.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A person may export out of Canada a product
  referred to in subsection (1) if the person has

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provided to an inspector or veterinary inspector
    evidence that the product meets the importing country's sanitary
    requirements with respect to the importation of the product;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} obtained a certificate issued by an inspector or
    veterinary inspector that identifies the product and sets out the
    following information

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the product meets the importing
      country's sanitary requirements with respect to the importation of
      the product, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if tests are required prior to export, a
      statement that they were performed, and that the results of the
      tests on the products meet the testing requirements of the
      importing country; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} confirmed that the results of any tests that have
    been carried out confirm that the product meets the requirements of
    the relevant sections of the Act and these Regulations.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall export a product of a rendering
  plant, or a fertilizer, fertilizer supplement or animal food that
  contains a product of a rendering plant, unless the certificate
  referred to in subsection (1) bears the mark of the official export
  stamp referred to in subsection (3).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The official export stamp required by this section
  shall contain the words "Government of Canada --- CANADIAN FOOD
  INSPECTION AGENCY --- Gouvernement du Canada --- AGENCE CANADIENNE
  D'INSPECTION DES ALIMENTS" and it shall be applied on a certificate
  only by an inspector, a veterinary inspector or a person authorized by
  an inspector or a veterinary inspector.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person except an inspector, a veterinary inspector
  or a person authorized by an inspector or a veterinary inspector shall
  have in his or her possession an official export stamp or any
  facsimile of one.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-147, s. 17
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 18(F)](#1124109-1124691){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124109-1124691.html"
  role="button"}

  role="button"}

### [Rest Period]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547418 .Subheading}

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall export any animal out of Canada to any country other than the
  United States unless he has obtained the consent of an inspector if
  the animal has not been at the place of embarkation for at least 12
  hours.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the export of
  animals out of Canada from an airport.

## [PART VIII.1]{.HLabel1}[Hatcheries and Supply Flocks]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1378616 .Part}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1378617 .Subheading}

**[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** The following definitions
apply in this Part.

[[balut]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[balut]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fertilized commercial poultry
    egg containing a partially developed embryo that is intended for
    human consumption. ([balut]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[commercial poultry]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[commercial poultry]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means poultry that are
    used for the production of meat, poultry products or by-products ---
    including balut --- for human consumption or for breeding to produce
    commercial poultry, but does not include poultry that are kept for
    any other reason, including poultry that are kept, bred or sold as
    pets or for use in shows, races, exhibitions or
    competitions. ([volaille commerciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[supply flock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[supply flock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a primary breeding flock
    of commercial poultry, or a flock that is descended from a such a
    flock, that produces eggs or chicks that are supplied to a
    hatchery. ([troupeau fournisseur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Testing Standards]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Testing Standards]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canadian
    Hatchery and Supply Flock Testing Standards, prepared by the Agency
    and published on its website, as amended from time to time. ([Normes
    d'épreuves]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-218, s. 1](#1378233-1378624){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1378233-1378624.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-72-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 72"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Hatchery Licensing Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1378625 .Subheading}

**[[72.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.1 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies to
a hatchery that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} receives eggs produced by a supply flock;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} incubates eggs for the production of commercial
  poultry; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} has a minimum setting capacity of 1 000 eggs.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-218, s. 1](#1378233-1378631){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1378233-1378631.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-72.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 72.1"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[72.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.2 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
operate a hatchery except in accordance with a licence that is issued by
the Minister under section 160.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-218, s. 1](#1378233-1378634){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1378233-1378634.html"
  role="button"}
:::

- **[[72.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  licence expires two years after the date of issuance or renewal that
  is specified in it, unless the licence is revoked before that date.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A licence is revoked if the licence holder ceases
  operation of the hatchery for 12 consecutive months or surrenders the
  licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-218, s. 1](#1378233-1378639){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1378233-1378639.html"
  role="button"}
:::

- **[[72.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  hatchery operator shall prepare, keep and maintain a written
  preventive control plan as a condition of their licence.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The hatchery operator shall implement their
  preventive control plan.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The preventive control plan shall include a
  description of the measures that have been or are to be implemented by
  the hatchery operator in order to mitigate the risk of introduction
  and spread of the biological hazards set out in Part I of the Testing
  Standards and to control and eliminate those hazards, in relation to
  the following elements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sanitation, biosecurity, biocontainment, pest
    control and chemicals;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} employee training in biosecurity measures;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} maintenance, cleaning and disinfection of hatchery
    facilities, equipment and related materials;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} maintenance of buildings, other structures and
    surrounding premises;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} feed and vaccines;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} receiving, transportation and storage; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} traceability.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-218, s. 1](#1378233-1378652){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1378233-1378652.html"
  role="button"}
:::

- **[[72.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  hatchery operator shall meet the requirements set out under the
  following headings of the Code of Practice for the Care and Handling
  of Hatching Eggs, Breeders, Chickens and Turkeys, published by the
  National Farm Animal Care Council, as amended from time to time, that
  are applicable to the species of poultry they are incubating:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Personnel Knowledge and Skills;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Hatcheries;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Housing and Environment;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} Feed and Water;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} Flock Health Management; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} Euthanasia.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The hatchery operator shall include in their
  preventive control plan a description of the measures that they have
  implemented or will implement to meet the requirements referred to in
  subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-218, s. 1](#1378233-1378663){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1378233-1378663.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Testing Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1378664 .Subheading}

- **[[72.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  hatchery operator shall conduct sampling and testing in accordance
  with Part I of the Testing Standards to monitor the sanitation of the
  hatchery during periods of operation and for the presence of diseases.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The hatchery operator shall keep a record of the
  results of sampling and testing conducted in the hatchery.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-218, s. 1](#1378233-1378669){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1378233-1378669.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Supply Flocks]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1378670 .Subheading}

**[[72.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.7 .sectionLabel}** A hatchery operator
shall obtain all eggs from a supply flock operator that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prepares, keeps and maintains a preventive control
  plan that describes the measures that have been or are to be
  implemented to comply with paragraphs (d) to (f);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} implements the preventive control plan;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} keeps a record that describes any deviation from the
  preventive control plan and the corrective measures that are taken;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} meets the sampling and testing requirements that are
  set out in Part II of the Testing Standards;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements set out under the following
  headings of the Code of Practice for the Care and Handling of Hatching
  Eggs, Breeders, Chickens and Turkeys, published by the National Farm
  Animal Care Council, as amended from time to time, that are applicable
  to the species of poultry they are raising:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} Personnel Knowledge and Skills,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} Housing and Environment,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} Feed and Water,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} Flock Health Management, and

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} Euthanasia; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} takes measures to mitigate the risk of introduction
  and spread of the biological hazards set out in Part II of the Testing
  Standards, and to control and eliminate those hazards, in relation to
  the following elements:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} management of access to the premises,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} employee training in biosecurity measures,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} maintenance, cleaning and disinfection of
    equipment, facilities and related materials,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} mortality management,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} feeds and vaccines,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} water management, and

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} pest control.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-218, s. 1](#1378233-1378691){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1378233-1378691.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Animal and Product Identification]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1378692 .Subheading}

- **[[72.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  hatchery operator shall ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} every box or other package they receive that
    contains eggs or chicks is clearly marked with information that
    identifies the supply flock; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} every box or other package that is used for the
    marketing of eggs or chicks is clearly marked with information that
    identifies the hatchery and the premises identification number,
    issued by the province, that identifies the location of the supply
    flock from which the eggs or chicks originate.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply if the box or other
  package is accompanied by an invoice or other document that specifies
  the information required by paragraph (1)(a) or (b).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-218, s. 1](#1378233-1378699){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1378233-1378699.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Records]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1378700 .Subheading}

**[[72.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72.9 .sectionLabel}** A hatchery operator
shall keep and maintain records that include the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the identity of the flock from which the hatchery is
  supplied with eggs or commercial poultry;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of eggs purchased or received and set;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number of eggs, including balut, sold;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the number of chicks hatched, purchased and sold;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the number of eggs and chicks culled and died;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the name of any vaccines and medications given to the
  eggs and chicks; and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the location to which chicks or eggs are sent after
  leaving the hatchery.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-218, s. 1](#1378233-1378720){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1378233-1378720.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART IX]{.HLabel1}[Eradication and Control of Diseases]{.HTitleText1} {#h-547424 .Part}

### [Screening Tests]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547425 .Subheading}

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/2019-99,
s. 5\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-73-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 73"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[73.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73.1 .sectionLabel}** Every owner or person
in charge of animals in an eradication area or control zone shall, when
requested to do so by a veterinary inspector or by an accredited
veterinarian with the approval of a veterinary inspector, permit tests
for communicable disease to be conducted on the animals.

::: HistoricalNote

### [Movement Permits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547479 .Subheading}

- **[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, without a permit issued by the Minister under section
  160, move, or cause to be moved, a member of the Cervidae family from
  one place in Canada to another place in Canada.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall receive, or possess, without a permit
  issued by the Minister under section 160, an animal that has been
  moved in contravention of subsection (1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person to whom a permit referred to in
  subsection (1) or (2) is issued, and every person to whose premises a
  member of the Cervidae family is moved under a permit, shall keep a
  copy of the permit.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-295, s. 10
- SOR/79-839, s. 22(F)
- SOR/97-85, s. 55
- SOR/2002-444, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 6](#1124072-1124597){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124072-1124597.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 76"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[77 to 78.25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77_to_78.25
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/93-159, s. 9\]]{.Repealed}

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547491 .Subheading}

**[[78.26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78.26 .sectionLabel}** Where, under this
Part, a permit, certificate or other document is required for the
removal or transportation of an animal, the person having the care or
custody of the animal shall, when requested to do so by an inspector or
peace officer appointed under the Act, produce the permit, certificate
or other document.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-205, s. 4
- SOR/78-597, s. 8
:::

**[[78.27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78.27 .sectionLabel}** Where, in the
opinion of the Minister, an animal is moved into an area or region in
violation of this Part, the Minister may order that the animal be taken
back without delay to the area or region from which it was moved or to
an area or region of equal or lesser health status.

.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/97-85, s. 59\]]{.Repealed}

- **[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  inspector may order the person having the possession, care or custody
  of a dog that is affected or suspected of being affected with rabies
  or to have been exposed to rabies to isolate or muzzle the dog.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where an inspector reports to the Minister that
  rabies exists or is suspected to exist in any area in Canada the
  Minister may, by order, designate the area and direct that all dogs or
  other animals within the area be detained, isolated or muzzled in such
  manner and during such period as the Minister may specify.

**[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** No person shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} hold a bird imported under a permit into Canada at an
  airport in a room in which a bird not imported under permit is held;
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} transport by air a bird imported under a permit into
  Canada from the place of entry on the same aircraft as a bird not
  imported under permit.

### [Segregation and Inspection]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547621 .Subheading}

**[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may order a
person who owns or has the possession, care or control of any poultry,
ruminant, or equine or porcine animal to segregate the animal and to
have the animal inspected in any manner and for any communicable disease
that the Minister specifies.

## [PART X]{.HLabel1}[General Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-547626 .Part}

### [Notices]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547627 .Subheading}

**[[91.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91.1 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may give
notice to any person, by any means of communication, of the appearance
of any disease among animals.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-839, s. 27
:::

- **[[91.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every laboratory that diagnoses or
  suspects the appearance in an animal or thing of a disease set out in
  Schedule VII shall notify the Minister immediately of the diagnosis or
  suspicion.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Along with that notification, the laboratory shall
  include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, address and telephone number of the
    person who owns or has the possession, care or control of the animal
    or thing;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the location of the animal or thing; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all other information that the laboratory has in
    relation to the animal or thing.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every laboratory that diagnoses or suspects the
  appearance in an animal or thing of a disease set out in Schedule VIII
  shall notify the Minister of the diagnosis or suspicion immediately
  after the end of the calendar year in which the appearance of the
  disease is diagnosed or suspected.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-155, s. 1
:::

### [Records]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547640 .Subheading}

**[[91.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91.3 .sectionLabel}** Every person required
to keep a record under these Regulations shall, unless it is otherwise
stated,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} keep the record for a period of two years from the
  date the requirement arose; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2012-286, s. 56\]]{.Repealed}

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} on receipt of a written request by an inspector,
  provide the inspector, in a form approved by the Minister, with the
  information contained in the record.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/82-590, s. 4
- SOR/2006-147, s. 18
- SOR/2012-286, s. 56
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-91.3-20070712.html "Link to previous version of section 91.3"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Quarantine]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547647 .Subheading}

- **[[91.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91.4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an inspector orders a quarantine of a disease agent, animal or thing,
  a notice of quarantine shall be communicated by personal delivery to
  the person who owns or has possession, care or control of the disease
  agent, animal or thing and the notice may specify the manner,
  condition, place or places and period of quarantine necessary to
  prevent the spread of the communicable disease.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In respect of a disease agent, animal or thing
  quarantined pursuant to these Regulations, no person shall do or
  permit to be done any of the following actions, without the
  authorization of an inspector:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} remove the disease agent, animal or thing from the
    place of quarantine;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} allow the disease agent, animal or thing to come
    into contact with an animal that is not quarantined under the same
    quarantine order;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} destroy the disease agent, animal or thing; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} treat or test the disease agent, animal or thing
    for a communicable disease.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person who owns or has the possession, care or
  control of an animal quarantined under these Regulations shall without
  delay notify a veterinary inspector of any quarantined animal that
  appears sick.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} In respect of a disease agent or thing quarantined
  pursuant to these Regulations, no person shall do or permit to be done
  any of the following actions, without the authorization of an
  inspector:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} move the disease agent or thing;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} alter the appearance of the disease agent or thing;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} remove of any tag, sign or other notice that the
    disease agent or thing is under quarantine; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} open any container or remove any wrapping or cover
    around the disease agent or thing.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person shall transport or cause to be transported
  a disease agent, animal or thing quarantined pursuant to these
  Regulations unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a licence for its transportation has been issued by
    an inspector;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the licence issued pursuant to
    paragraph (a) has been provided to the person in charge of the
    conveyance transporting the disease agent, animal or thing; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the disease agent, animal or thing is transported
    directly to the location stated in the licence.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Every person who receives a notice referred to in
  subsection (1) shall comply with the notice.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 62
- SOR/2012-286, s. 57(E)
- SOR/2015-55, s. 5(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-91.4-20121214.html "Link to previous version of section 91.4"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[91.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91.5 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2000-183, s. 31\]]{.Repealed}

### [Animal Markets]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547669 .Subheading}

- **[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no public
  sale, auction or market of livestock shall be held in any place unless
  the person conducting the sale, auction or market provides

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} facilities for the inspection and testing of the
    animals offered for sale at such place, including a room for the
    conduct of laboratory tests by an inspector; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/97-85, s. 63\]]{.Repealed}

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) does not apply to a sale of a
  farmer's livestock conducted on his own farm.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 63
:::

**[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** Every person conducting a
public sale, auction or market of livestock shall keep the place where
such sale, auction or market is held in a sanitary condition.

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** Every person conducting a
public sale, auction or market of livestock, [Camelidae]{.otherLang
lang="la"}, [Cervidae]{.otherLang lang="la"}, chickens, turkeys or game
birds shall keep and make available for inspection by an inspector a
record showing, with respect to each animal sold or received at the
sale, auction or market, the complete, legal names and addresses of the
consignor of every animal received and the purchaser of every animal
sold at the sale, auction or market.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/98-409, s. 11
:::

**[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** An inspector may order
the person having the possession, care or custody of a bovine received
for sale at a public sale, auction or market of livestock to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} mark or tag the animal for identification; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} have the animal examined and tested for disease.

<!-- -->

- **[[95.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 3, the
  Minister may require an owner, operator or person in charge of a
  public sale, auction or market of livestock to have the express
  written permission of the Minister for the public sale, auction or
  market of a bovine and, where the Minister so requires, the owner,
  operator or person in charge shall ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the bovine is marked or tagged for identification
    and examined by an inspector or a person approved by the Minister
    pursuant to subsection (2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of brucellosis testing, a specimen
    is, or specimens are collected from the bovine

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} at the premises of the public sale, auction or
      market of livestock,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} by an inspector or by a person who has been
      approved by the Minister pursuant to subsection (2) and collects
      the specimen or specimens under the supervision or to the
      satisfaction of an inspector, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} at a time and in a manner determined by the
      Minister; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where brucellosis testing of a specimen or
    specimens collected pursuant to paragraph (b) has not been completed
    prior to sale, the prospective purchaser is informed of this at the
    time of sale by the person in charge of the public sale, auction or
    market of livestock, in a manner approved by the Minister.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, in writing, approve a person for
  the purpose of performing a service referred to in paragraph (1)(a)
  or (b) where he is satisfied that that person is able competently to
  perform that service, and the Minister may, at any time, withdraw such
  an approval.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person to whom subsection (1) applies shall allow
  the public sale, auction or market of a bovine where the express
  written permission of the Minister is refused.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every person to whom subsection (1) applies shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} identify the specimen or specimens collected
    pursuant to paragraph (1)(b) by recording on the container of the
    specimen the identity of the bovine from which it was collected;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} keep a record containing

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} particulars sufficient to identify the bovine
      from which the specimen or specimens were collected pursuant to
      paragraph (1)(b),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date of collection of the said specimen or
      specimens, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name and location of the premises on which
      the specimen or specimens were collected; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} deliver to the nearest inspector, with all
    practicable speed, the specimen or specimens collected pursuant to
    paragraph (1)(b) and a copy of the record kept pursuant to paragraph
    (b).

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a bovine that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is under 24 months of age; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is a steer or spayed heifer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-295, s. 16
- SOR/79-839, s. 28(F)
- SOR/82-590, s. 5
- SOR/87-457, s. 1
- SOR/93-159, s. 14
- SOR/97-85, s. 64
:::

**[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** No person shall offer an
animal for sale at a public sale, auction or market of livestock unless
the animal is identified by tag or brand or is one of a lot confined to
a pen.

**[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/93-159,
s. 15\]]{.Repealed}

### [Marking of Animals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547708 .Subheading}

**[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98 .sectionLabel}** Where a person
administers a test for a reportable disease to a bovine, equine, goat,
pig or sheep and the animal reacts positively to the test, the person
shall permanently mark the animal with a red tag bearing the word
"reactor" placed in its left ear or in such other manner as the Minister
may direct.

- **[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  inspector may mark or cause an animal inspected by him to be marked
  with a tag or in such other manner as the Minister may direct so as to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} identify the animal preparatory to a test for
    disease; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} indicate that the animal

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} reacted positively to a test, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is suspected of being affected with a
      communicable disease.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An accredited veterinarian may mark or cause an
  animal inspected by him to be marked with a tag or in such other
  manner as the Minister may direct so as to identify the animal.

**[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 99,
no person shall, unless authorized by an inspector, place or affix a tag
or other mark referred to in section 98, 99 or 201 on an animal.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-100-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 100"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101 .sectionLabel}** No person shall, unless
authorized by an inspector, remove or deface a tag or other mark placed
on an animal pursuant to section 98, 99 or 201.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-101-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 101"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Seals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547723 .Subheading}

**[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102 .sectionLabel}** An inspector may affix
seals or other devices to a conveyance, container or other thing, for
the purposes of the Act and these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 65
:::

**[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103 .sectionLabel}** No person shall,
without the authorization of an inspector, break, alter, tamper with, or
remove any seal or other device affixed to a conveyance, container or
other thing pursuant to section 102.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 65
:::

### [Disinfection]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547730 .Subheading}

- **[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When
  a veterinary inspector finds or suspects that an animal is affected
  with or died from a communicable disease he may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} order

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the owner or occupier of any outhouse, stable,
      yard or other place affected or suspected of being affected with a
      communicable disease, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the owner or operator of any aircraft, railway
      car, vehicle or vessel affected or suspected of being affected
      with a communicable disease,

    to clean and disinfect such outhouse, stable, yard or other place or
    such aircraft, railway car, vehicle or vessel; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} order any person entering or leaving any outhouse,
    stable, yard or other place affected or suspected of being affected
    with a communicable disease to clean and disinfect any footwear,
    clothing or other thing worn or carried by him.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an animal is required to be destroyed under
  section 27.6, 37 or 48 of the Act, every person in charge of a
  conveyance in which the animal is subsequently carried shall,
  immediately after the animal is unloaded from the conveyance, clean
  and disinfect the conveyance under the supervision of an inspector at
  the nearest place where facilities for that purpose are available.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/92-585, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 12](#1124090-1124666){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124090-1124666.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 18(F)](#1124109-1124692){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124109-1124692.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-104-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 104"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every owner or person in charge of a
  crate or other container in which poultry are to be transported shall
  thoroughly clean and disinfect the crate or container before poultry
  are loaded therein unless the crate or container is new or was
  thoroughly cleaned and disinfected following its last use for
  transporting poultry.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/98-409, s. 12\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every owner or person in charge of a container from
  which animal manure, aircraft garbage or ship's refuse, as those terms
  are defined in subsection 47.1(1), has been discharged under section
  47.1 shall clean and disinfect it before it is used again.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/98-409, s. 12
- SOR/2003-6, s. 80
- SOR/2009-18, s. 19(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-105-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 105"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every carrier shall clean and
  disinfect every railway car, aircraft or vessel that has been used for
  the transportation of poultry or livestock.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/97-85, s. 66\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An inspector may order a person in charge of a motor
  vehicle that has been used for the transportation of livestock to
  clean and disinfect, within a specified period of time, the motor
  vehicle at the nearest place where facilities for that purpose are
  available or at such other place as the inspector may specify.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/97-85, s. 66\]]{.Repealed}

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person shall bring from a country other that the
  United States a conveyance that has been used to transport poultry,
  ruminants, equines or porcines unless it has been cleaned and
  disinfected.

- [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall bring from the United States a
  conveyance that has been used to transport poultry or porcines unless
  it has been cleaned and disinfected.

- [(5.2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (5.1) does not apply to a conveyance

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that has transported Canadian porcines to a
    slaughtering establishment in the United States where inspection is
    provided by the Food Safety and Inspection Service of the United
    States Department of Agriculture and that has returned directly to
    Canada from that establishment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that has not transported porcines other than those
    mentioned in paragraph (a) while in the United States;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is not licensed to transport livestock between
    locations in the United States; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} from which as much manure as could be removed with
    a shovel and broom at an ambient temperature of 20°C has, in fact,
    been removed.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Every person in charge of a railway car, motor
  vehicle or container that has been used for the transportation of a
  commodity that is required to be cleaned and disinfected pursuant to
  section 107 shall immediately after it is unloaded clean and disinfect
  the railway car, vehicle or container.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} All yards, stables, sheds or other premises used by
  railway or steamship companies or other persons for the accommodation
  of animals shall be maintained in a sanitary condition and an
  inspector may order the person in charge of the yard, stable, shed or
  other premises to clean and disinfect the premises.

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} Every railway company and motor carrier shall affix
  to both sides of every railway car or motor vehicle used to transport
  a commodity that is required to be cleaned and disinfected pursuant to
  section 107 a placard stating in bold face letters not less than 1 1/2
  inches in height the words "Restricted Import Animal Product"
  "[Produit animal importé sous restriction]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" and
  "Clean and Disinfect this Car" "[Wagon à nettoyer et à
  désinfecter]{.otherLang lang="fr"}".

- [(9)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[stockyard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  has the same meaning as in the Livestock and Livestock Products Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-597, s. 13
- SOR/79-295, s. 17
- SOR/95-473, s. 11
- SOR/97-85, s. 66
- SOR/98-584, s. 4
- SOR/2015-55, s. 6(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-106-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 106"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107 .sectionLabel}** Where a commodity that
is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} raw wool, hair or bristles,

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} untanned hides and skins,

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} animal glands and organs, or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} gluestock,

has been imported into Canada from any country other than the United
States, it shall be cleaned, disinfected or treated at the establishment
approved by the Minister for that purpose and to which it was
transported.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 67
:::

**[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** Every place or thing
required to be cleaned and disinfected pursuant to sections 104 to 106
shall be cleaned and disinfected

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} where applicable, by removing all animal matter,
  manure, litter and refuse therefrom; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} by applying a disinfectant under the supervision or
  to the satisfaction of an inspector.

<!-- -->

- **[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  veterinary inspector may order the person conducting a public sale,
  auction or market of livestock to clean and disinfect the place where
  such sale, auction or market is conducted, under the supervision or to
  the satisfaction of an inspector within a time specified by the
  veterinary inspector.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the place where a public sale, auction or market
  of livestock is held is not thoroughly cleaned and disinfected in
  accordance with the notice of a veterinary inspector, no sale, auction
  or market of livestock shall be held in such place without the consent
  of the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 18(F)](#1124109-1124694){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124109-1124694.html"
  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-109-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 109"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Samples of Milk and Cream from Dairies, etc.]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547779 .Subheading}

**[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110 .sectionLabel}** Every sample of milk or
cream supplied by the operator of a cheese factory, creamery or dairy on
the request of the Minister pursuant to section 6 of the Act shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be refrigerated to a temperature not below 4°C (40°F)
  and not above 7°C (45°F); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} contain potassium dichromate or other preservative of
  a strength and quantity that will preserve the sample but not affect
  the test results.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/92-585, s. 2
- SOR/95-475, s. 4(E)
:::

### [Meat, Meat By-products or Carcasses in Feeding Livestock or Poultry]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547785 .Subheading}

**[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2008-20, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

**[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112 .sectionLabel}** No person shall feed
meat, meat by-products or food that is suspected to contain meat or meat
by-products to swine or poultry, or permit swine or poultry to have
access to the meat or by-products.

::: HistoricalNote

**[[113]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113 .sectionLabel}** Section 112 does not
apply to any feed approved or registered under the [Feeds
Act](/eng/acts/F-9).

::: HistoricalNote

- SOR/97-85, s. 68

**[[113.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113.1 .sectionLabel}** Subject to Part
XIV, no person shall feed the carcass of an animal or part of one to
livestock or poultry unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the carcass or part of it has been rendered in a
  rendering plant in respect of which a permit has been issued in
  accordance with Part XIV; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2008-20, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is processed in a manner which would prevent the
  introduction into Canada of any reportable disease or any other
  serious epizootic disease.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-478, s. 15
- SOR/2008-20, s. 2

### [Disposal of Diseased Carcasses]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547799 .Subheading}

**[[114]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-114 .sectionLabel}** A veterinary inspector
may order a person who owns or has the possession, care or control of an
animal that has died of, or is suspected of having died of, a
communicable disease or that is destroyed under section 27.6, 37 or 48
of the Act to dispose of the carcass in any manner that the veterinary
inspector specifies.

::: HistoricalNote

### [Animal Semen Production Centre]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547803 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/97-85, s. 69

\]
:::

- **[[115]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-115 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall operate an animal semen production centre or collect or
  process semen from a ruminant or porcine except under and in
  accordance with a permit issued by the Minister under section 160.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a person who
  collects animal semen that is used only for evaluating the quality of
  the semen and that is not used in or exposed to any live animal.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-478, s. 16
:::

**[[116]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-116 .sectionLabel}** Every person who
operates an animal semen production centre shall immediately segregate
any animal that has been exposed to a communicable disease from all
other animals in the centre that have not been exposed to the disease,
or remove the exposed animal from the centre.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 35
- SOR/78-205, s. 5
- SOR/79-295, s. 18
- SOR/97-85, s. 70
- SOR/97-478, s. 16
:::

- **[[117]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-117 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall collect in an animal semen production centre or store in
  or distribute from such a centre or from an animal semen distribution
  centre, any animal semen that is affected with or has been exposed to
  a communicable disease that is capable of being transmitted in semen.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where animal semen that is affected with or has been
  exposed to a communicable disease that is capable of being transmitted
  in semen has been collected in an animal semen production centre or
  has been stored in such a centre or in an animal semen distribution
  centre, the person operating the centre shall immediately destroy the
  semen under the supervision of an inspector.

**[[118]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-118 .sectionLabel}** Where an inspector
finds that animal semen stored in an animal semen production centre or
animal semen distribution centre or other place is contaminated by
bacteria, a virus or any other micro-organism capable of introducing
disease in animals, he may cause all or any semen of the animal from
which the contaminated semen was collected to be destroyed or order the
person having the possession, care or custody of the animal semen to
destroy any or all of it.

- **[[119]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-119
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who operates an animal
  semen production centre shall keep and make available for inspection
  by an inspector a record showing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date each animal entered the centre;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the owner of each animal or
    of the person from whom the animal was acquired;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the premises from which each animal entered the
    centre;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all tests performed on each animal, the date and
    results of each test and the person who performed the tests;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} each date semen was collected from each animal, the
    quantity collected and the disposition of the semen;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the identity of the semen that is stored in the
    centre; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the date each animal was removed from the centre,
    the reason for the removal and the disposition of each animal.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who collects, stores, distributes or
  sells animal semen shall keep and make available for inspection by an
  inspector

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a record of the dates and quantities of semen
    imported, exported, sold, distributed, used or otherwise disposed
    of; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a record of all semen stored by him identified as
    to the animals from which it was collected.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every record required to be kept pursuant to this
  section shall be in a form approved by the Minister and shall be
  maintained for a minimum period of seven years.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-295, s. 19
- SOR/97-85, s. 71
- SOR/97-478, s. 17
:::

## [PART XI]{.HLabel1}[Veterinary Biologics]{.HTitleText1} {#h-547833 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/95-54, s. 4(F)
- SOR/2018-79, s. 4(F)

\]
