**[[120]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[manufacture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[manufacture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include the sole
    process of labelling; ([fabriquer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[veterinary biologic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[veterinary biologic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include an
    antibiotic except when it is used as a preservative or when it is an
    integral part of the veterinary biologic. ([produit biologique
    vétérinaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-295, s. 20
- SOR/86-291, s. 1
- SOR/95-54, s. 6(F)
- SOR/2002-438, s. 8
- [SOR/2018-79, s. 4](#1044379-1244991){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044379-1244991.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-120-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 120"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Permits to Release Veterinary Biologics]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547841 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2002-438, s. 9(F)
- SOR/2018-79, s. 4(F)

\]
:::

**[[120.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120.1 .sectionLabel}** In sections 120.2
to 120.6,

[[confinement procedure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[confinement procedure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any physical,
    chemical, operational or biological control, or any combination
    thereof, intended to restrict the exit or dispersal from confinement
    of a veterinary biologic that contains an organism or a part or
    product of an organism; ([méthode de confinement]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[containment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[containment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means containment in accordance
    with the Laboratory Biosafety Guidelines established by the
    Department of National Health and Welfare and the Medical Research
    Council of Canada and published in 1990, as amended from time to
    time; ([confinement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[environment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the components of the
    Earth and includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} air, land and water,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all layers of the atmosphere,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all organic and inorganic matter and living
      organisms, and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the interacting natural systems that include
      components referred to in paragraphs (a) to
      (c); ([environnement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[live genetically modified veterinary biologic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[live genetically modified veterinary
    biologic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a live veterinary biologic that
    contains or is made from an organism and is produced by recombinant
    DNA technology; ([produit biologique vétérinaire vivant et
    génétiquement modifié]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[release]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[release]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any discharge or emission of a
    veterinary biologic into the
    environment. ([dissémination]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-8, s. 2
- SOR/2002-438, ss. 9(F), 18(F)
- [SOR/2018-79, s. 4](#1044379-1244993){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044379-1244993.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-120.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 120.1"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[120.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this section,
  [[organism]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a part or product of an
  organism.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Sections 120.3 to 120.6 do not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a veterinary biologic

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which a product licence has been
      issued, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that contains only organisms that are

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the same as organisms contained in a veterinary
        biologic referred to in subparagraph (i), or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} of a species that is substantially equivalent
        to a species of organisms contained in a veterinary biologic
        referred to in subparagraph (i), in terms of pathogenicity, host
        specificity, potential impact on non-target organisms, on
        biodiversity and on biogeochemical cycles, mode of action,
        persistence and dispersal in the environment and gene flow; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the release of a veterinary biologic, that contains
    organisms, under containment or in accordance with confinement
    procedures in a manner that prevents the dissemination of any
    genetic material from the veterinary biologic into the environment.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-8, s. 2
- SOR/2002-438, s. 18(F)
- [SOR/2018-79, s. 4(F)](#1044379-1244994){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044379-1244994.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-120.2-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 120.2"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[120.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall release a veterinary biologic unless the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} submits an application for a permit for the
    proposed release to the Minister, accompanied by sufficient
    information, including the information referred to in section 120.4,
    to enable the Minister to determine whether the proposed release is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} unlikely to result in the introduction into
      Canada or the spread within Canada of any vector, disease or toxic
      substance, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} unlikely to pose a risk of harm to the
      environment or to human or animal health; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is issued a permit for the proposed release under
    section 160.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the accompanying information that is required
  to be provided to the Minister pursuant to paragraph (1)(a) in respect
  of the proposed release of a veterinary biologic is the same as
  information that has already been provided to the Minister before the
  coming into force of sections 120.1 and 120.2, this section and
  sections 120.4 to 120.6, or that is provided on or after the coming
  into force of sections 120.1 and 120.2, this section and sections
  120.4 to 120.6 in relation to an application for another permit, the
  information is not required to be provided again.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-8, s. 2
- SOR/2002-438, s. 18(F)
- [SOR/2018-79, s. 4(F)](#1044379-1244995){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044379-1244995.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-120.3-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 120.3"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Information Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547876 .Subheading}

- **[[120.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The accompanying information that is
  required to be provided by a person to the Minister pursuant to
  paragraph 120.3(1)(a) includes the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person or body responsible for the
    proposed release and the name of the person who will be in charge of
    carrying out the release;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a live genetically modified
    veterinary biologic

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a description of the donor organism and the
      methods of incorporation of the genes from the donor organism into
      the host, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a description of the live genetically modified
      veterinary biologic, including details relating to expression of
      the new gene and the stability of the incorporation of the new
      gene, and a comparison of the characteristics of the live
      genetically modified organism with those of the unmodified
      organism;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the protocol followed in preparing the veterinary
    biologic and any diluent to be used with it;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the procedures to be followed in handling, storing,
    administering, testing, releasing and disposing of the veterinary
    biologic and any diluent to be used with it;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the tests used to establish the purity, safety,
    potency and efficacy of the veterinary biologic and the purity and
    safety of any diluent used with it, and the results of all the
    tests;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the proposed starting date, time period and site of
    the release;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the proposed measures to mitigate any risk of harm
    to the environment or to human or animal health; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} any other information in respect of the veterinary
    biologic that is relevant to identifying any risk of harm to the
    environment or to human or animal health.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The information required by paragraph (1)(h) or any
  part of that information may be omitted if the Minister determines, on
  the basis of a written scientific rationale provided by the person
  referred to in subsection (1), that the information or part is not
  relevant or cannot practicably be obtained and is not required for the
  issuance of a permit under section 120.5, and notifies the person of
  that determination.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-8, s. 2
- SOR/2002-438, ss. 10(F), 18(F)
- [SOR/2018-79, s. 4(F)](#1044379-1244996){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044379-1244996.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-120.4-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 120.4"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Issuance of Permits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547892 .Subheading}

- **[[120.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may issue a permit for
  the release of a veterinary biologic under section 160 where the
  Minister, in addition to being satisfied that the proposed release
  meets the criteria set out in subsection 160(1.1), is satisfied that,
  to the best of the Minister's knowledge and belief, the proposed
  release is unlikely to pose a risk of harm to the environment or to
  human or animal health.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In evaluating the risk of harm to the environment and
  to human and animal health for the purposes of subsection (1), the
  Minister shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} consider such matters as

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the effects of the proposed release on the
      environment and on human and animal health, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the magnitude of the exposure to the environment
      of the veterinary biologic involved in the proposed release; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} assess whether the veterinary biologic will enter
    or may enter the environment in a quantity or concentration or under
    conditions

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} having or that may have an immediate or long-term
      harmful effect on the environment or on human or animal health,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} constituting or that may constitute a danger to
      the environment on which human or animal life depends, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} constituting or that may constitute a danger in
      Canada to human or animal life or health.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the conditions referred to in
  paragraph 160(2)(b), a permit for the release of a veterinary biologic
  shall contain such conditions as the Minister considers advisable to
  prevent harm to the environment or to human or animal health.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any of the reasons set out in any of
  paragraphs 160(3)(a) to (c), the Minister may cancel or suspend a
  permit for the release of a veterinary biologic if the Minister has
  reason to believe that failure to do so could result in harm to the
  environment or to human or animal health.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-8, s. 2
- SOR/2002-438, s. 18(F)
- [SOR/2018-79, s. 4(F)](#1044379-1244997){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044379-1244997.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-120.5-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 120.5"){.PITLink}
:::

### [New Information Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547907 .Subheading}

- **[[120.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where, at any time after the
  applicant submits an application for a permit referred to in paragraph
  120.3(a) or is issued a permit for the release of a veterinary
  biologic under section 160, the applicant becomes aware of any new
  information regarding the risk of harm to the environment or to human
  or animal health, or the risk of introducing into Canada or spreading
  within Canada any vector, disease or toxic substance, that could be
  caused as a result of the release, the applicant shall immediately
  provide the new information to the Minister.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the Minister, on the basis of the new
  information provided by an applicant pursuant to subsection (1),
  re-evaluates the risk of harm to the environment and to human and
  animal health posed by the release and determines that there is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a risk that is less than was apparent at the time
    that information was first provided or at the time any new
    information was subsequently provided, the Minister may

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} where the release has already been authorized,
      maintain the existing conditions respecting the release,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} change the conditions respecting the release, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} remove any conditions respecting the release;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a risk that is greater than was apparent at the
    time that information was first provided or at the time any new
    information was subsequently provided, the Minister may amend the
    permit for the release by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} imposing additional conditions respecting the
      release, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} changing the conditions respecting the release;
      or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an unacceptable risk, the Minister shall

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} refuse to issue a permit for the release, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where a permit for the release has already been
      issued, cancel the permit and require the applicant to stop the
      release and to take any appropriate action necessary to eliminate
      or minimize the risk.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In re-evaluating the risk of harm to the environment
  and to human and animal health on the basis of new information for the
  purposes of subsection (2), the Minister shall consider the matters
  referred to in paragraph 120.5(2)(a) and make the assessment referred
  to in paragraph 120.5(2)(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-8, s. 2
- SOR/2002-438, s. 18(F)
- SOR/2012-286, s. 58(F)
- [SOR/2018-79, s. 4(F)](#1044379-1244998){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044379-1244998.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Permits to Import]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547924 .Subheading}

- **[[121]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-121 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall import a veterinary biologic into Canada unless the
  person does so under and in accordance with a permit issued by the
  Minister.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Where a permit referred to in subsection (1) has
  been issued, the veterinary biologic shall be shipped directly to
  Canada from the manufacturer's premises or from such other premises,
  in which veterinary biologics may be lawfully manufactured under the
  applicable foreign laws, that were designated in the application for
  the permit.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/95-475, s. 4\]]{.Repealed}

- **[[122]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), every
  applicant for a permit to import a veterinary biologic into Canada
  shall include with his application

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} when requested by the Minister, such samples of the
    veterinary biologic and any diluent to be used therewith as will
    enable the Minister to analyse the veterinary biologic;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a product outline;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the results of such tests of the veterinary
    biologic as will enable the Minister to analyse the veterinary
    biologic;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a sample of each package and container used or to
    be used for packing the veterinary biologic and any diluent to be
    used therewith;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the instructions or other written
    material to be included with each package or container of a
    veterinary biologic;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a copy of every label, tag or other marking to be
    attached to, marked on or contained in every package or container in
    which a veterinary biologic is to be packed;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the names of all persons to be employed in the
    manufacture or testing of the veterinary biologic and information
    with respect to the education, qualifications and experience of such
    persons; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} any information the Minister may require in order
    to determine, in the course of assessing the safety of the
    veterinary biologic, whether the introduction of the veterinary
    biologic into the environment could result in the spread within
    Canada of a vector, disease or toxic substance that would have a
    harmful effect on human or animal health.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the Minister has issued a permit to a person to
  import a veterinary biologic into Canada, he may issue a permit

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to that person to import the veterinary biologic
    into Canada in any subsequent year, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to any other person to import the veterinary
    biologic into Canada,

  and that person is exempt from any of the requirements of
  subsection (1) as the Minister may specify.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall furnish the applicant referred to
  in subsection (1) with a report showing the result of any test
  performed on the sample supplied pursuant to paragraph (1)(a).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every person to whom a permit is issued to import a
  veterinary biologic shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} keep and make available for inspection by a
    veterinary inspector a record in a form approved by the Minister
    with respect to the importation, sale and distribution of the
    veterinary biologic and retain that record for at least two years
    following the expiration date of the veterinary biologic to which
    the record relates; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} furnish the Minister with such samples of the
    veterinary biologic as the Minister may require from time to time.

### [Establishment Licence and Product Licence]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547953 .Subheading}

**[[123]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
prepare, manufacture, preserve, pack, label or test a veterinary
biologic for use in Canada or for export unless the person does so under
and in accordance with an establishment licence issued by the Minister.

the person does so under and in accordance with a product licence issued
by the Minister.

**[[125]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-125 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, SOR/97-85,
s. 72\]]{.Repealed}

**[[126]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-126 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 127,
every applicant for a product licence shall include with his application
the material and information referred to in subsection 122(1).

**[[127]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-127 .sectionLabel}** Where the Minister has
issued a product licence to a person, he may issue a product licence to
such person in any subsequent year and that person is exempt from any of
the requirements of subsection 122(1) as the Minister may specify.

### [Requirements of Operation in a Licensed Establishment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-547963 .Subheading}

- **[[128]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-128 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall prepare, manufacture, preserve, pack, label, store or
  test a veterinary biologic in a licensed establishment unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the establishment and the equipment therein is
    sound in construction, clean, in good repair and equipped with means
    to maintain it in a sanitary condition;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the area immediately adjacent to the establishment
    is clean and protected against the incursion of animals;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} animal wastes, effluents from processing or
    testing, contaminated matter and dead animals are decontaminated
    before being removed or discharged from the establishment;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the floors and walls of every room and area in
    which a veterinary biologic is prepared, manufactured, preserved or
    tested are of a hard finish suitable for cleaning;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the establishment has drainage, plumbing and
    sewerage that are

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} adequate to handle all wastes, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} equipped with suitable traps and vents;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the establishment has dressing rooms, lavatories
    and showers that are

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} adequate in size and equipment for the number of
      persons using them, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} well lighted and ventilated to the outside;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} eating rooms, if provided, are separate from, and
    do not lead directly into, any room used for preparing,
    manufacturing, preserving, storing, testing, packing or labelling a
    veterinary biologic;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} rooms or areas are provided, where required, that
    are capable of being maintained at a uniform and constant
    temperature within any desired range and are equipped with
    thermometers that provide a continuous permanent record of
    temperature;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} means are provided for warning of any failure of
    power or equipment or any change in the required temperature;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} rooms, areas and equipment are provided, where
    required, that are capable of being maintained free of air-borne
    contaminants to any desired degree and preventing the escape of
    micro-organisms therefrom;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} animals being used in a testing program or for any
    special purpose are segregated from all animals not being used in
    such program or for such purpose;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} a separate room is provided for animals being
    examined or tested

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} [ante mortem]{.otherLang lang="la"}, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} [post mortem]{.otherLang lang="la"}; and

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} every person employed therein is qualified to
    perform the task assigned to him.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every holder of a licence issued under these
  Regulations shall without delay inform the Minister of any change or
  addition to the material or information furnished to the Minister for
  the purpose of obtaining the licence.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person employed in a licensed establishment
  shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/97-85, s. 73\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be immunized against diseases likely to be
    encountered therein; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} wear clothing and use equipment adequate to protect
    him against all hazards likely to be encountered therein.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise permitted by a veterinary inspector,
  no veterinary biologic shall be tested in a licensed establishment
  except in a separate room or building that is used exclusively for
  testing veterinary biologics.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} No diagnostic examination or procedure involving the
  use of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a dead or diseased animal,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} diseased animal matter, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an unidentified culture of micro-organisms, fungi
    or moulds

  shall be conducted in a licensed establishment except in a room or
  area separate from and not leading into any room or area used for
  preparing, manufacturing, preserving, storing or testing a veterinary
  biologic.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise permitted by a veterinary inspector,
  no research or experimentation shall be conducted in a licensed
  establishment except in a room or area separate from and not leading
  into any room or area used for preparing, manufacturing, preserving,
  storing or testing a veterinary biologic.

biologic imported, sold, advertised or offered for sale in Canada shall
be stored at a temperature between 2°C and 7°C unless otherwise stated
in the product outline or the labelling.

**[[131]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-131 .sectionLabel}** Where the Minister is
satisfied from tests of a veterinary biologic, or otherwise, that a
veterinary biologic is unsafe to use, is likely to cause communicable
disease in animals or is contaminated or ineffective, he may, by order,
prohibit the importation, manufacture, sale or distribution of the
veterinary biologic.

- **[[131.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-131.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where an emergency exists with
  respect to the availability of and need for a veterinary biologic, the
  Minister may exempt that veterinary biologic from the application of
  any of the provisions of these Regulations during the period of the
  emergency.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An exemption referred to in subsection (1) shall be
  in writing and shall state the veterinary biologic that is exempted,
  the provision or provisions of these Regulations from which it is
  exempted and the reasons for that exemption.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may at any time cancel the exemption
  referred to in subsection (1).

- **[[132]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall sell, advertise or offer for sale a veterinary biologic
  unless it is packaged and labelled in accordance with these

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall be clearly and prominently displayed on the
    label; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall be readily visible by a purchaser under the

biologic sold, advertised or offered for sale in Canada shall carry a

- **[[134]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-134
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), every
  label of a veterinary biologic sold, advertised or offered for sale in
  Canada shall show

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the assigned name of the veterinary biologic,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the manufacturer of the veterinary
    biologic or, if there is more than one manufacturer of that
    veterinary biologic, the name of the first or the name of the final
    manufacturer,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the place where the manufacturer referred to in
    paragraph (b) manufactures the veterinary biologic,

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the lot or serial number or other means of
    identifying the veterinary biologic,

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the same establishment licence number, whether
    Canadian or foreign, on all components of the label except that the
    Minister may, in writing, exempt from that requirement diluents
    manufactured in Canada,

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} directions for use of the veterinary biologic or
    that directions for its use are contained inside the package,

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the expiration date of the veterinary biologic,

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the components of the veterinary biologic,
    including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} viruses, bacteria, toxoids and antibodies, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} antibiotics, if added during the production
      process as preservatives,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/80-428, s. 11\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the net quantity of the veterinary biologic in the
    container, expressed in metric units or in doses,

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the temperature range, expressed in metric units,
    necessary to maintain prescribed potency of the veterinary biologic,

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a veterinary biologic manufactured
    for use in food producing animals, the cautionary statement
    indicating the appropriate withdrawal period as stated in the
    product outline on the basis of which the import permit or product
    licence was issued, and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} the words "For veterinary use only" and "[Pour
    usage vétérinaire seulement]{.otherLang lang="fr"}" or other wording
    to the same effect, and

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} any cautionary information necessary to ensure the
    safe handling and use of the veterinary biologic.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the label of a veterinary biologic is too small
  to show all the information required by subsection (1), any such
  information as the Minister may permit may be shown on the directions
  for use inside the package.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), the information required
  by subsection (1), as well as any supplemental information appearing
  on the label, shall be shown in both official languages, and may
  additionally be shown in whole or in part in any other language.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person may sell, advertise or offer for sale in
  Canada a veterinary biologic that is labelled in only one official
  language if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister determines that the veterinary
    biologic is needed for research or emergency use; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person undertakes to ensure its safe storage,
    handling and administration.

  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as provided in subsection
  (2), no person shall sell or offer for sale a rabies vaccine to anyone

- **[[135]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-135 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, in any advertisement for the sale of a veterinary
  biologic, make any claim with respect to the purity, safety, potency
  and efficacy of the veterinary biologic that is not supported by the
  product outline for such veterinary biologic.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, in any advertisement for the sale of
  a veterinary biologic, make any representation that is false,
  misleading or deceptive or that is likely to create an erroneous
  impression regarding the character, value, quality, composition, merit
  or safety of the advertised veterinary biologic.

**[[135.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-135.1 .sectionLabel}** Every holder of a
licence or permit issued under this Part shall report to the Minister,
in writing, any information concerning, or any evidence of, a
significant deficiency in safety, potency or efficacy of a veterinary
biologic within 15 days after the date on which that information or
evidence is known to the holder.

## [PART XII]{.HLabel1}[Transport of Animals]{.HTitleText1} {#h-548075 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548076 .Subheading}

- **[[136]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-136 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[assembly centre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[assembly centre]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place to which
      animals are transported for the purpose of assembly and includes
      an auction market, an assembly yard and a holding facility, other
      than a slaughter establishment, that holds, on behalf of a
      slaughter establishment, animals that are destined soon afterwards
      for slaughter at that slaughter establishment. ([centre de
      rassemblement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[commercial carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[commercial carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the owner of a motor vehicle who is engaged in
        the business of transporting animals by land for financial
        benefit;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the owner of an aircraft who is engaged in the
        business of transporting animals by air for financial benefit;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the owner of a vessel who is engaged in the
        business of transporting animals by water for financial benefit;
        or

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a railway company. ([transporteur
        commercial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[compromised]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[compromised]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an animal,
      means an animal that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is bloated but has no signs of discomfort or
        weakness;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has acute frostbite;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is blind in both eyes;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} has not fully healed after a procedure,
        including dehorning, detusking or castration;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} is lame other than in a way that is described
        in the definition [[unfit]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef};

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} has a deformity or a fully healed amputation
        and does not demonstrate signs of pain as a result of the
        deformity or amputation;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} is in a period of peak lactation;

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} has an unhealed or acutely injured penis;

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} has a minor rectal or minor vaginal prolapse;

      - [(j)]{.lawlabel} has its mobility limited by a device applied to
        its body including hobbles other than hobbles that are applied
        to aid in treatment;

      - [(k)]{.lawlabel} is a wet bird; or

      - [(l)]{.lawlabel} exhibits any other signs of infirmity, illness,
        injury or of a condition that indicates that it has a reduced
        capacity to withstand transport. ([fragilisé]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[confine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[confine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, for the purpose of
      transporting an animal, to hold an animal in a conveyance or
      container from the time that the animal is in the conveyance or
      container until the time that the animal is out of the conveyance
      or container. ([confinement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[container]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a structure that is
      moveable, that has rigid sides and a rigid bottom and may have a
      cover and that is used to confine an animal and includes a cargo
      container and a crate. ([caisse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[humanely kill]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[humanely kill]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means to kill as rapidly as
      possible with the least possible pain, suffering, fear and anxiety
      and includes to slaughter in accordance with applicable
      legislation. ([tuer sans cruauté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[nutritional deficit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[nutritional deficit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an inadequate
      amount of feed or deprivation of feed that causes behavioural or
      physiological effects. ([déficit nutritionnel]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[safe water]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[safe water]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means potable water or water
      that does not pose a risk to the health of the animal drinking
      it. ([eau salubre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[unfit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unfit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an animal, means an
      animal that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is non-ambulatory;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has a fracture that impedes its mobility or
        causes it to exhibit signs of pain or suffering;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is lame in one or more limbs to the extent that
        it exhibits signs of pain or suffering and halted movements or a
        reluctance to walk;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is lame to the extent that it cannot walk on
        all of its legs;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} is in shock or is dying;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} has a prolapsed uterus or a severe rectal or
        severe vaginal prolapse;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} exhibits signs of a generalized nervous system
        disorder;

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} is a porcine that is trembling, has difficulty
        breathing and has discoloured skin;

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} has laboured breathing;

      - [(j)]{.lawlabel} has a severe open wound or a severe laceration;

      - [(k)]{.lawlabel} has sustained an injury and is hobbled to aid
        in treatment;

      - [(l)]{.lawlabel} is extremely thin;

      - [(m)]{.lawlabel} exhibits signs of dehydration;

      - [(n)]{.lawlabel} exhibits signs of hypothermia or hyperthermia;

      - [(o)]{.lawlabel} exhibits signs of a fever;

      - [(p)]{.lawlabel} has a hernia that

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} impedes its movement, including when a hind
          limb of the animal touches the hernia as the animal is
          walking,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} causes the animal to exhibit signs of pain
          or suffering,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} touches the ground when the animal is
          standing in its natural position, or

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} has an open wound, ulceration or obvious
          infection;

      - [(q)]{.lawlabel} is in the last 10% of its gestation period or
        has given birth during the preceding 48 hours;

      - [(r)]{.lawlabel} has an unhealed or infected navel;

      - [(s)]{.lawlabel} has a gangrenous udder;

      - [(t)]{.lawlabel} has severe squamous cell carcinoma of the eye;

      - [(u)]{.lawlabel} is bloated to the extent that it exhibits signs
        of discomfort or weakness;

      - [(v)]{.lawlabel} exhibits signs of exhaustion; or

      - [(w)]{.lawlabel} exhibits any other signs of infirmity, illness,
        injury or of a condition that indicates that it cannot be
        transported without suffering. ([inapte]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the application of this Part,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} loading of an animal begins when the animal is
    handled, moved or caught for the purpose of confining it in a
    conveyance or a container and ends when the animal is confined in
    the conveyance, or if it is confined in a container, when the
    container is placed on the conveyance;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an animal that is confined in a
    conveyance, unloading of the animal begins when the animal is
    handled or moved for the purpose of removing it from the conveyance
    and ends when the animal is removed from the conveyance or from any
    ramp, gangway, chute, step or apparatus used for unloading the
    animal; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an animal that is confined in a
    container, unloading of the animal begins when the container is
    handled or moved for the purpose of removing it from the conveyance
    and ends when the container is removed from the conveyance and the
    animal is removed from the container.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An animal that is both
  [[compromised]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} and
  [[unfit]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection (1), is
  deemed not to be compromised.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1226884){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1226884.html"
  role="button"}
:::
