:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548078 .Subheading}

**[[137]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-137 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies to
the transport of animals entering or leaving Canada or within Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1226888){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1226888.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-137-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 137"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Knowledge and Skills]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548080 .Subheading}

**[[138]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138 .sectionLabel}** Every person who loads,
confines or transports an animal in or unloads an animal from a
conveyance or container shall have the necessary knowledge and skills to
conduct those activities in compliance with this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 76
- SOR/2005-181, s. 2
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1226891){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1226891.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-138-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 138"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Training]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1226892 .Subheading}

- **[[138.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every commercial carrier shall
  provide training to, or ensure that training is received by, its
  employees and agents or mandataries who load, confine or transport an
  animal in or unload an animal from a conveyance or container or who
  take part in decision making, or advising the person operating the
  conveyance, in respect of the loading, confining, transporting or
  unloading of an animal so that they have the necessary knowledge and
  skills to conduct those activities in compliance with this Part.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The training shall cover subjects that include the
  following in respect of the species of animals that are to be loaded,
  confined, transported and unloaded:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} animal behaviour;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an assessment of an animal's capacity to withstand
    loading, confinement, transport and unloading;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} animal handling, restraint and space requirements
    and methods for the loading, confinement, transport and unloading of
    animals;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a contingency plan;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} effective monitoring of animals during loading,
    confinement, transport and unloading; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the risk factors set out in subsection 138.3(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1226903){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1226903.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Contingency Plans]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1226904 .Subheading}

- **[[138.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every commercial carrier and any
  other person who transports animals in the course of business or for
  financial benefit shall have a contingency plan that establishes
  measures that are to be taken in order to comply with the requirements
  of this Part if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are any unforeseen delays or circumstances
    that could result in the animal's unnecessary suffering, injury or
    death; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the animal becomes compromised or unfit during
    loading, confinement, transport or unloading.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any person who is required to have a contingency plan
  under subsection (1) shall inform their employees and agents or
  mandataries who load, confine, transport or unload animals or who take
  part in decision making, or advising the person operating the
  conveyance, in respect of the loading, confining, transporting or
  unloading of animals of the contingency plan.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1226911){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1226911.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Assessment and Monitoring of Risk Factors Related to Transport]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1226912 .Subheading}

- **[[138.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who loads, confines or
  transports an animal in or unloads an animal from a conveyance or
  container, or causes one to be so loaded, confined, transported or
  unloaded, shall, before loading, confining, transporting or unloading
  the animal, assess the animal's capacity to withstand the loading,
  confinement, transporting and unloading by taking into account any
  risk factors that could reasonably be viewed as having an impact on
  the animal's capacity to withstand the loading, confining,
  transporting and unloading, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the current condition of the animal;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any pre-existing infirmity, illness, injury or
    condition of the animal;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the space requirements for the animal;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the compatibility of the animal with any other
    animal;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} animal handling and restraint methods;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the expected time that the animal will be without
    feed, safe water and rest;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the expected duration of the transport and
    confinement of the animal in the conveyance or container;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the foreseeable delays during transport and at the
    destination;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the foreseeable weather conditions during
    transport;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the foreseeable conditions that may be encountered
    during transport that could result in sharp inclines and declines,
    vibration and shifting of the container or swaying of the
    conveyance; and

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the type and condition of the conveyance, container
    and equipment.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who confines or transports an animal in
  a conveyance or container shall monitor the animal in a manner and
  frequency that is appropriate to assess the animal's capacity to
  withstand the confinement and transport, taking into account the risk
  factors set out in subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1226928){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1226928.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Unfit Animals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548093 .Subheading}

- **[[139]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-139
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (5),
  no person shall load, confine or transport an animal that is unfit, or
  cause one to be loaded, confined or transported, in a conveyance or
  container.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An unfit animal may be loaded and confined in a
  conveyance or container and be directly transported to and unloaded at
  a place, other than a slaughter establishment or assembly centre,
  where it can receive veterinary care if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is individually loaded and unloaded without
    having to negotiate any ramps inside the conveyance;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is isolated during confinement and transport;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} measures are taken to prevent the animal's
    unnecessary suffering, injury or death during loading, confinement,
    transport and unloading; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a veterinarian recommends that the animal be
    transported to receive veterinary care.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An unfit animal may be loaded and confined in a
  conveyance or container and be directly transported to and unloaded at
  an assembly centre where it can receive veterinary care if the unfit
  animal is seized and detained under a provincial or federal Act and
  the requirements set out in paragraphs (2)(a) to (d) are met.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every person who confines or transports an animal
  that becomes unfit during confinement or transport in a container or
  conveyance, other than a vessel, shall take reasonable measures as
  soon as possible to prevent the animal's unnecessary suffering, injury
  or death and, as soon as possible,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} transport the animal directly to the nearest place
    where it can receive care or be humanely killed; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the animal is not transported to the nearest
    place, humanely kill the animal in the conveyance.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} If an animal becomes unfit during confinement or
  transport while on board a vessel, the vessel master or a veterinarian
  shall, as soon as possible,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable measures to prevent the animal's
    unnecessary suffering, injury or death or cause such reasonable
    measures to be taken by a person on board the vessel who is trained
    to provide care to the animals and provide care to the animal or
    cause care to be provided to the animal by a person on board the
    vessel who is trained to provide care to the animals; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if reasonable measures are not taken and care is
    not provided, cause the animal to be humanely killed by a person on
    board the vessel who is trained to use humane killing devices.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 77
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1226944){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1226944.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-139-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 139"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[139.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-139.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), no person
  shall unload an unfit animal, or cause an unfit animal to be unloaded,
  from a conveyance or container for the purpose of humanely killing the
  animal unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an unfit animal that is
    non-ambulatory,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the animal is rendered unconscious before it is
      unloaded, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the animal is humanely killed before it is
      unloaded; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an unfit animal that is ambulatory,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the animal is unloaded individually in a manner
      that is not likely to cause unnecessary suffering, injury or
      death,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the animal is rendered unconscious before it is
      unloaded, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the animal is humanely killed before it is
      unloaded.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of an unfit animal that is non-ambulatory
  and in a container, the animal may be manually removed from the
  container before it is rendered unconscious or humanely killed if to
  do so would not likely cause the animal to unnecessarily suffer,
  sustain an injury or die.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1226956){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1226956.html"
  role="button"}
:::

- **[[139.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-139.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A veterinary inspector or an
  inspector acting under the advice of a veterinary inspector may, if
  they have reasonable grounds to believe that an unfit animal is being
  or has been loaded, confined, transported or unloaded in contravention
  of section 139 or 139.1, make an order directing the person who is in
  contravention of either of those sections to take measures in order to
  prevent unnecessary suffering, injury or death of the animal.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The order may include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a requirement that the animal be humanely killed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a requirement that the animal be transported as
    soon as possible directly to a place where the animal can receive
    care or be humanely killed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the place to which the animal is to be transported;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any condition to be met when loading, confining,
    transporting or unloading the animal; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which and the time when the animal is
    to be humanely killed.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person who receives an order made under this
  section shall comply with the order.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1226967){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1226967.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Compromised Animals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548106 .Subheading}

- **[[140]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (6),
  no person shall load, confine or transport a compromised animal in or
  unload a compromised animal from a conveyance or container, or cause
  one to be so loaded, confined, transported or unloaded, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is isolated;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is individually loaded and unloaded without
    having to negotiate any ramps inside the conveyance;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} measures are taken that are necessary to prevent
    the animal's suffering, injury or death during loading, confinement,
    transport and unloading; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it is transported directly to the nearest place,
    other than an assembly centre, where it can receive care or be
    humanely killed.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(a) and (b) do not apply to rabbits and
  poultry, other than ratites, that are confined in containers.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) does not apply to a compromised
  animal that is confined and transported with one other animal with
  which it is familiar if to do so is unlikely to cause either animal
  suffering, injury or death and if they are segregated from other
  animals.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(d), a compromised animal may be
  transported directly to an assembly centre where it can receive care
  or be humanely killed if the animal has been seized and detained under
  a provincial or federal Act.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Every person who confines or transports an animal
  that becomes compromised during confinement or transport in a
  container or conveyance, other than a vessel, shall take reasonable
  measures as soon as possible to prevent the animal's unnecessary
  suffering, injury or death and, as soon as possible,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} transport the animal directly to the nearest place
    where it can receive care or be humanely killed; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the animal is not transported to the nearest
    place, humanely kill the animal in the conveyance.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} If an animal becomes compromised during confinement
  or transport while on board a vessel, the vessel master or a
  veterinarian shall, as soon as possible,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable measures to prevent the animal's
    unnecessary suffering, injury or death or cause such reasonable
    measures to be taken by a person on board the vessel who is trained
    to provide care to the animals and provide care to the animal or
    cause care to be provided to the animal by a person on board the
    vessel who is trained to provide care to the animals; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if reasonable measures are not taken and care is
    not provided, cause the animal to be humanely killed by a person on
    board the vessel who is trained to use humane killing devices.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/82-590, s. 11
- SOR/97-85, s. 78
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1226984){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1226984.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-140-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 140"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[140.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A veterinary inspector or an
  inspector acting under the advice of a veterinary inspector may, if
  they have reasonable grounds to believe that a compromised animal is
  being or has been loaded, confined, transported or unloaded in
  contravention of section 140, make an order directing the person who
  is in contravention of that section to take measures in order to
  prevent unnecessary suffering, injury or death of the animal.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The order may include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a requirement that the animal be humanely killed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a requirement that the animal be transported as
    soon as possible directly to a place where the animal can receive
    care or be humanely killed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the place to which the animal is to be transported;
    and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any condition to be met when loading, confining,
    transporting or unloading the animal.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person who receives an order made under this
  section shall comply with the order.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1226994){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1226994.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Livestock, Camelids or Cervids of Eight Days of Age or Less]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548112 .Subheading}

- **[[141]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-141 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall load, confine or transport livestock, camelids or cervids
  of eight days of age or less in or unload such an animal from a
  conveyance or container or cause one to be so loaded, confined,
  transported or unloaded, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the animal is loaded and unloaded individually
    without having to negotiate any ramps within the conveyance;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sufficient space is provided to allow the animal to
    lie down without lying on top of another animal;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} measures are taken that are necessary to prevent
    the animal's suffering, injury or death during loading, confinement,
    transport and unloading;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the animal is segregated from animals that are not
    livestock, camelids or cervids of eight days of age or less;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the expected period of time between the beginning
    of the animal's loading and the end of the animal's unloading is not
    longer than 12 hours;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} stops during transport are made only to load other
    livestock, camelids or cervids of eight days of age or less; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the animal is transported to a final destination
    that is other than an assembly centre.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall reload an animal referred to in
  subsection (1) after it has been unloaded at its final destination.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(d) does not apply to livestock,
  camelids or cervids of eight days of age or less who are loaded,
  confined or transported in or unloaded from a conveyance or container
  with their dam if to do so is unlikely to cause either animal
  suffering, injury or death.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/80-428, s. 12
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227007){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227007.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-141-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 141"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Lactating Animals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1227008 .Subheading}

**[[142]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-142 .sectionLabel}** No person shall load,
confine or transport a lactating animal, or cause one to be so loaded,
confined or transported, in a conveyance or container without its
suckling offspring unless the animal is milked at intervals that are
sufficient to prevent mammary engorgement.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/95-475, s. 4
- SOR/97-85, s. 79
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227010){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227010.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-142-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 142"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Young Ruminants]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548131 .Subheading}

- **[[143]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-143 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall load, confine or transport ruminants that are too young
  to be fed exclusively on hay and grain in, or unload such animals
  from, a conveyance or container, or cause one to be so loaded,
  confined, transported or unloaded, unless the expected period of time
  between the beginning of the animal's loading and the end of the
  animal's unloading is not longer than 12 hours.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall reload an animal referred to in
  subsection (1) after it has been unloaded at its final destination.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 80
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227015){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227015.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-143-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 143"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Animal Handling]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548146 .Subheading}

- **[[144]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, during the loading, confinement, transporting or
  unloading of an animal in or from a conveyance or container,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} beat, strike, whip or kick the animal;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} use a prod, whip or any other driving device on the
    animal in a manner that is likely to cause the animal's suffering,
    injury or death;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} use a prod, whip or any other driving device on an
    animal to make it move if it does not have a clear path to move;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} apply an electric prod or a device that has a
    similar effect to an animal unless the animal is a bovine or porcine
    of at least three months of age and the prod or device is not
    applied to sensitive areas of the animal including the belly and the
    anal, genital and facial regions of the animal;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} drag the animal;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} lift the animal by its fleece, fur, feathers, head,
    neck, ears or horns or by one wing;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} handle or lift the animal by its tail; or

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} handle the animal in any other way that is likely
    to cause the animal's suffering, injury or death.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall, when an animal is in a container,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} drop, kick or throw the container; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} handle the container in any other manner that is
    likely to cause the animal's suffering, injury or death.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 81
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227030){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227030.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-144-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 144"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[145]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-145 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who is loading or unloading an animal in or from a conveyance
  or container shall use a fixed or movable ramp, gangway, chute, step
  or apparatus unless the animal is not likely to suffer, sustain an
  injury or die by stepping directly from or onto the ground or other
  surface.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall load or unload an animal in or from a
  conveyance or container, or cause one to be so loaded or unloaded,
  using a fixed or movable ramp, gangway, chute, step or apparatus
  unless it is used in a manner that is not likely to cause the animal's
  suffering, injury or death and the following conditions with respect
  to the ramps, gangways, chutes, steps or apparatuses are met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} they can bear the weight to which they are
    subjected without collapsing, twisting, breaking or bending;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} there are side rails of sufficient strength and
    height to prevent the animal from falling off;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} they have a surface that is designed, constructed
    and maintained to prevent the animal from tripping, slipping and
    falling; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} they are placed so that there is no unprotected gap
    through which the animal could trip, slip, fall or escape.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Side rails are not required if the animal is loaded
  and unloaded individually in a manner that is not likely to cause the
  animal's suffering, injury or death.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall load or unload livestock or cervids
  --- or cause them to be loaded or unloaded --- into or from a
  conveyance or container using ramps, gangways, chutes or apparatuses
  that have a slope from the horizontal that exceeds

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 20° in the case of a porcine;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 25° in the case of a bovine;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 30° in the case of an equine; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} 35° in the case of a cervid, caprine or ovine.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227046){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227046.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-145-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 145"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Protection from Inadequate Ventilation and Weather Conditions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548161 .Subheading}

**[[146]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-146 .sectionLabel}** No person shall load,
confine or transport an animal in or unload an animal from a conveyance
or container, or cause one to be so loaded, confined, transported or
unloaded, if the animal is likely to suffer, sustain an injury or die
due to inadequate ventilation or by being exposed to meteorological or
environmental conditions.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227050){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227050.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-146-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 146"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Exposure to Toxic or Noxious Things]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1227051 .Subheading}

**[[146.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-146.1 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
load, confine or transport an animal in or unload an animal from a
conveyance or container, or cause one to be so loaded, confined,
transported or unloaded, if the animal is likely to suffer, sustain an
injury or die by being exposed to anything that is toxic or noxious,
including exhaust from the conveyance.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227054){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227054.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Space Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548163 .Subheading}

- **[[147]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-147 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall load, confine or transport an animal, or cause one to be
  loaded, confined or transported, in a conveyance or container unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of livestock, cervids, camelids and
    ratites, the animal is able to stand at all times within the
    conveyance or container with all feet on the floor, with head
    elevated, with sufficient space to permit a full range of head
    movement and without any part of its body coming into contact with a
    deck, roof or top of the conveyance or cover of the container;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of poultry, other than ratites,
    confined in a container, the animal is able to maintain a squatting
    or sitting position with sufficient space to permit a full range of
    head movement without coming into contact with the cover of the
    container; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of all other animals, and poultry that
    is not confined in a container, the animal is able to maintain its
    preferred position with sufficient space to permit a full range of
    head movement.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall load, confine or transport an equine,
  or cause an equine to be loaded, confined or transported by land, in a
  conveyance that has more than one deck.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227063){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227063.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-147-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 147"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Overcrowding]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548165 .Subheading}

- **[[148]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall load an animal, or cause one to be loaded, in a
  conveyance or container, other than a container that is used to
  transport an animal in an aircraft, in a manner that would result in
  the conveyance or container becoming overcrowded, or transport or
  confine an animal in a conveyance or container, or cause one to be
  transported or confined, in a conveyance or container that is
  overcrowded.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), overcrowding
  occurs when, due to the number of animals in the container or
  conveyance,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the animal cannot maintain its preferred position
    or adjust its body position in order to protect itself from injuries
    or avoid being crushed or trampled;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the animal is likely to develop a pathological
    condition such as hyperthermia, hypothermia or frostbite; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the animal is likely to suffer, sustain an injury
    or die.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-85, s. 82
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227076){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227076.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-148-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 148"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[148.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148.1 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
transport an animal by air, or cause one to be transported by air,
unless it is transported in a container that meets the stocking density
guidelines that are set out in the Live Animals Regulations, 44th
edition, published by the International Air Transport Association, as
amended from time to time.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227079){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227079.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Isolation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548189 .Subheading}

- **[[149]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-149 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall load, confine, transport or unload incompatible animals,
  or cause incompatible animals to be loaded, confined, transported or
  unloaded, in the same conveyance or container unless they are isolated
  from one another.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), animals are
  incompatible if any of the animals are likely to suffer, sustain an
  injury or die if they are loaded, confined, transported or unloaded
  together.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227085){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227085.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-149-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 149"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Conveyances and Containers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548191 .Subheading}

- **[[150]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-150 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall load, confine or transport an animal, or cause one to be
  loaded, confined or transported, in a conveyance or container unless
  the conveyance and the container, if the animal is in a container, are
  designed, constructed, equipped, maintained and used to prevent the
  animal's suffering, injury or death and the conveyance and the
  container, if the animal is in a container, meet the following
  conditions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} they are suitable for the species of animal;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} they prevent the animal's escape;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} they provide adequate ventilation for each animal;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} they provide a floor that prevents the animal from
    tripping, slipping and falling;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} they are not likely to collapse or topple over;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} they do not have exposed bolt heads, angles or
    other projections;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} they do not contain objects that are unsecured;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} they have no insecure fittings;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} they can be cleaned except in the case of a
    container that is used for one-time animal transport; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a container, the animal is visible
    from outside the container or at least two of the container's outer
    sides have a readily visible sign or symbol indicating the presence
    within of a live animal and a readily visible sign or symbol
    indicating the upright position of the container.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a container that is
  used to confine or transport an animal in an aircraft.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall load, confine or transport, or cause
  to be loaded, confined or transported, livestock, cervids, camelids or
  ratites in a conveyance or container unless the conveyance or, if the
  animal is in a container, the container has a floor that is strewn
  with sufficient sand, straw, wood shavings or other bedding material
  to absorb and prevent the pooling or escape of water, urine and liquid
  manure.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall confine or transport an animal in a
  container unless the container is secured to the conveyance in a
  manner that prevents it from moving during transport.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} No person shall transport an animal by air, or cause
  an animal to be transported by air, unless it is transported in a
  container that meets the design and construction requirements that are
  set out in the Live Animals Regulations, 44th edition, published by
  the International Air Transport Association, as amended from time to
  time.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227103){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227103.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 18(F)](#1124109-1124699){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124109-1124699.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-150-20190415.html "Link to previous version of section 150"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Vessels]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548193 .Subheading}

- **[[151]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall load, confine or transport livestock and poultry, or
  cause them to be loaded, confined or transported, in a vessel unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vessel is equipped with passageways to permit
    the care of the livestock and poultry;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the vessel is equipped with an enclosed area or pen
    to accommodate livestock and poultry that become injured, ill,
    compromised or unfit during transport;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the vessel is equipped with lighting --- including
    emergency lighting --- that is appropriate to permit the care of the
    livestock and poultry;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the vessel has on board lighting equipment that is
    appropriate to enable the examination of the livestock and poultry;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the vessel has on board humane killing devices that
    are in good working order and are an appropriate type for the
    species, sex, age and weight of the livestock and poultry;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the vessel has on board a sufficient quantity and
    type of supplies, including medication, for the care of the
    livestock and poultry on the vessel taking into account the species
    of the livestock or poultry and the duration of the transport; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the vessel has on board dispensing systems to
    provide feed and safe water to the livestock and poultry.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The sea carrier shall have a person on board the
  vessel who is trained to use the humane killing devices that are on
  board the vessel.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The sea carrier shall have an appropriate number of
  trained persons on board the vessel to provide for the care of the
  livestock and poultry.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every sea carrier shall, before departure, have on
  board the vessel,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a sufficient amount of feed and safe water for each
    animal to be transported, having regard to the expected duration of
    the transport, to prevent a nutritional deficit and to prevent them
    from becoming dehydrated; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an additional one-day supply of feed and safe water
    for each animal based on the amount determined in paragraph (a) for
    every period of four days or less of the expected duration of the
    transport.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The sea carrier shall store the feed and safe water
  in a place and in a manner that will prevent the feed and safe water
  from posing a risk to the animal's health and from otherwise becoming
  unsuitable for their consumption.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-597, s. 15
- SOR/79-839, s. 33
- SOR/80-516, s. 13
- SOR/82-590, s. 12
- SOR/93-159, s. 16
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227120){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227120.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-151-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 151"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[151.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151.1 .sectionLabel}** If the duration of
the transport of the livestock and poultry is expected to exceed six
hours, the sea carrier or vessel master shall, at least 24 hours before
the departure, provide a veterinary inspector with the following
information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the planned date and time of departure from the port
  of origin and arrival at the destination;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person who will be in charge of
  caring for the livestock and poultry; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the procedures for communication that would allow the
  person who is in charge of caring for the livestock and poultry to
  obtain veterinary advice as required during transport.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227126){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227126.html"
  role="button"}
:::

**[[151.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151.2 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
confine or transport livestock and poultry on a vessel, or cause them to
be confined or transported on a vessel, in the vicinity of an engine
casing or any boiler room casing if it is likely to cause the livestock
or poultry to suffer, sustain an injury or die unless the casing is
covered and insulated to prevent that suffering, injury or death.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227129){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227129.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Feed, Safe Water and Rest]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548214 .Subheading}

**[[152]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152 .sectionLabel}** At the time of loading
an animal for transport, a commercial carrier or any other person
transporting the animal in the course of business or for financial
benefit shall determine the date, time and place where the animal was
last fed, watered and rested.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227132){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227132.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 18(F)](#1124109-1124701){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124109-1124701.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152-20190415.html "Link to previous version of section 152"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[152.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall load, confine or
  transport an animal in a conveyance or container, or cause one to be
  so loaded, confined or transported, unless the person provides the
  animal with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} feed of an appropriate type for its species, age
    and condition and in amounts that are sufficient to prevent a
    nutritional deficit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} safe water in amounts that are sufficient to
    prevent the animal from becoming dehydrated; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} rest that is appropriate for its species, age and
    condition to prevent the animal from suffering from exhaustion.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In order to meet the requirements set out in
  subsection (1), every person who loads, confines or transports an
  animal in a conveyance or container shall monitor the animal on a
  regular basis.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227140){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227140.html"
  role="button"}
:::

- **[[152.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall load, confine or
  transport an animal in or unload an animal from a conveyance or
  container, or cause one to be so loaded, confined, transported or
  unloaded, unless the person provides the animal with feed, safe water
  and rest at intervals that do not exceed the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 12 hours for any compromised animals;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of safe water, 24 hours for broiler
    chickens, spent laying hens and rabbits and in the case of feed and
    rest for those animals, 28 hours;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 28 hours for equines and porcines; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} 36 hours for all other animals.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), no person shall load, confine
  or transport newly hatched poultry in or unload such animals from a
  conveyance or container, or cause them to be so loaded, confined,
  transported or unloaded, unless the person provides them with feed,
  safe water and rest within 72 hours after the time of hatching.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), no person shall load, confine
  or transport ruminants that are too young to be fed exclusively on hay
  and grain and livestock, camelids or cervids of eight days of age or
  less in or unload such animals from a conveyance or container, or
  cause them to be so loaded, confined, transported or unloaded, unless
  the person provides them with feed, safe water and rest within 12
  hours after the time they were last provided with feed, safe water and
  rest prior to loading.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), an interval
  begins

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of feed, when the animal was last fed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of safe water, when the animal was last
    given safe water; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of rest, when the animal was last
    rested for a minimum of eight consecutive hours.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227154){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227154.html"
  role="button"}
:::

**[[152.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.3 .sectionLabel}** Every person who
loads, confines or transports an animal in or unloads an animal from a
conveyance or container shall ensure that the following conditions are
met whenever the conveyance is stopped for the purpose of providing
feed, safe water and rest to the animal:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} equipment that is designed, constructed and
  maintained to feed and water the animal is used;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} sufficient space is provided to allow the animal to
  lie down without lying on top of other animals;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} floors that are well-drained and clean are provided
  so that the animal is not likely to trip, slip, fall, suffer, sustain
  an injury or die;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} sufficient straw or other bedding is provided so that
  the animal is not likely to suffer, sustain an injury or die;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} sufficient straw or other bedding is provided so that
  the animal is kept clean and dry;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} protection from meteorological and environmental
  conditions is provided so that the animal is not likely to suffer,
  sustain an injury or die; and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} adequate ventilation is provided so that the animal
  is not likely to suffer, sustain an injury or die.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227164){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227164.html"
  role="button"}
:::

- **[[152.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Section 152.2 does not apply in the
  case of an animal that is loaded, confined or transported in a
  conveyance if the conveyance meets the following conditions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is equipped with a dispensing system that is
    designed, constructed and maintained so that feed is available to
    the animal as required;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is equipped with a dispensing system that is
    designed, constructed and maintained so that safe water is available
    to the animal at all times;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is equipped with forced ventilation that is
    designed, constructed, maintained and used so that adequate
    ventilation is provided to the animal at all times;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it is equipped with a rigid exterior with
    adjustable venting that is designed, constructed, maintained and
    used so that adequate ventilation is provided to the animal and the
    animal is protected from meteorological and environmental conditions
    at all times;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} it is equipped with a system that electronically
    monitors and records temperatures and humidity in the parts of the
    conveyance where the animal is most likely to be exposed to
    temperatures and humidity that would likely cause the animal to
    suffer, sustain an injury or die;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} it is equipped with a system that alerts the person
    operating the conveyance when a set high or low temperature or
    humidity reading in the conveyance is reached;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} it has sufficient space to allow the animal to lie
    down without lying on top of other animals;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} it has floors that are well-drained and clean so
    that the animal is not likely to trip, slip, fall, suffer, sustain
    an injury or die;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it has sufficient straw or other bedding so that
    the animal is not likely to suffer, sustain an injury or die; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} it has sufficient straw or other bedding so that
    the animal is kept clean and dry.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 152.2 does not apply in the case of an animal
  that is loaded, confined or transported in a container that is in a
  conveyance that meets the conditions set out in subsection (1) if the
  container meets the following conditions:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is designed, constructed and maintained so that
    the animal in the container is able to access the feed dispensed by
    the feed dispensing system on the conveyance;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is designed, constructed and maintained so that
    the animal in the container is able to access, at all times, the
    safe water dispensed by the safe water dispensing system on the
    conveyance;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is designed, constructed and maintained so that
    the animal in the container is provided with adequate ventilation at
    all times;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it has sufficient space to allow the animal to lie
    down without lying on top of other animals;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} it has floors that are well-drained and clean so
    that the animal is not likely to trip, slip, fall, suffer, sustain
    an injury or die;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} except in the case of rabbits and poultry, it has
    sufficient straw or other bedding so that the animal is not likely
    to suffer, sustain an injury or die; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} it has sufficient straw or other bedding so that
    the animal is kept clean and dry.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227186){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227186.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Transfer of Care]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548225 .Subheading}

- **[[153]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person who transports an animal shall leave the animal at a slaughter
  establishment or assembly centre unless the person has provided the
  consignee with a written notice that the animal has arrived at the
  slaughter establishment or assembly centre and a document that
  contains the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the condition of the animal on arrival;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date and time when and the place where the
    animal was last fed, watered and rested; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date and time of arrival of the animal at the
    slaughter establishment or assembly centre.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The responsibility for the care of the animal is
  transferred from the person who transports the animal to the consignee
  as soon as the consignee acknowledges receipt of the notice and
  document.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the consignee who assumes
  responsibility for the care of an animal shall comply with the
  requirements with respect to feed, water and rest set out in sections
  152.1 and 152.2.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 36
- SOR/97-85, s. 83
- SOR/98-409, s. 14(F)
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227195){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227195.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-153-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 153"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Records]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548240 .Subheading}

- **[[154]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every commercial carrier and any
  other person who transports animals in the course of business or for
  financial benefit shall, for each shipment of animals, make a record
  at the time of loading the animals that includes the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the shipper, consignee and
    person operating the conveyance in which the animals are
    transported;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the identifying number or registration number of
    the conveyance;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the number of square metres or square feet of floor
    area available to the animals in the conveyance or, if the animals
    are in a container, in the container;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date and time when and the place where the
    conveyance or container was last cleaned and disinfected;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the date and time when and the place where the
    animals are loaded;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the number, description and weight of the animals;
    and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the date and time when the animals were last fed,
    watered and rested prior to loading.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person referred to in subsection (1) shall, as
  soon as possible, include in the record any changes to the information
  provided in subsection (1) and add the following information to the
  record:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date and time when and the place where the
    animals are fed, watered and rested; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date, time and place of arrival of the animals
    at the destination.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person referred to in subsection (1) shall,
  with each shipment of animals being transported, keep on board the
  original record or a copy of the record.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-69, s. 37
- [SOR/2019-38, s. 2](#1111516-1227210){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1111516-1227210.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-154-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 154"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Dead and Seriously Injured Animals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548247 .Subheading}

**[[155]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155 .sectionLabel}** Every air carrier and
sea carrier that transports an animal from Canada shall, as soon as
possible after arrival at the destination, send a document to the
veterinary inspector at the port of embarkation respecting every animal
that was seriously injured, has died or was killed during transport,
stating in each case the cause of the injury or death.

::: HistoricalNote

  role="button"}

## [PART XIII]{.HLabel1}[Permits and Licences]{.HTitleText1} {#h-548265 .Part}

### [Form and Conditions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548266 .Subheading}

- **[[160]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  application for a permit or licence required under the Act shall be in
  a form approved by the Minister.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall issue a permit or licence
  required under the Act if the Minister determines that the activity
  for which the permit or licence is issued would not, or would not be
  likely to, result in the introduction into or spread within Canada of
  a vector, disease or toxic substance or its introduction into another
  country from Canada.

- [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} A permit or licence issued by the Minister under
  these Regulations may be issued as a general permit or licence to
  owners or persons having the possession, care or control of an animal
  or thing for which the permit or licence is issued.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any permit or licence required under the Act shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be in a form approved by the Minister; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} contain such conditions as are necessary to prevent
    the introduction of communicable disease into Canada or into any
    other country from Canada and the spread of communicable disease
    within Canada.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may cancel or suspend a permit or
  licence issued under the Act if he or she has reason to believe that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any condition under which the permit or licence was
    issued or any condition contained in the permit or licence has not
    been complied with;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any provision of the Act or these Regulations has
    not been complied with; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} failure to do so could result in the introduction
    into or spread within Canada of a vector, disease or toxic substance
    or its introduction into another country from Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/79-839, s. 34
- SOR/92-23, s. 3
- SOR/92-650, s. 4
- SOR/93-159, s. 17
- SOR/95-475, s. 4(F)
- SOR/2004-80, s. 17
- SOR/2006-147, s. 19
- SOR/2012-286, s. 60
- SOR/2017-94, s. 14
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 14](#1124094-1124674){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124094-1124674.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-160-20170519.html "Link to previous version of section 160"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[160.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160.1 .sectionLabel}** Every person who is
subject to a permit or licence issued under the Act shall comply with
the conditions contained in the permit or licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/93-159, s. 18
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 15](#1124103-1124682){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124103-1124682.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-160.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 160.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Issue of Licences by an Inspector]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548282 .Subheading}

- **[[161]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-161 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  inspector may issue a licence authorizing the marketing, selling,
  disposing, exposing for sale or transporting of animals or things
  affected with or suffering from or suspected of being affected with or
  suffering from infectious or contagious disease.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A licence issued pursuant to subsection (1) may state
  how, when and where animals or things affected with or suffering from
  or suspected of being affected with or suffering from infectious or
  contagious disease may be marketed, sold, disposed of, exposed for
  sale or transported.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A licence issued pursuant to subsection (1) is valid
  only when used by the person to whom it is issued.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall sell or transfer a licence issued
  pursuant to subsection (1) to any other person.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/78-597, s. 16
:::

### [Amendment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1124683 .Subheading}

**[[161.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-161.1 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may,
whether on his or her own initiative or on the application of the
holder, amend a permit or licence issued under the Act if the amendment
is made as the result of new information, or a change in circumstance,
that has been brought to the Minister's attention and does not increase
the risk of the introduction into or spread within Canada of a vector,
disease or toxic substance or its introduction into another country from
Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 16](#1124105-1124686){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124105-1124686.html"
  role="button"}
:::

## [PART XIV]{.HLabel1}[Food for Ruminants, Livestock and Poultry, Rendering Plants, Fertilizers and Fertilizer Supplements]{.HTitleText1} {#h-548290 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2006-147, s. 20

\]
:::

### [Prohibited Material]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548293 .Subheading}

- **[[162]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-162 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this Part, [[prohibited material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means anything
  that is, or that contains any, protein that originated from a mammal,
  other than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a porcine or equine;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} milk or products of milk;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} gelatin derived exclusively from hides or skins or
    products of gelatin derived exclusively from hides or skins;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} blood or products of blood; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} rendered fats, derived from ruminants, that contain
    no more than 0.15% insoluble impurities or their products.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Prohibited material that has been treated in a manner
  approved by the Minister to inactivate the agents that cause
  transmissible spongiform encephalopathies is no longer prohibited
  material.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-362, s. 4
- SOR/2006-147, s. 21
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-162-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 162"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[163]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-163 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who identifies prohibited material by means of adding to it a
  marker or tracer substance that has been approved by the Minister in
  the manner specified in that approval, is not required to keep the
  records referred to in subsections 165(2) and 166(2) and section 171.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who identifies prohibited material in
  accordance with subsection (1) shall maintain a record of the manner
  in which the marker or tracer substance was added to the prohibited
  material.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-362, s. 4
:::

### [Feeding Prohibited Material to a Ruminant.]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548309 .Subheading}

**[[164]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-164 .sectionLabel}** No person shall feed
prohibited material to a ruminant.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-362, s. 4
:::

### [Rendering Plants]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548313 .Subheading}

- **[[165]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-165 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall operate a rendering plant unless the person does so under
  a permit issued in accordance with section 160.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} No person who operates a rendering plant shall
  manufacture a prohibited material on the same premises as material not
  prohibited from being fed to ruminants without having dedicated
  manufacturing lines, equipment and conveyances to prevent the mixing
  or contamination of material not prohibited from being fed to
  ruminants with or by prohibited material.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who operates a rendering plant shall
  keep a record of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date of production of all products of the
    rendering plant;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whether or not any product of the rendering plant
    is, or contains any, prohibited material;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name, and quantity of, and any other
    information that is sufficient to identify, the products of the
    rendering plant; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of any person to whom any
    product of the rendering plant is distributed or sold and the
    information referred to in paragraph (c) with respect to that
    product.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The records that are required by subsection (2) to be
  kept by a person who operates a rendering plant shall be kept by that
  person for 10 years from the date the requirement arises.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person who operates a rendering plant shall
  distribute or sell any product of the rendering plant that contains
  prohibited material unless the documentation required by these
  Regulations relating to the product and any label on any packaging or
  container containing the product is marked conspicuously, legibly and
  indelibly with the following statement:

  "Feeding this product to cattle, sheep, deer or other ruminants is
  illegal and is subject to fines or other punishment under the [Health
  of Animals Act](/eng/acts/H-3.3)./[Il est interdit d'en nourrir les
  boeufs, moutons, cerfs et autres ruminants et des amendes ou autres
  peines sont prévues à cet égard par la [Loi sur la santé des
  animaux](/eng/acts/H-3.3).]{.otherLang lang="fr"}"

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-362, s. 4
- SOR/2006-147, s. 22
- SOR/2015-55, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-165-20070712.html "Link to previous version of section 165"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Importation of Products of Rendering Plants]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548327 .Subheading}

- **[[166]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-166 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall import any product of a rendering plant unless the person
  does so under a permit issued in accordance with section 160.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who imports or has the possession, care
  or control of any product of a rendering plant shall keep for 10 years
  a record of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the rendering plant and the
    date of production of the product;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the exporter;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name, lot number and quantity of the product
    and any other information that is sufficient to identify the
    product;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of any person to whom any
    product is distributed or sold and the information referred to in
    paragraph (c) with respect to that product; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} whether or not the product is, or contains any,
    prohibited material.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-362, s. 4
- SOR/2006-147, s. 23
- SOR/2015-55, s. 9
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-166-20070712.html "Link to previous version of section 166"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Importation or Sale of Products of Rendering Plants]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548338 .Subheading}

**[[167]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-167 .sectionLabel}** No person who imports
or has the possession, care or control of a product of a rendering plant
shall sell or distribute the product unless the documentation required
by these Regulations relating to the product and any label on any
packaging or container containing the product is marked conspicuously,
legibly and indelibly with the statement referred to in subsection
165(4).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-362, s. 4
- SOR/2009-220, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-167-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 167"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Recall Procedures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548342 .Subheading}

- **[[167.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-167.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who operates a
  rendering plant shall establish and maintain written procedures to
  facilitate an effective recall of the products of the plant.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who imports any product of a rendering
  plant shall establish and maintain written procedures to facilitate an
  effective recall of the product.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-147, s. 24
- SOR/2012-286, s. 61(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-167.1-20070712.html "Link to previous version of section 167.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Food and Food Ingredients]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548348 .Subheading}

**[[168]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-168 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import,
manufacture, package, label, store, distribute, sell or advertise for
sale any animal food for ruminants that contains prohibited material.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-362, s. 4
:::

**[[169]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-169 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import,
manufacture, package, store, distribute, sell or advertise for sale any
animal food for equines, porcines, chickens, turkeys, ducks, geese,
ratites or game birds that contains prohibited material unless the
documentation required by these Regulations relating to the animal food
and any label on any packaging or container containing the animal food
is marked conspicuously, legibly and indelibly with a statement approved
by the Minister that indicates that the animal food shall not be fed to
ruminants.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-362, s. 4
:::

- **[[170]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-170 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall have any prohibited material or anything, including an
  animal food for equines, porcines, chickens, turkeys, ducks, geese,
  ratites or game birds, that contains prohibited material on the same
  premises or in the same conveyance as a product of a rendering plant
  that does not contain prohibited material or any animal food for
  ruminants, without having procedures to prevent the mixing or
  contamination of the rendering plant product or animal food for
  ruminants, with prohibited material.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In a case referred to in subsection (1), the person
  shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the procedures are followed from the
    time the product or animal food is received until it leaves their
    possession, care or control; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} keep for 10 years a written record of the rendering
    plant products, the animal food for ruminants and the prohibited
    material.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a person fails to comply with subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person shall change the records to show that
    all of the product or animal food is prohibited material and any
    label on any packaging or container containing the product or animal
    food shall be marked conspicuously, legibly and indelibly with a
    statement approved by the Minister that indicates that the product
    or animal food shall not be fed to ruminants;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all of the product or animal food shall be
    considered to be prohibited material for the purposes of section
    164; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person shall recall any product or animal food
    that may have been destined for feeding to ruminants if the product
    or animal food is found to contain prohibited material or if the
    Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that the product or
    animal food contains prohibited material.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-362, s. 4
- SOR/2006-147, s. 25
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-170-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 170"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Recall Procedures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548366 .Subheading}

**[[170.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-170.1 .sectionLabel}** Every person who
imports, manufactures, packages, labels, stores, distributes, sells or
advertises for sale any animal food for ruminants, equines, porcines,
chickens, turkeys, ducks, geese, ratites or game birds shall establish
and maintain written procedures to facilitate an effective recall of the
animal food.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-147, s. 26
- SOR/2012-286, s. 62(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-170.1-20070712.html "Link to previous version of section 170.1"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[170.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-170.2 .sectionLabel}** Every person who
manufactures, imports, sells or distributes a fertilizer or fertilizer
supplement containing prohibited material, other than a rendered fat,
shall establish and maintain written procedures to facilitate an
effective recall of the fertilizer or fertilizer supplement.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-147, s. 26
- SOR/2012-286, s. 62
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-170.2-20070712.html "Link to previous version of section 170.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Records]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548373 .Subheading}

- **[[171]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-171
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who manufactures animal
  food for ruminants, equines, porcines, chickens, turkeys, ducks,
  geese, ratites or game birds shall keep, for 10 years, records that
  contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the formula for the animal food, including the name
    and weight of each ingredient used for each lot of the animal food;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a mixing sheet that shows that each lot of the
    animal food has been produced in accordance with the formula
    referred to in paragraph (a);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} information as to whether or not the animal food
    contains any prohibited material;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date of preparation of the animal food;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the lot number and any other information used to
    identify each lot of animal food; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of any person to whom any
    animal food is distributed or sold and a description of the food,
    including the name and quantity.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who imports, packages, stores,
  distributes, sells or advertises for sale animal food for ruminants,
  equines, porcines, chickens, turkeys, ducks, geese, ratites or game
  birds shall keep, for 10 years, records that contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, the lot number and any other information
    used to identify the animal food;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of any person to whom the
    animal food is distributed or sold and a description of the animal
    food, including the name and quantity; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} information as to whether or not the animal food
    contains any prohibited material.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person who owns or has the possession, care or
  custody of a ruminant shall keep copies of all invoices for animal
  food that contains prohibited material.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/97-362, s. 4
- SOR/2006-147, s. 27
- SOR/2007-24, s. 7
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-171-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 171"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[171.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-171.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who manufactures a
  fertilizer or fertilizer supplement containing prohibited material,
  other than a rendered fat, shall keep for 10 years records that are
  sufficient

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to demonstrate that the person

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} has not used specified risk material in any form,
      whether or not incorporated into another thing, as an ingredient
      of the fertilizer or fertilizer supplement, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has used specified risk material in any form,
      whether or not incorporated into another thing, as an ingredient
      of the fertilizer or fertilizer supplement only in accordance with
      a permit issued under section 160 for the purpose of section 6.4;
      and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to facilitate an effective recall of the fertilizer
    or fertilizer supplement.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The records shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of any person who supplied the
    prohibited material to the person manufacturing the fertilizer or
    fertilizer supplement and a statement, signed by the supplier, that
    the prohibited material does not contain specified risk material or
    that it contains specified risk material in accordance with a permit
    issued under section 160 for the purpose of section 6.4;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the formula for the fertilizer or fertilizer
    supplement, including the name and weight of each ingredient used
    for each lot of the fertilizer or fertilizer supplement;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a mixing sheet that shows that each lot of the
    fertilizer or fertilizer supplement has been produced in accordance
    with the formula referred to in paragraph (b);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date of preparation of the fertilizer or
    fertilizer supplement;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any information used to identify each lot of the
    fertilizer or fertilizer supplement; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of any person to whom any of
    the fertilizer or fertilizer supplement is sold or distributed and a
    description of the fertilizer or fertilizer supplement, including
    the name and quantity.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[specified risk
  material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in section 6.1.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-147, s. 28
- SOR/2009-18, s. 18
- SOR/2012-286, s. 63(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-171.1-20090630.html "Link to previous version of section 171.1"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[171.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-171.2 .sectionLabel}** Every person who
imports, sells or distributes a fertilizer or fertilizer supplement
containing prohibited material, other than a rendered fat, shall keep
for 10 years records that are sufficient to facilitate an effective
recall of the fertilizer or fertilizer supplement, including

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, the lot number and other information used
  to identify the fertilizer or fertilizer supplement; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of any person to whom the
  fertilizer or fertilizer supplement is sold or distributed and a
  description of the fertilizer or fertilizer supplement, including the
  name and quantity.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2006-147, s. 28
:::

## [PART XV]{.HLabel1}[Animal Identification]{.HTitleText1} {#h-548410 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548411 .Subheading}

- **[[172]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-172 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  definitions in this section apply in this Part.

  [[administrator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[administrator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2014-23, s.
      3\]]{.Repealed}

  [[animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bison, bovine, ovine or
      pig. ([animal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[approved slap tattoo]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[approved slap tattoo]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a slap tattoo
      bearing an identification number issued by the responsible
      administrator in respect of a site under paragraph
      174(2)(a). ([tatouage au marteau approuvé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[approved tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[approved tag]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a tag, chip or other
      indicator approved by the Minister under subsection 173(1) and
      listed on the Agency's web site as an approved tag. ([étiquette
      approuvée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[bison]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bison]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an animal, other than an
      embryo or a fertilized egg, of the subspecies [Bison bison
      bison]{.otherLang lang="la"}, [Bison bison athabascae]{.otherLang
      lang="la"} or [Bison bison bonasus]{.otherLang
      lang="la"}. ([bison]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[bovine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bovine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an animal, other than an
      embryo or a fertilized egg, of the species [Bos taurus]{.otherLang
      lang="la"} or [Bos indicus]{.otherLang
      lang="la"}. ([bovin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[bred]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[bred]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an animal, means that
      the animal has been mated either naturally or artificially or has
      provided semen, ova or embryos for
      reproduction. ([sailli]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual, a
      partnership, a corporation, a cooperative, an association or an
      organization that sells or distributes approved
      tags. ([distributeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[farm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[farm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means land, and all buildings and
      other structures on that land, that is used under one management
      for breeding or raising animals but does not include an artificial
      insemination unit. ([ferme]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[farm of origin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[farm of origin]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the farm on which an
      animal is born or, if an animal is not born on a farm, the first
      farm to which it is moved after its birth. ([ferme
      d'origine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[farm or ranch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[farm or ranch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2014-23, s.
      3\]]{.Repealed}

  [[organization that manages an animal identification system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[organization that manages an animal identification
      system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual, a partnership, a
      corporation, a cooperative, an association or an organization that
      is authorized by provincial legislation to manage an animal
      identification system. ([organisme de gestion d'un système
      d'identification des animaux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ovine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ovine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an animal, other than an
      embryo or a fertilized egg, of the genus [Ovis]{.otherLang
      lang="la"}. ([ovin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pig]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pig]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an animal, other than an embryo
      or a fertilized egg, of the genus [Sus]{.otherLang
      lang="la"}. ([porc]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[responsible administrator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[responsible administrator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
      who is authorized by the Minister to receive information in
      relation to animals or things to which the Act or these
      Regulations apply, is listed on the Agency's web site as an
      administrator and administers a national identification program in
      relation to certain animals of all or part of one or more genera,
      species or subspecies that are located in one or more
      provinces. ([administrateur responsable]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place where animals or
      carcasses of animals are kept or collected but does not include a
      conveyance. ([installation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In this Part, other than in subsection 175.1(2),
  paragraph 186(1)(a) and subsection 186(2), any reference to the
  carcass of an animal or to a part of the carcass of an animal does not
  include any part of the carcass that is intended for human
  consumption.

::: HistoricalNote
