### [Registration of Linked Sites]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548433 .Subheading}

- **[[172.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-172.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On application from the operators of
  two farms where pigs are kept, the responsible administrator shall
  register the two sites as being linked with each other for the
  purposes of this Part for a period of six months if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sites and all sites having linked health status
    with either of them are all located in only one of the following
    parts of Canada:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the eastern part of Canada that includes Ontario,
      Quebec, Nova Scotia, New Brunswick, Prince Edward Island and
      Newfoundland and Labrador, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the western part of Canada that includes
      Manitoba, British Columbia, Saskatchewan, Alberta, Yukon,
      Northwest Territories and Nunavut;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pigs are transported between the sites at least
    three times per month;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the operators of the sites have reported to the
    responsible administrator information identifying all of the
    conveyances being used to transport pigs between the sites and all
    movements of pigs between the sites are made exclusively by those
    conveyances; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the sites were not registered as
    being linked with each other during the three-month period before
    the application, the operators of the sites have reported each
    transport of pigs between the sites to the responsible administrator
    for that three-month period and the reports of each operator are
    consistent with those of the other.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If by the end of the 10th day of any month in the
  six-month period for which two sites are registered as being linked
  the operators of the two sites have not reported to the responsible
  administrator the total number of pigs that were transported between
  the sites and the number of pig movements between the sites during the
  preceding month, the registration in respect of the two sites being
  linked shall be cancelled at the end of that day for the remainder of
  the six-month period.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of this section, two sites have
  linked health status with each other if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the two sites are registered under subsection (1)
    as being linked with each other or have been registered under that
    subsection as being linked with each other at any time in the
    preceding six months; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one of the two sites, at any time in the preceding
    six months, is considered to have had linked health status under
    this section with another site that, at any time in those six
    months, is considered to have had linked health status under this
    section with the other of the two sites.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-23, s. 4
:::

### [Approval, Issuance and Revocation of Tags]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548448 .Subheading}

- **[[173]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-173 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may approve or revoke a tag, chip or other indicator for the
  identification of an animal, or the carcass of an animal, for the
  purposes of this Part.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} When considering the approval of a tag, chip or other
  indicator, the Minister shall take into account whether

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the tag, chip or other indicator bears a unique
    identification number;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the tag, chip or other indicator may not readily be
    altered or otherwise tampered with;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the tag, chip or other indicator is difficult to
    counterfeit;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the identification number on the tag, chip or other
    indicator is easily and reliably readable; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the tag, chip or other indicator is designed to be
    retained by any animal to which it may be applied.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} When considering the revocation of an approved tag,
  chip or other indicator, the Minister shall take into account whether
  there is any other tag, chip or other indicator that offers improved
  performance over that approved tag, chip or other indicator with
  respect to the criteria set out in paragraphs (2)(b) to (e).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2010-137, s. 1
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-173-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 173"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[174]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-174 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At
  the request of the operator of a site, the responsible administrator
  may issue approved tags or cause them to be issued for the purpose of
  identifying animals on that site.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} At the request of the operator of a site where pigs
  are kept, the responsible administrator may issue an identification
  number in respect of that site or cause one to be issued for the
  purpose of being applied by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} approved slap tattoos to pigs from that site that
    are destined for slaughter; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} indicators to pigs from that site that are destined
    for export.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} At the request of an importer of animals, the
  administrator may issue approved tags or cause them to be issued for
  the purpose of identifying the animals to be imported.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} When requesting an identification number in respect
  of a site under subsection (2), the operator of the site shall report
  to the responsible administrator their name, address and telephone
  number.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2014-23, s. 5
:::

[Previous
Version](section-174-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 174"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Reporting Requirement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548469 .Subheading}

**[[174.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-174.1 .sectionLabel}** A distributor, or
an organization that manages an animal identification system, that sells
or distributes approved tags shall, within 24 hours after selling or
distributing them, report the following information in respect of those
approved tags to the responsible administrator:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, address and telephone number of the person
  to whom they were sold or distributed;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date they were sold or distributed;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} their unique identification numbers; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the total number that were sold or distributed.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2005-192, s. 2
- SOR/2014-23, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-174.1-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 174.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Identification Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548477 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-23, s. 7

\]
:::

- **[[175]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided in this
  Part, every person who owns or has the possession, care or control of
  a bison, bovine or ovine or of the carcass of a bison, bovine or ovine
  shall ensure that it is identified by an approved tag that is applied
  to it before it is removed from a site.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who applies, or causes the application
  of, an approved tag to an animal, or the carcass of an animal, shall
  ensure that the tag is for the species of that animal and is applied
  to the animal, or the carcass, for which the tag was issued under
  subsection 174(1).

- [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who owns or has the possession, care
  or control of an animal or a carcass of an animal shall ensure that
  the approved tag that is applied to it is applied to its ear with the
  logo and number facing forward.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided in this Part, every
  person who owns or has the possession, care or control of a bison,
  bovine or ovine or of the carcass of a bison, bovine or ovine shall
  ensure that it bears the approved tag referred to in subsection (1) at
  all times after it is removed from its farm of origin.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person who owns or has the possession, care or
  control of a pig to which an approved tag has been applied shall
  ensure that it bears the approved tag until it is identified in some
  other manner provided for under these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2003-409, s. 2
- SOR/2005-192, s. 3
- SOR/2014-23, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-175-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 175"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[175.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided in this
  section, every person who owns or has the possession, care or control
  of a pig shall ensure that it is identified by an approved tag that is
  applied to it before it is removed from a site.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the transportation
  of pigs, other than bred pigs, between parts of a farm that are not
  contiguous or between farms if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the operator of the departure site ensures that the
    pigs being transported are accompanied with the following
    information in a form that can be immediately read by an inspector:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the location of the departure site and the
      location of the destination site,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date and time that the conveyance carrying
      the pigs left the departure site,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the number of pigs loaded onto the conveyance,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the identification number on any approved tag
      applied to the pigs, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the licence plate number or, if there is no
      licence plate, other identification of the conveyance;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operator of the departure site reports that
    information to the responsible administrator within seven days after
    the departure of the pigs; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the operator of the destination site, within seven
    days after the pigs' reception, reports the following information to
    the responsible administrator:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the location of the departure site and the
      location of the destination site,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date and time that the conveyance carrying
      the pigs arrived at the destination site,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the number of pigs and pig carcasses that
      arrived at the destination site,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the identification number on any approved tag
      applied to the pigs, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the licence plate number or, if there is no
      licence plate, other identification of the conveyance.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The reporting requirements set out in
  paragraphs (2)(b) and (c) do not apply if at the time of the
  transportation the departure site and the destination site are
  registered as being linked with each other under section 172.1.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} There is no requirement to identify pigs if they are
  transported from one part of a farm to another contiguous part of that
  farm.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a pig is transported directly from any site to any
  other site that is an abattoir or that is used for the purpose of
  collecting pigs for transport to an abattoir, every person who owns or
  has the possession, care or control of the pig at the departure site
  shall ensure that it is identified by an approved tag or approved slap
  tattoo before it is moved from that site.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (5), the operator of a site that
  is used for the purpose of collecting pigs for transport to an
  abattoir shall apply an approved tag to any pig bearing only an
  approved slap tattoo that remains at the collection site for longer
  than 96 hours or that is sent anywhere other than to an abattoir.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} Every person who exports a pig shall ensure that,
  before its export, it has applied to it an indicator approved by the
  importing country and bearing an identification number issued by the
  responsible administrator under paragraph 174(2)(b).

- [(8)]{.lawlabel} Every person who owns or has the possession, care or
  control of a pig carcass or part of a pig carcass that is transported
  from any site to any other site shall ensure that the carcass or the
  part is accompanied with the following information in a form that can
  be immediately read by an inspector:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the location of the departure site and the location
    of the destination site;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date and time that the conveyance carrying the
    carcass or the part left the departure site; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the licence plate number or, if there is no licence
    plate, other identification of the conveyance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-23, s. 9
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 24](#1515135-1516231){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515135-1516231.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Record-keeping and Information Reporting Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548515 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2014-23, s. 10

\]
:::

- **[[175.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  operator of a site who removes or causes the removal of an ovine
  18 months of age or older from the site shall keep a record of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the identification number on the approved tag that
    is applied  to the ovine;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of removal;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the reason for removal; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the owner or person having
    the possession, care or control of the ovine at the destination to
    which it is removed.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of an ovine
  that is transported directly for slaughter either to an establishment
  where food animals are slaughtered by the holder of a licence that is
  issued under paragraph 20(1)(b) of the [Safe Food for Canadians
  Act](/eng/acts/S-1.1) or to an establishment that is registered under
  an Act of a province that provides for the inspection of ovine
  carcasses.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The operator of a site who receives or causes the
  reception of a bred ovine shall keep a record of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the identification number on the approved tag that
    is applied  to the ovine;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date of reception; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the owner or person who had
    the possession, care or control of the ovine at the site from which
    it was removed.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every person who is required to keep a record under
  this section shall keep the record for a period of at least five
  years.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2003-409, s. 3
- SOR/2005-192, s. 4(E)
- SOR/2014-23, s. 11
- [SOR/2018-108, s. 406](#1044678-1110075){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1044678-1110075.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-175.1-20140701.html "Link to previous version of section 175.1"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[175.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided in this
  Part, if a pig is transported from one site to another, the operator
  of the departure site, within seven days after the pig's departure,
  and the operator of the destination site, within seven days after the
  pig's reception, shall report the following information to the
  responsible administrator:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the location of the departure site and the location
    of the destination site;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the operator of the departure site,
    the date and time that the conveyance carrying the pig left the
    departure site and, in the case of the operator of the destination
    site, the date and time that the conveyance arrived at the
    destination site;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the operator of the departure site,
    the number of pigs loaded onto the conveyance and, in the case of
    the operator of the destination site, the number of pigs and pig
    carcasses that arrived at the destination site; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the licence plate number or, if there is no licence
    plate, other identification of the conveyance.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} There is no requirement to report to the responsible
  administrator or keep records on the movement of pigs transported from
  one part of a farm to another contiguous part of that farm.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person who operates a site where an approved
  tag or approved slap tattoo is applied to a pig shall report the
  following information to the responsible administrator within seven
  days after the pig's departure:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the location of the departure site and the location
    of the destination site;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date and time that the conveyance carrying the
    pig left the departure site;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the identification numbers on the approved tags and
    approved slap tattoos applied to the pigs; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the licence plate number or, if there is no licence
    plate, other identification of the conveyance.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If pigs are transported from a site to an abattoir,
  the operator of the departure site, within seven days after the
  departure of the pigs, and the operator of the abattoir, within seven
  days after the reception of the pigs, shall report the following
  information to the responsible administrator:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the location of the departure site and the location
    of the abattoir;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the operator of the departure site,
    the date and time that the conveyance carrying the pigs left the
    departure site and, in the case of the operator of the abattoir, the
    date and time that the conveyance arrived at the abattoir;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the operator of the departure site,
    the number of pigs loaded onto the conveyance and, in the case of
    the operator of the abattoir, the number of pigs and pig carcasses
    that arrived at the abattoir;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the operator of the abattoir, the
    identification numbers on the approved tags or the approved slap
    tattoos applied to the pigs; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the licence plate number or, if there is no licence
    plate, other identification of the conveyance.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a pig carcass or part of a pig carcass is
  transported from one site to another, the operator of the departure
  site, within seven days after the departure of the carcass or the
  part, and the operator of the destination site, within seven days
  after the reception of the carcass or the part, shall report the
  following information to the responsible administrator:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the location of the departure site and either the
    name of the operator of the destination site or the location of the
    destination site;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the operator of the departure site,
    the date that the conveyance carrying the carcass or the part left
    the departure site and, in the case of the operator of the
    destination site, the date that the conveyance arrived at the
    destination site; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the operator of the destination
    site, the licence plate number or, if there is no licence plate,
    other identification of the conveyance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-23, s. 12
:::

**[[175.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175.3 .sectionLabel}** Every person who is
required under this Part to report information in respect of a pig, pig
carcass or part of a pig carcass shall keep a record of that information
for five years.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-23, s. 12
:::

**[[175.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175.4 .sectionLabel}** If pigs are
transported as described in subsection 175.01(2) between two sites that
are registered as being linked with each other, the operator of the
departure site and the operator of the destination site shall keep a
record of the following information for five years after the departure
of the pigs:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the location of the departure site and the location
  of the destination site;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the operator of the departure site,
  the date and time that the conveyance carrying the pigs left the
  departure site and, in the case of the operator of the destination
  site, the date and time that the conveyance arrived at the destination
  site;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the operator of the departure site,
  the number of pigs loaded onto the conveyance and, in the case of the
  operator of the destination site, the number of pigs and pig carcasses
  that arrived at the destination site;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the identification numbers on the approved tags
  applied to the pigs; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the licence plate number or, if there is no licence
  plate, other identification of the conveyance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-23, s. 12
:::

### [Prohibitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548567 .Subheading}

**[[176]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-176 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 183,
no person shall remove or cause the removal of a bison, bovine or ovine
or the carcass of a bison, bovine or ovine from a site unless it bears
an approved tag that has been applied to it under section 175.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2003-409, s. 4
- SOR/2005-192, s. 5
- SOR/2014-23, s. 13
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-176-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 176"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[176.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-176.1 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
remove or cause the removal of a pig from a site unless the pig bears an
approved tag or an approved slap tattoo that has been applied to it
under section 175.01 or the pig is otherwise dealt with in accordance
with that section.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-23, s. 13
:::

- **[[177]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-177
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 183 and
  subsection 184(2), no person shall transport or cause the
  transportation of a bison, bovine or ovine or the carcass of a bison,
  bovine or ovine that does not bear an approved tag.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 183 and subsection 184(2), no
  person shall receive or cause the reception of a bison, bovine or
  ovine or the carcass of a bison, bovine or ovine that does not bear an
  approved tag.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2005-192, s. 5
- SOR/2014-23, s. 13
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-177-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 177"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[177.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-177.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 184(2), no
  person shall transport, cause the transportation of, receive or cause
  the reception of a pig unless the pig bears an approved tag or an
  approved slap tattoo that has been applied to it under section 175.01
  or the pig is otherwise dealt with in accordance with that section.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to the reception of
  pigs that are transported as described in subsection 175.01(2) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time of the transportation, the departure
    site and the destination site are registered as being linked with
    each other; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operator of the destination site, within seven
    days after the pigs' reception, reports the following information to
    the responsible administrator:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the location of the departure site and the
      location of the destination site,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date and time that the conveyance carrying
      the pigs arrived at the destination site,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the number of pigs and pig carcasses that
      arrived at the destination site,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the identification number on any approved tag
      applied to the pigs, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the licence plate number or, if there is no
      licence plate, other identification of the conveyance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-23, s. 13
:::

- **[[178]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-178
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 183, no person
  shall apply or cause the application of an approved tag issued under
  subsection 174(1) to an animal or the carcass of an animal that is not
  on the site in respect of which the approved tag was issued.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} No person shall apply or cause the application of,
  to a pig, an approved slap tattoo or, in the case of pigs that are
  destined for export, an indicator that bears an identification number
  issued by a responsible administrator in respect of a site unless the
  pig is at that site.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall apply, or cause the application of,
  an approved tag issued to an importer under subsection 174(3) to an
  animal that has not been imported by the importer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2014-23, s. 14
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 25](#1515140-1516233){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515140-1516233.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-178-20140701.html "Link to previous version of section 178"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[179]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-179 .sectionLabel}** Except as authorized
under paragraph 186(1)(a) or 187(1)(a), no person shall remove, or cause
the removal of, an approved tag, or an approved tag that has been
revoked, from an animal or the carcass of an animal.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2005-192, s. 6
- SOR/2010-137, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-179-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 179"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[180]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-180 .sectionLabel}** No person shall apply,
or cause the application of, an approved tag from an animal or the
carcass of an animal to another animal or the carcass of another animal.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
:::

**[[180.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-180.1 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
apply or cause the application of an approved tag to an animal or the
carcass of an animal that is not listed in the definition
[[animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in section 172.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-23, s. 15
:::

**[[181]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-181 .sectionLabel}** No person shall alter
an approved tag to change its tamper-proof nature or its identification
number or to make the identification number unreadable.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2005-192, s. 7
:::

**[[182]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-182 .sectionLabel}** No person shall make,
sell or provide a tag, chip or other indicator that so closely resembles
an approved tag that it is likely to be mistaken for one.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
:::

### [Tagging Site]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548612 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2003-409, s. 5
- SOR/2005-192, s. 8

\]
:::

- **[[183]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-183 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  bison or a bovine may be transported from its farm of origin without
  having an approved tag applied to it to a site for the purpose of
  having an approved tag applied to it at that site if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the site is on the list
    that sets out tagging sites approved by the responsible
    administrator and is published on that administrator's web site; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an approved tag issued to the farm of origin under
    subsection 174(1) will be supplied, along with the bison or bovine,
    by the operator of the farm of origin or, through a prior
    arrangement with the manager of the tagging site, will be issued and
    applied to the bison or bovine at the tagging site.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The manager of a tagging site shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the bison or bovine is not mixed with
    any other person's animals that do not bear approved tags;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the approved tag is applied to the
    bison or bovine immediately after it arrives at the site; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} keep records, and make them available on request to
    the responsible administrator, of enough information about the bison
    or bovines received at the site to enable their origin to be traced,
    including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the names and addresses of the owners or persons
      having the possession, care or control of the animals before the
      transportation of the animals to the site,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the dates of the arrival of the animals at the
      site, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the identification numbers on the approved tags
      applied to the animals and the dates of the application of those
      tags to the animals.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the manager of a tagging site has declared in a
  written application that the equipment and infrastructure at the site
  are adequate to enable the application of an approved tag to a bison
  or bovine without endangering its safety or the safety of the
  personnel at the site, the responsible administrator shall approve the
  site and include the name and address of the site on its list of
  approved tagging sites.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the manager of a tagging site does not comply with
  subsection (2) or (3), the responsible administrator shall remove the
  name and address of the tagging site from the list of approved tagging
  sites if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the responsible administrator has caused to be
    delivered to the manager of the tagging site a notice that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} describes the non-compliance, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} specifies the period within which the manager
      may rectify the non-compliance;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person has not rectified the non-compliance
    within the period specified in the notice; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person has been given an opportunity to be
    heard in respect of the proposed removal.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the responsible administrator removes the name and
  address of the tagging site from the list, that administrator shall,
  without delay,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} notify the manager of the tagging site that the
    name and address have been removed from the list; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} cause a notice of the removal to be published on
    its web site.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the name and address of a tagging site have been
  removed from the list, the manager of the site may reapply under
  subsection (3) to have the site approved as a tagging site.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2003-409, s. 6
- SOR/2005-192, s. 9
- SOR/2014-23, s. 16
- SOR/2015-55, s. 10(F)
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 26](#1515142-1516251){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515142-1516251.html"
  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-183-20150227.html "Link to previous version of section 183"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Loss of an Approved Tag or Application of a New Approved Tag]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548641 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2005-192, s. 10

\]
:::

- **[[184]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-184
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  if an animal that is required to bear an approved tag does not bear an
  approved tag, bears an approved tag that has been revoked or loses its
  approved tag, the person who owns or has the possession, care or
  control of the animal shall immediately apply a new approved tag to
  it.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), an animal that loses its
  approved tag while being transported may continue to be transported
  until it reaches the next site where it will be unloaded, and it may
  be received at that site only if a new approved tag is applied to the
  animal without delay after it is received at that site.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An animal that is received at an abattoir without an
  approved tag or approved slap tattoo does not have to have a new
  approved tag applied to it if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is slaughtered at the abattoir;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person who operates the abattoir keeps a record
    of enough information about the origin of the animal to enable the
    origin to be traced, including, if it is known by that person,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the number of the approved tag that was lost and,
      in the case of an animal to which more than one approved tag had
      been applied since the animal's birth, the numbers of all of them,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the owner or person
      having the possession, care or control of the animal when it was
      brought to the abattoir and the date when it was brought to the
      abattoir, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the licence plate number or, if there is no
      licence plate, other identification of the conveyance that brought
      the animal to the abattoir; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a bison or a bovine, the person who
    operates the abattoir reports to the responsible administrator,
    within 30 days after the animal is slaughtered, the information that
    the person is required by paragraph (b) to record in respect of the
    animal.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} An organization that manages an animal identification
  system shall, if it receives the information referred to in
  paragraph (3)(b), report the information to the responsible
  administrator within 30 days after receiving it.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2003-409, s. 7
- SOR/2005-192, s. 11
- SOR/2010-137, s. 3
- SOR/2012-286, s. 64(F)
- SOR/2014-23, s. 17
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 27](#1515144-1516253){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515144-1516253.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-184-20140701.html "Link to previous version of section 184"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[185]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-185
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who applies, or causes
  the application of, a new approved tag to an animal, or the carcass of
  an animal, that does not bear an approved tag, bears an approved tag
  that has been revoked, or that has lost its approved tag, shall keep a
  record of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of the new approved tag; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} enough information about the origin of the animal
    or the carcass to enable the origin to be traced, including, if it
    is known by that person,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the number of the approved tag that was
      previously applied to the animal or carcass and, in the case of an
      animal or carcass to which more than one approved tag has been
      applied since the animal's birth, the numbers of all of them,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the owner or person
      having the possession, care or control of the animal or carcass
      when it was brought to the site where the new approved tag was
      attached to it and the date when it was brought to the site, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the licence plate number or, if there is no
      licence plate, other identification of the conveyance that brought
      the animal or carcass to the site where the new approved tag was
      attached to it.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of the
  application of an approved tag to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an animal before it leaves its farm of origin; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the carcass of an animal before the carcass leaves
    the animal's farm of origin.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person who applies, or causes the application
  of, a new approved tag to an animal, or the carcass of an animal, that
  already bears an approved tag or an approved tag that has been revoked
  shall, within 30 days after the new approved tag is applied, report to
  the responsible administrator the number of the new approved tag as
  well as the number of the previously applied tag.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} An organization that manages an animal identification
  system shall, if it receives the information referred to in
  subsection (3), report the information to the responsible
  administrator within 30 days after receiving it.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2005-192, s. 12
- SOR/2010-137, s. 4
- SOR/2014-23, s. 18
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-185-20100617.html "Link to previous version of section 185"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Animal Death or Slaughter]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548671 .Subheading}

- **[[186]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-186 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an animal bearing an approved tag is slaughtered at an abattoir or
  otherwise dies at an abattoir, the operator of the abattoir

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may remove any tag from the animal or the animal's
    carcass; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a bison or a bovine, shall report
    the death of the animal and the number of the approved tag to the
    responsible administrator within 30 days after the death.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The operator of an abattoir where an animal bearing
  an approved tag or approved slap tattoo is slaughtered shall maintain
  the ability to identify the animal's carcass in the abattoir until the
  carcass is approved for human consumption or is condemned.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an animal bearing an approved tag or an approved
  tag that has been revoked is slaughtered or otherwise dies at a site
  other than an abattoir, the operator of the site shall keep a record
  of the date of slaughter or death of the animal and the identification
  number on its tag.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} An organization that manages an animal identification
  system shall, if it receives the information referred to in
  paragraph (1)(b), report the information to the responsible
  administrator within 30 days after receiving it.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2014-23, s. 19\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2003-409, s. 8
- SOR/2005-192, s. 13
- SOR/2010-137, s. 5
- SOR/2014-23, s. 19
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 28](#1515149-1516257){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515149-1516257.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-186-20140701.html "Link to previous version of section 186"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[187]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-187
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person, including a renderer,
  dead stock operator, post-mortem laboratory official and veterinarian,
  who disposes of the carcass of a bison, bovine or ovine bearing an
  approved tag or an approved tag that has been revoked

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may remove the tag from the carcass; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall report the identification number on the tag
    to the responsible administrator within 30 days after disposing of
    the carcass.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person, including a renderer, dead stock
  operator, post-mortem laboratory official and veterinarian, who
  disposes of the carcass of a bison, bovine or ovine not bearing an
  approved tag anywhere but on the farm of origin where the animal died
  shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} collect enough information about the carcass to
    enable the origin to be traced, including, if it is known by that
    person,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the site from which the carcass was removed and
      the date when the carcass was removed from that place, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the owner or person
      having the possession, care or control of the carcass when it was
      removed from that place; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} report that information to the responsible
    administrator within 30 days after disposing of the carcass.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An organization that manages an animal identification
  system shall, if it receives the information referred to in
  paragraph (1)(b) or (2)(a), report that information to the responsible
  administrator within 30 days after receiving it.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2005-192, s. 14
- SOR/2010-137, s. 6
- SOR/2014-23, s. 20
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-187-20100617.html "Link to previous version of section 187"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Export]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548694 .Subheading}

- **[[188]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-188
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who exports a bison or
  bovine shall report the identification number on the animal's approved
  tag to the responsible administrator within 30 days after the
  exportation.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who exports pigs shall report the
  following information to the responsible administrator within seven
  days after the exportation:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the locations of the last sites at which the pigs
    were kept before they were exported and the number of pigs that were
    from each of those sites;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the locations to which the pigs were exported and
    the number of pigs that were exported to each of those locations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the dates on which the pigs were loaded onto the
    conveyance by which they were exported and the number of pigs that
    were loaded on each of those dates;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} except in the case of cull breeding pigs that are
    exported for immediate slaughter from a site that is used for the
    purpose of collecting pigs for transport to an abattoir, the
    identification numbers on the indicators approved by the importing
    country that have been applied to the pigs and that identify the
    last sites at which they were kept before they were exported and the
    number of pigs bearing each of those identification numbers; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the licence plate number or, if there is no licence
    plate, other identification of the conveyance.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2003-409, s. 9
- SOR/2005-192, s. 15
- SOR/2014-23, s. 21
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 29](#1515151-1516259){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515151-1516259.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-188-20140701.html "Link to previous version of section 188"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Import]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548705 .Subheading}

- **[[189]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-189
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who imports an animal
  shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} apply or cause the application of an approved tag
    to the animal either before importation or immediately after the
    animal reaches its initial destination;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a bison, bovine or ovine, report to
    the responsible administrator

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the identification number on the animal's
      approved tag, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} enough information about the animal to allow the
      origin to be traced; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a pig, report to the responsible
    administrator

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the location of the last site at which the pig
      was kept before it was imported,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the location to which the pig was imported,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the pig was received,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the identification number on the pig's approved
      tag, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the licence plate number or, if there is no
      licence plate, other identification of the conveyance by which the
      pig was imported.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The reports referred to in paragraphs (1)(b) and (c)
  shall be made

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a bison is being imported, within 60 days after
    importation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a bovine is being imported, within 30 days after
    importation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if an ovine is being imported, within 7 days after
    importation; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if a pig is being imported, within 7 days after
    importation.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(a) and (b) do not apply to a bison,
  bovine or ovine that is imported for immediate slaughter.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) does not apply to an animal bearing
  an indicator of a foreign country if the Minister determines that the
  indicator meets the criteria set out in subsection 173(2) and that the
  identification number on the indicator can be entered and tracked in
  the responsible administrator's database.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections 175(3) and 185(3) and
  sections 175.01, 175.1, 176 to 177.1, 179 to 181, and 186 to 189, if
  an imported animal bears an indicator of a foreign country and the
  Minister determines that the indicator meets the criteria set out in
  subsection 173(2) and that the identification number on the indicator
  can be entered and tracked in the responsible administrator's
  database, the indicator is deemed to be an approved tag that was
  issued and applied to the animal in accordance with this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2000-416, s. 1
- SOR/2003-409, s. 10
- SOR/2005-192, s. 16(F)
- SOR/2014-23, s. 22
- [SOR/2025-47, s. 30](#1515153-1516379){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1515153-1516379.html"
  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-189-20140701.html "Link to previous version of section 189"){.PITLink}

### [Information Obtained by a Responsible Administrator]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548728 .Subheading}

**[[189.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-189.1 .sectionLabel}** A responsible
administrator shall maintain a database and other records obtained under
this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-23, s. 23
:::

- **[[189.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-189.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a person who is a responsible
  administrator obtains information under this Part in relation to an
  animal or the carcass of an animal and that animal or carcass was
  previously located in a province in respect of which the person is not
  the responsible administrator, the person shall provide that
  information without delay to the responsible administrator in respect
  of the province in which that animal or carcass was located.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A responsible administrator may allow persons to have
  access to the information that that administrator obtains under this
  Part for the purpose of providing services in relation to the database
  if those persons agree in writing not to disclose the information to
  any other person.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every responsible administrator shall allow the
  Agency to have access to the information that that administrator
  obtains under this Part.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A responsible administrator shall allow any person to
  have access to the information that that administrator obtains under
  this Part if the Agency advises that administrator that the access is
  provided for in an agreement or memorandum of understanding that the
  Agency has entered into under subsection 14(1) of the [Canadian Food
  Inspection Agency Act](/eng/acts/C-16.5).

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a person (in this subsection referred to as the
  "former administrator") ceases to be the responsible administrator for
  animals of all or part of a genus, species or subspecies that are
  located in a province and another person (in this subsection referred
  to as the "new administrator") becomes the responsible administrator
  for animals of that genus, species or subspecies or of that part that
  are located in that province, the former administrator shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provide to the new administrator the information
    that the former administrator has obtained under this Part; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after receiving from the new administrator an
    acknowledgement in writing that the transferred information has been
    entered successfully into the database or other records maintained
    by the new administrator, permanently delete that information from
    the database and other records maintained by the former
    administrator except if the former administrator has the express,
    free and informed consent of any person to whom the information
    relates to retain that information.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-23, s. 23
:::

## [PART XVI]{.HLabel1}[Aquatic Animals]{.HTitleText1} {#h-548742 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548743 .Subheading}

**[[190]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-190 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[aquatic animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[aquatic animal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any finfish, mollusc or
    crustacean, or any part of a finfish, mollusc or crustacean at any
    life stage, as well as any germplasm of those animals. ([animal
    aquatique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[eviscerated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eviscerated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a finfish, means
    that the internal organs, excluding the brain and gills, have been
    removed. ([éviscéré]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[finfish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[finfish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any cold-blooded aquatic
    vertebrate possessing fins and gills. ([poisson à
    nageoires]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[germplasm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[germplasm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means semen, male or female germ
    cells, or genetic material taken from a male or female germ cell for
    the purpose of producing a zygote. ([matériel
    génétique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[offal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[offal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to an aquatic animal,
    means waste portions including the visceral and non-visceral organs,
    cut-offs and raw material. ([abats]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[species]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[species]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of germplasm, the
    species of the aquatic animal that it is
    from. ([espèce]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Susceptible Species of Aquatic Animals List]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Susceptible Species of Aquatic Animals
    List]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the document entitled Susceptible
    Species of Aquatic Animals that is published on the Agency's
    website, as amended from time to time. ([liste des espèces d'animaux
    aquatiques vulnérables]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 3
- [SOR/2021-41, s. 2](#1289056-1289595){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1289056-1289595.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-190-20101210.html "Link to previous version of section 190"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Importation of Aquatic Animals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548753 .Subheading}

#### [Aquatic Animals Set Out in the Susceptible Species of Aquatic Animals List]{.HTitleText3} {#h-548754 .Subheading}

**[[191]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-191 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import
an aquatic animal set out in the Susceptible Species of Aquatic Animals
List except in accordance with a permit issued under section 160.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 4
- [SOR/2021-41, s. 6](#1289065-1289626){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1289065-1289626.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-191-20111210.html "Link to previous version of section 191"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Pet Aquatic Animals]{.HTitleText3} {#h-548758 .Subheading}

- **[[192]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-192
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 191, an aquatic
  animal set out in the Susceptible Species of Aquatic Animals List may
  be imported without a permit for use as a pet if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the aquatic animal is a member of one of the
    following species:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} [Barbonymus gonionotus]{.otherLang lang="la"},

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} [Carassius auratus]{.otherLang lang="la"},

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} [Colisa lalia]{.otherLang lang="la"},

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} [Danio rerio]{.otherLang lang="la"},

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} [Glossogobius giuris]{.otherLang lang="la"},

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} [Osphronemus goramy]{.otherLang lang="la"},

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} [Oxyeleotris marmorata]{.otherLang lang="la"},

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} [Puntius sophore]{.otherLang lang="la"},

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} [Symphysodon discus]{.otherLang lang="la"},

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} [Toxotes chatareus]{.otherLang lang="la"},

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} [Trichogaster pectoralis]{.otherLang lang="la"},
      or

    - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} [Trichogaster trichopterus]{.otherLang
      lang="la"};

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the aquatic animal has not been taken to a show or
    display outside Canada;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the aquatic animal is imported by its owner;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the aquatic animal is accompanied or picked up by
    its owner at the point of entry into Canada; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the owner presents proof of the owner's identity
    and his or her ownership of the aquatic animal to the inspector.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} An aquatic animal imported under subsection (1) shall
  be kept in an aquarium in the household of its owner, and the owner
  shall not, for the year following the importation, expose it to any
  aquatic animals other than those kept in the household.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The owner of an aquatic animal imported under
  subsection (1) shall not, in the period of 90 days after the
  importation, import another aquatic animal under that subsection.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The owner of an aquatic animal imported under
  subsection (1) shall keep the records of the importation, including
  the documents required under paragraph (1)(e).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 4
- [SOR/2021-41, s. 3](#1289061-1289609){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1289061-1289609.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2021-41, s. 6](#1289065-1289628){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1289065-1289628.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-192-20111210.html "Link to previous version of section 192"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Aquatic Animals for Personal Use]{.HTitleText3} {#h-548771 .Subheading}

- **[[193]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-193
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 191, an aquatic
  animal set out in the Susceptible Species of Aquatic Animals List may
  be imported without a permit if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the aquatic animal is imported by a person for his
    or her personal use;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person brings the aquatic animal into Canada or
    picks it up at the point of entry into Canada; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person presents, to the inspector, proof of his
    or her identity and proof of the manner in which he or she acquired
    the aquatic animal.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The quantity of aquatic animals that may be imported
  under subsection (1) shall not exceed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} four crustaceans;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} three kilograms of molluscs; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} ten finfish that are not eviscerated.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 4
- [SOR/2021-41, s. 6](#1289065-1289634){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1289065-1289634.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-193-20111210.html "Link to previous version of section 193"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Aquatic Animals not Set Out in the Susceptible Species of Aquatic Animals List]{.HTitleText3} {#h-548783 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2021-41, s. 6

\]
:::

**[[194]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-194 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import
an aquatic animal that is not set out in the Susceptible Species of
Aquatic Animals List unless it is accompanied by a document that is
satisfactory to an inspector and that includes the following
information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the exporter;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the importer;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the taxonomic name of the aquatic animal, the life
  stage, and the number being imported, if more than one; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the country in which the aquatic animal was born or
  where the germplasm came from and, in the case of an aquatic animal,
  whether it was born in captivity or in the wild.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 4
- [SOR/2021-41, s. 6](#1289065-1289641){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1289065-1289641.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-194-20111210.html "Link to previous version of section 194"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Importation of Carcasses and Offal]{.HTitleText3} {#h-548791 .Subheading}

**[[195]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-195 .sectionLabel}** No person shall import
into Canada, except in accordance with a permit issued under section 160

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the carcass or a part of the carcass of a finfish set
  out in the Susceptible Species of Aquatic Animals List for use as
  bait, for use in feeding to, or manufacturing feed for aquatic
  animals, for research or diagnosis or, if the carcass has not been
  eviscerated, for any purpose that will produce offal or effluent
  containing anything from the finfish;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the carcass or a part of the carcass of a mollusc set
  out in the Susceptible Species of Aquatic Animals List, for use as
  bait, for use in feeding to, or manufacturing feed for aquatic
  animals, for research or diagnosis, or for any purpose that will
  produce offal or effluent containing anything from the mollusc;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the carcass or a part of the carcass of a crustacean
  set out in the Susceptible Species of Aquatic Animals List, for use as
  bait, for use in feeding to, or manufacturing feed for aquatic
  animals, for research or diagnosis, or for any purpose that will
  produce offal or effluent containing anything from the crustacean; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} offal from a finfish, mollusc or crustacean set out
  in the Susceptible Species of Aquatic Animals List, for use as bait,
  for use in feeding to, or manufacturing feed for aquatic animals, for
  research or diagnosis, or for any purpose that will produce effluent
  containing anything from that offal.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 4
- [SOR/2021-41, s. 4(E)](#1289063-1289668){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1289063-1289668.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2021-41, s. 6](#1289065-1289669){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1289065-1289669.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-195-20111210.html "Link to previous version of section 195"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Preventing the Spread of Diseases of Aquatic Animals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548799 .Subheading}

#### [Eradication Areas]{.HTitleText3} {#h-548800 .Subheading}

**[[196]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-196 .sectionLabel}** Each province, each
territory, and the territorial sea and contiguous zone of Canada taken
together, is established as an eradication area in which

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} any finfish, mollusc or crustacean set out in the
  Susceptible Species of Aquatic Animals List may be inspected,
  segregated and tested for any disease listed in the schedule to the
  [Reportable Diseases Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-91-2); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} disease eradication programs may be instituted for
  preventing the spread of any disease listed in the schedule to the
  [Reportable Diseases Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-91-2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 5
- [SOR/2021-41, s. 6](#1289065-1289673){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1289065-1289673.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-196-20101210.html "Link to previous version of section 196"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[197]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-197 .sectionLabel}** Every owner or person
having the possession, care or control of an aquatic animal or a thing
in an eradication area shall, when requested to do so by a veterinary
inspector, by an inspector or by an accredited veterinarian with the
approval of a veterinary inspector, permit tests to be conducted on the
aquatic animal or the thing for any of the diseases listed in the
schedule to the [Reportable Diseases
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-91-2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 5
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 17](#1124107-1124688){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124107-1124688.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-197-20101210.html "Link to previous version of section 197"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[198]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-198 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may declare an eradication area, or a part of one, to be an
  infected area with respect to any of the diseases of aquatic animals
  listed in the schedule to the [Reportable Diseases
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-91-2) if the disease has been
  identified in the eradication area, or part of it, and may designate
  the aquatic animals susceptible to that disease, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the infected animals and any things that have been
    exposed to the disease have been treated or disposed of in a manner
    that satisfies a veterinary inspector that the disease has been
    eliminated from the eradication area or part of it, and the result
    of an epidemiological examination satisfies the veterinary inspector
    in charge of the examination that the disease has been eradicated
    from the eradication area or part of it; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the animals, things or disease are located in a
    containment facility.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may declare an eradication area, or a
  part of one, to be a free area with respect to any of the diseases of
  aquatic animals listed in the schedule to the [Reportable Diseases
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-91-2) if he or she is satisfied that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the eradication area, or part of it, is free of
    that disease, based on one or more of the following factors:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount of time since the disease was last
      identified in the area or part of it,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the examination of all suspected outbreaks and
      the decision by the veterinary inspector that the disease is not
      present,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the actions taken to eradicate the disease, if
      it was identified, and the success of those actions, based on the
      factors set out in paragraphs (1)(a) and (b),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the disease detection activities are sufficient
      to detect the presence of the disease,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the measures taken to prevent the introduction of
      the disease into the eradication area, or part of it, and the
      ability to enforce those measures,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the physical barriers to the spread of the
      disease,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} any other scientific information relevant to
      the disease, and

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the separation of the free area, or part of
      it, from any infected area by a buffer area; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} disease detection activities sufficient to detect
    the presence of the disease are maintained while the declaration is
    in effect.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may declare an eradication area, or a
  part of one, to be a buffer area for any of the diseases listed in the
  schedule to the [Reportable Diseases
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-91-2) if he or she is satisfied that
  even though the disease has not been detected within the eradication
  area, or part of it, that area or part of it is at risk of becoming
  infected, because of its epidemiological relationship to an infected
  area.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may declare an eradication area, or a
  part of one, to be a provisionally free area for any of the diseases
  listed in the schedule to the [Reportable Diseases
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-91-2) if the eradication area or
  part of it is not an infected area, free area or buffer area.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Any declaration under this section shall include a
  description of the eradication area, or part of it, the name of the
  disease on which the declaration is based and the list of the species
  of aquatic animals and carcasses or parts of carcasses of those
  aquatic animals that are susceptible to that disease.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 5
- [SOR/2019-99, s. 18(F)](#1124109-1124704){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1124109-1124704.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-198-20101210.html "Link to previous version of section 198"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Movement of Aquatic Animals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548829 .Subheading}

- **[[199]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-199 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall move, or cause to be moved, an aquatic animal or a thing
  specified in a declaration made under subsection 198(1) from an
  eradication area, or a part of one, that has been declared an infected
  area for a disease named in the declaration, to a free area, buffer
  area or provisionally free area for that disease, except in accordance
  with a permit issued under section 160.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall move, or cause to be moved, an
  aquatic animal or a thing specified in a declaration made under
  subsection 198(3) from an eradication area, or a part of one, that has
  been declared a buffer area for a disease named in the declaration, to
  a free area, another buffer area or a provisionally free area for that
  disease, except in accordance with a permit issued under section 160.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall move, or cause to be moved, an
  aquatic animal or thing specified in a declaration made under
  subsection 198(4) from an eradication area, or a part of one, that has
  been declared a provisionally free area for a disease named in the
  declaration, to a free area for that disease, except in accordance
  with a permit issued under section 160.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 5
:::

**[[200]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-200 .sectionLabel}** If, in the opinion of
the Minister, an aquatic animal is moved into an area in violation of
section 199, the Minister may order that the aquatic animal be taken
back without delay to the area from which it was moved or to an area of
equal or lesser health status.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 5
:::

### [Marking of Aquatic Animals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548839 .Subheading}

**[[201]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-201 .sectionLabel}** If an aquatic animal is
tested for a disease listed in the schedule to the [Reportable Diseases
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-91-2) or a disease named in Schedule
VII and the animal reacts positively to the test, the owner of the
animal shall ensure that it is marked with an identifier appropriate to
the species or that its container is identified as containing an
infected animal.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 5
:::

### [General]{.HTitleText2} {#h-548843 .Subheading}

**[[202]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202 .sectionLabel}** If, under this Part, a
permit, certificate or other document is required for the removal or
transportation of an aquatic animal, the person having the possession,
care or control of the aquatic animal shall, when requested to do so by
an inspector or peace officer appointed under the Act, produce the
permit, certificate or other document.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2010-296, s. 5
:::

::: ScheduleRP
## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- SOR/2006-147, s. 35

  - [35]{.lawlabel} Sections 6.21 and 6.22 of the [Health of Animals
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._296), as enacted by section
    12, do not apply before January 30, 2008, except to operators of
    registered establishments, as defined in subsection 2(1) of the
    [Meat Inspection Act](/eng/acts/M-3.2), and persons employed in
    those establishments.

- --- SOR/2022-218, s. 5

  - [5]{.lawlabel} For the one-year period that begins on the day on
    which these Regulations come into force, any person may, instead of
    complying with Part VIII.1 of the [Health of Animals
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._296), comply with sections
    79 to 79.2 of the [Health of Animals
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._296) and the [Hatchery
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1023) as they read
    immediately before that day.
